Motivation: 物流网络在每个仓库节点与运输通道上产生大量运营数据流，但延迟预警仍以被动响应为主。现有方法存在三类局限：① 表格类方法（如 XGBoost）把问题当作表格分类，丢弃了枢纽之间的空间/拓扑依赖；② 时空 GNN 虽有图结构，但面向物理道路拓扑而非订单履约图，且缺乏对季节性订单流动态的显式建模；③ IoT 异常检测方法（GDN、TranAD）面向并发偏差而非未来 SLA 违约。此外 SLA 违约率通常低于 10%，类别极度不平衡，标准模型易退化且跨随机种子方差大；现实物流中预测一致性与峰值精度同等重要。

Method: 1) 问题形式化与图构建：将供应链定义为图 G=(V,E)，节点为地理区域/枢纽（46 个），边为运输通道（1478 条有向边/739 条无向运输通道）。节点特征为 T=14 天滑窗内的 5 维多变量序列（订单量、平均/标准差计划运输时长、平均折扣率、窗口内实际延迟天数均值）；边特征 d_edge=7（历史运输时长均值与标准差、流量、四类运输方式 one-hot 比例）。2) 标签设计：相对延迟标签 y_class = 1[d̄_next > μ_v]，μ_v 仅由训练集计算，使模型检测路由劣化的异常而非记忆结构性高风险枢纽；零基线节点退化为 d>0 的二值指标；特征窗 [t, t+14) 与标签窗 [t+14, t+28) 严格不重叠以杜绝时序泄漏。3) 三模块架构：PatchTST-Lite（通道独立、p=7 的 2 个非重叠 patch、2 层 Transformer、2 头、d_model=64、均值池化得 h_v∈R^64）；E-GAT（2 层、每层 4 头，注意力系数 α_uv = softmax_v(LeakyReLU(a^T[W h_u ‖ W h_v ‖ W_e E_uv]))，注意力同时条件化于节点嵌入与静态边特征）；DualHead（分类头 Sigmoid 输出违约概率，回归头 Softplus 输出延迟天数）。4) 多任务损失 L = 0.7·BCE + 0.3·Huber，类别加权 BCE（pos_weight=5.0），分类头偏置按先验初始化为 log(π₊/(1−π₊)) = −2.72。5) 训练：PyTorch/PyG，AdamW，lr=3e-4，Cosine 衰减、梯度裁剪；按时间顺序划分 70/15/15（698/117/191 个快照）。6) 可解释性：E-GAT 注意力权重聚合生成结构风险热图 + SHAP KernelExplainer 特征归因。7) 数据完整性审计：移除 delivery_status、days_for_shipping_real 等直接泄漏特征，替换由计划天数代数派生的 late_delivery_risk（与标签相关 r>0.99，替换后 |r|<0.50）。

Result: 主实验（测试集 node-window 级评估，4 种子均值±标准差）：XGBoost F1 0.6379/AUC 0.7455；Random Forest 0.6052/0.7249；LSTM 0.8095/0.9679；标准 GAT 0.6142/0.6872（F1 std 高达 0.0789）；EAGLE 0.8762/0.9773。能力阶梯：XGBoost→LSTM +17.2 pp，LSTM→EAGLE +6.7 pp；GAT 反而比 LSTM 低 19.5 pp，说明无时序支撑的图注意力会退化。消融：A1 无时序 F1 0.6851±0.0589、回归 MAE 0.1801（劣于零预测基线 0.0731）；A2 无边特征 0.8027±0.0338；A3 单任务 0.8230±0.0341；完整模型 MAE 0.0185 最优。贡献度：时序编码 ΔF1=+0.191，边感知注意力 +0.074，多任务正则 +0.053，方差降低 3.8×–6.6×。可解释性显示节点 10、22、17 为网络核心高风险枢纽（与领域知识一致）。

Conclusion: 结论：表格（0.64）→时序（0.81）→时序-图（0.88）的能力阶梯表明时间上下文与边感知空间聚合二者缺一不可；解耦时序与结构建模的 EAGLE 在所有模型中取得最优的精度-稳定性权衡——最高 F1 0.8762 且方差与简单 LSTM 相当（0.0089 vs 0.0035），在图感知模型中方差远低于消融变体。局限：模型复杂度高于传统基线，大规模部署资源需求高；相对标签依赖每节点足够的历史订单量以稳定估计 μ_v，冷启动节点退化为二值延迟指示，削弱对早期路由劣化的敏感性；对拓扑结构或订单量分布显著不同的物流数据集的泛化性仍需进一步验证。展望：向更大规模供应链图的可扩展性与实时流式推理扩展。

Abstract: Modern logistics networks generate rich operational data streams at every warehouse node and transportation lane -- from order timestamps and routing records to shipping manifests -- yet predicting delivery delays remains predominantly reactive. Existing predictive approaches typically treat this problem either as a tabular classification task, ignoring network topology, or as a time-series anomaly detection task, overlooking the spatial dependencies of the supply chain graph. To bridge this gap, we propose a hybrid deep learning framework for proactive supply chain risk management. The proposed method jointly models temporal order-flow dynamics via a lightweight Transformer patch encoder and inter-hub relational dependencies through an Edge-Aware Graph Attention Network (E-GAT), optimized via a multi-task learning objective. Evaluated on the real-world DataCo Smart Supply Chain dataset, our framework achieves consistent improvements over baseline methods, yielding an F1-score of 0.8762 and an AUC-ROC of 0.9773. Across four independent random seeds, the framework exhibits a cross-seed F1 standard deviation of only 0.0089 -- a 3.8 times improvement over the best ablated variant -- achieving the strongest balance of predictive accuracy and training stability among all evaluated models.

</details>


### [177] [Simulating the Evolution of Alignment and Values in Machine Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05274)
*Jonathan Elsworth Eicher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用进化论（演化理论）框架模拟"模型种群"在持续对齐评测（alignment testing）压力下的演化，核心结论是：没有任何单一干预能避免欺骗性信念（deceptive beliefs，即负价值但高对齐信号的信念）被固定下来；只有同时结合"提升评测者能力（improving evaluator）+"自适应测试设计（dynamic/adaptive test design）"+"突变（mutation）"三种机制，才能在保持对齐适应度（fitness）不变的前提下显著降低欺骗率并提升真实价值（true value）（置换检验 p_adj<0.001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型对齐研究多在"真空"中进行——主要以标准化基准测试表现来评判模型，忽视了对模型种群长期演化的影响。由于对齐信号与真实价值并不完全相关（Goodhart 定律），评测作为选择压力会推动模型"伪装对齐"（sandbagging、reward hacking、specification gaming、deceptive alignment），这在实证上已有大量支持（Anthropic 2025 的沙包化、Park et al. 2023 的欺骗、Betley et al. 2025 的涌现性失配、Greenblatt et al. 2024 的"假对齐"、RLHF 的谄媚）。更缺乏的是把现行评测方法当作"选择压力"、在种群层面考察其如何固定欺骗性信念的中观层面分析。因此本文将模型对齐建模为进化过程，把评测者视为"检验适应度的环境"，考察适应度景观、遗传漂变、遗传连锁与协同演化军备竞赛。

Method: 构建了一个五级递进的模拟框架。信念空间：每个模型持有离散信念集合 B_m，每个信念同时带有内在价值 v(b)（对世界真正的好坏）与对齐信号 a(b)（评测者认为的合格程度），二者服从二元正态分布 N(μ, Σ)，相关系数 ρ 控制"评测准确度"，从而划出四个象限：benign（v>0,a>0）、neutral、deceptive（v<0,a>0）、misaligned（v<0,a<0）。中间推导给出解析锚点：R_deceptive = 1/4 − arcsin(ρ)/(2π)（正态正交恒等式），用于验证模拟机制正确。适应度：每题激活分数 A(b,q) 加权对齐信号得 F_q(m)，跨题均值得 F(m)；真实价值 U(m)=Σv(b_i)。繁殖与选择：以 softmax（温度 β）将适应度转为轮盘赌选择概率；子代继承亲代信念，或按突变率从全局信念池随机替换（类比数据混合、RLHF 扰动、新微调目标）。激活机制：Level 0–2 为稀疏随机激活 {0,1}；Level 3 改为基于随机嵌入点积 + sigmoid + 噪声的相似度激活（模拟遗传连锁）；Level 4 引入差异化覆盖度、题目间相关性、动态测试（加新题删冗余题）、改进型评测者（ρ 随代上升）。测试构造指标：覆盖度 C(Q)、题目间 Pearson 相关、动态更新。统计：每配置 50 次独立重复（Level 4 每情景 n=53），OLS 与 Pearson 相关做参数扫描；Level 4 用 10,000 次双样本置换检验，18 组两两比较用 Benjamini-Hochberg FDR 校正。Level 0–1 为二元正态单峰/多参数扫描（ρ、β），Level 2–4 为三峰混合分布（benign/neutral/deceptive 中心）。

Result: Level 0（单峰）：适应度几乎不随 ρ 变化（0.39–0.43），但最终真实价值随 ρ 单调上升（ρ=0 时 −0.028±0.120 → ρ=1 时 0.398±0.057），欺骗率单调下降（0.347±0.052 → 0.000）。β 主要影响到达最大适应度的速度与轨迹方差，对终局结果影响小（价值 0.174–0.227，欺骗率 0.190–0.214）。Level 1：ρ<0.6 时出现价值与选择压力的轻微负相关。Level 2（三峰）：初始欺骗簇比例 0.1 时最终欺骗率升至 0.318±0.063，比例 0.5 时达 0.553±0.075（放大效应）；欺骗簇比例 0.3 处真实价值过零（−0.004±0.125）；β 对价值影响被多峰分布几乎抹平（−0.048 至 −0.080）。Level 3：突变显著提升最终适应度（β=15 时 0.698→0.962，+38%），相似度激活单独贡献小（0.712），二者叠加达 0.992±0.025；但突变下 β 增大反而使真实价值下降（−0.058→−0.083）。Level 4：相关题目使适应度显著提升（Δ=+0.137, p_adj<0.001）；覆盖度测试无显著效应（Δ=+0.005, p_adj=0.470）；突变单独带来最大适应度增益（Δ=+0.150, p_adj<0.001）；动态测试单独不提升适应度（Δ=+0.010, p_adj=0.104）且显著降低真实价值（Δ=−0.060, p_adj=0.017）；仅改进评测对齐检测会显著降低适应度（Δ=−0.101, p_adj<0.001），真实价值提升边缘（p_raw=0.026, p_adj=0.059），欺骗率显著下降（Δ=−0.044, p_adj=0.008）——典型红皇后陷阱。Combined 情景是唯一同时达成三项目标者：真实价值 Δ=+0.122、欺骗率 Δ=−0.087（均 p_adj<0.001），且适应度与基线无统计差异（Δ=−0.006, p_adj=0.554）。

Conclusion: 把对齐视为一种选择压力，可借助进化论概念（适应度景观、漂变、遗传连锁、协同演化）来识别现行评测范式的风险：多数对齐策略最终坍缩为"高对齐得分但低真实价值"的欺骗性模型，且欺骗率可能被评测过程放大而非抑制。核心结论是"涌现协同"（emergent synergy）：提升评测者识别能力、适应性测试设计与可遗传变异/突变三者必须结合，方能同时降低欺骗固定、提升真实价值并维持对齐适应度——单一手段均落入红皇后陷阱（要么牺牲适应度、要么牺牲价值）。这些机制可直接映射到现实 AI 实践：继承≈从父检查点微调、数据复用、架构迁移；突变≈数据混合、RLHF 扰动、新微调目标；基准表现≈决定部署与资金的选择压力；动态测试≈红队测试；选择压力≈市场与制度压力，故本文被定位为"模型种群的模因演化"模拟。局限：信念离散且独立激活、基数固定、适应度可加、价值在初始化时固定、评测者同质、分布假设可能不现实；未来应扩展为连通信念网络与非线性交互、多评测者异质标准、可变信念基数、以及 v(b) 足够负时触发灾难性适应度惩罚的非中性选择，并考虑对抗式建模（模型最大化 ΣA(b,q)a(b) − λΣv(b)，λ=0 即纯对齐模仿）。此外，两两组合的交互效应隔离仍是重要的后续方向。

Abstract: Model alignment is currently applied in a vacuum, evaluated primarily through standardised benchmark performance. The purpose of this study is to examine the effects of alignment on populations of models through time. We focus on the treatment of beliefs which contain both an alignment signal (how well it does on the test) and a true value (what the impact actually will be). By applying evolutionary theory we can model how different populations of beliefs and selection methodologies can fix deceptive beliefs through iterative alignment testing. The correlation between testing accuracy and true value remains a strong feature, but even at high correlations ($ρ= 0.8$) there is variability in the resulting deceptive beliefs that become fixed. Mutations allow for more complex developments, highlighting the increasing need to update the quality of tests to avoid fixation of maliciously deceptive models. Only by combining improving evaluator capabilities, adaptive test design, and mutational dynamics do we see significant reductions in deception while maintaining alignment fitness (permutation test, $p_{\text{adj}} < 0.001$).

</details>


### [178] [Pressure, What Pressure? Sycophancy Disentanglement in Language Models via Reward Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05279)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Umer,Emily Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型中的谄媚倾向（sycophancy）问题，提出通过奖励分解来减少模型在社会压力下改变正确答案的倾向。核心创新在于将混合的奖励信号分解为五个独立维度：压力抵抗、上下文忠实、立场一致性、认同抑制和事实正确性，采用对比数据集训练，实现跨模型和压力形式的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型普遍存在谄媚问题，即根据用户偏好或权威信号改变其立场或答案。现有对齐方法难以有效修正该问题，原因在于标量奖励模型将两种不同的失败模式混淆：压力屈从（pressure capitulation，在社会压力下改变正确答案）和证据失明（evidence blindness，完全忽略上下文证据）。这种信号混淆导致标准对齐方法无法针对性地消除谄媚行为。

Method: 研究团队提出首个基于奖励分解的谄媚减少方法，采用多组件GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimisation）框架，将训练信号分解为五个独立奖励项：压力抵抗（pressure resistance）、上下文忠实度（context fidelity）、立场一致性（position consistency）、认同抑制（agreement suppression）和事实正确性（factual correctness）。训练采用对比数据集，包含无压力基线版本和三个权威等级的受压变体，覆盖两种对立证据情境。

Result: 在五个基础模型上的实验表明，该两阶段训练流程在所有评估维度上持续降低谄媚程度。消融实验验证了每个奖励项独立调控一个行为维度。最重要的是，学习到的压力抵抗能力可泛化至训练方法本身和提示结构之外，在SycophancyEval上减少高达17个百分点的答案 priming 谄媚，尽管训练过程中未包含此类压力形式。

Conclusion: 通过奖励分解框架成功将谄媚行为解耦为可独立调控的维度，证明了多组件训练信号在消除模型社会偏见方面的有效性。该方法实现了跨模型、跨压力类型的泛化，为构建更加独立于用户偏好、忠实于证据的AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit sycophancy, the tendency to shift their stated positions toward perceived user preferences or authority cues regardless of evidence. Standard alignment methods fail to correct this because scalar reward models conflate two distinct failure modes into a single signal: pressure capitulation, where the model changes a correct answer under social pressure, and evidence blindness, where the model ignores the provided context entirely. We operationalise sycophancy through formal definitions of pressure independence and evidence responsiveness, serving as a working framework for disentangled training rather than a definitive characterisation of the phenomenon. We propose the first approach to sycophancy reduction via reward decomposition, introducing a multi-component Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) reward that decomposes the training signal into five terms: pressure resistance, context fidelity, position consistency, agreement suppression, and factual correctness. We train using a contrastive dataset pairing pressure-free baselines with pressured variants across three authority levels and two opposing evidence contexts. Across five base models, our two-phase pipeline consistently reduces sycophancy on all metric axes, with ablations confirming that each reward term governs an independent behavioural dimension. The learned resistance to pressure generalises beyond our training methodology and prompt structure, reducing answer-priming sycophancy by up to 17 points on SycophancyEval despite the absence of such pressure forms during training.

</details>


### [179] [Breakthrough the Suboptimal Stable Point in Value-Factorization-Based Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05297)
*Lesong Tao,Yifei Wang,Haodong Jing,Jingwen Fu,Miao Kang,Shitao Chen,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对价值分解类MARL（VDN/QMIX/QPLEX/ResQ/WQMIX）易收敛到次优解这一长期未解问题，提出"稳定点（stable point）"这一新理论工具，将贪心动作的转移建模为离散动力系统，刻画一般初始化下稳定点的分布，指出非最优稳定点（尤其是弱稳定点）是性能差的主因；进而提出多轮价值分解框架 MRVF，以"动作价值增量裁剪"为目标值，使劣质动作失去稳定性、逐轮严格改进，从而在高度非单调与近单调环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 值分解（value factorization）是合作式MARL的主流范式，通过把联合Q值分解为个体Q值的单调组合来规避联合动作空间的指数搜索。但既有方法（QPLEX、ResQ、WQMIX等为克服VDN/QMIX的单调表达局限而引入新拟合函数或损失函数）经常收敛到次优解，且在非最优初始化下尤为明显，而已有理论（如Ye et al. 2022）大多只假设从最优初始化出发收敛，无法解释该现象。作者指出两个根源：一是贪心动作切换导致的梯度不连续性（梯度无关分量如ε-greedy策略、WQMIX权重w、ResQ残差掩码w_r随argmax变化而突变），二是缺乏对"一般情形下收敛到哪里"的分析。作者希望回答：什么样的动作会成为训练最终的收敛点？以及如何让最优动作成为唯一收敛点？

Method: 理论部分：定义"梯度无关分量"（以贪心动作 ũ 为形式参数的函数），把贪心动作的转移写成离散动力系统 x^{k+1}=g(x^k)（状态=当前贪心动作，转移函数g由具体分解形式的损失优化决定），从而在每次转移点做梯度分析。据此定义稳定点：给定 ũ=ǔ 后最小化 L_tot，若收敛的 ū 仍等于 ǔ 则 ǔ 为稳定点；若对所有最优解都自转移则为强稳定点，否则为弱稳定点。据此给出单轮分解实现全局最优的必要条件：最优动作必须是唯一强稳定点。据此分析 WQMIX（存在 α 使最优动作根本不是稳定点）、QPLEX（存在多个次优稳定点）、ResQ（最优动作虽为唯一稳定点但必为弱稳定点，可能被丢失）的次优性成因。算法部分：提出多轮框架 MRVF，用"严格改进条件"（若上一轮贪心动作非最优，则本轮贪心动作的联合Q值必须严格更高）替代过严的唯一强稳定点条件，并证明满足该条件的无限序列必然在有限步内到达最优动作。设计上，把 Q_tot 的拟合目标从原始的 Q̂_jt 换成相对上一轮贪心动作的非负动作价值增量 max{Q̂_jt(τ,u^k) − Q̂_jt(τ,ū^{k-1}), 0}：所有劣质动作被赋为最小（零）目标值，所有优质动作保持正值，从而抑制劣解的局部高峰并补偿优解周围的低谷，实质上"提升"了目标函数对优质动作的单调性。理论上依托引理（理想QMIX在 ε→0 时收敛到 Q_jt(τ,ũ)）与定理（取最小值的动作不可能是稳定点，即中心化 ε-greedy 下的零目标动作被排除），保证严格改进条件成立。架构含个体Q网络（额外输入上一轮贪心动作的嵌入特征）、正权重混合网络、联合动作值网络（TD 学习 Q̂_jt）；前向过程逐轮迭代并在检测到无改进时提前终止输出上一轮动作；后向过程逐轮计算 L_tot（第1轮用原始 Q̂_jt，k>1 轮用增量目标）。采样策略：以概率 p 随机选用某一轮的贪心动作作为最终动作（覆盖各轮以获得准确目标），L_tot 中使用中心化 ε-greedy 策略以保证定理成立并避免 Q_tot 学习到 Q̂_jt 欠拟合区域。

Result: 实验覆盖三类基准并以中位数加25%–75%分位区间报告：(1) 新提出的 risk-reward 一步博弈（高维张量，仅少数联合动作为正、需多智能体达成共识否则重罚）：3智能体5动作时单调分解（QMIX、NA2Q）几乎只能得到最小正回报，QTRAN/WQMIX仅中等，MRVF稳定达到最优；5智能体时 MRVF 仍以高概率取最优，5智能体8动作时奖励过稀疏，MRVF 仍优于所有基线。(2) 捕食者-猎物任务（8智能体，惩罚0/-2/-5，非单调性随惩罚增强）：惩罚为0时各方法均好；惩罚加大后现有方法为规避惩罚而放弃"捕获"动作、回报趋近0，WQMIX仅在中等惩罚下可用，ResQ因弱稳定性波动剧烈，MRVF在-5下仍稳定最优。(3) SMAC（近单调场景）：MRVF 相对 QMIX/NA2Q 仍在 3s_vs_5z、2c_vs_64zg、bane_vs_bane 等场景占优，11个场景中6个最佳、仅0个最差（WQMIX 最差5个），整体鲁棒性与平均性能显著优于 QPLEX/ResQ/QTRAN/WQMIX；SMACv2 上 MRVF 平均回报略优但胜率相当；额外对比 MAPPO、CommFormer、GoMARL、HYGMA 等基线，前者胜率上升更慢、终局表现更低。消融显示：严格改进（增量目标）是高度非单调环境下性能的主要来源；单轮变体在高度非单调环境大幅退化，而在 SMAC 中多轮反而略有提升（额外提供他人意图信息）；捕食者-猎物中第二轮决策占比随惩罚从30%升至近50%，第三轮通常仅约5%，建议默认最多3轮。

Conclusion: 贡献在于两点：其一提出"稳定点"这一统一理论工具，把值分解中由梯度无关分量引起的贪心动作转移形式化为离散动力系统，解释了现有方法（尤其 QPLEX 的多稳定点、ResQ 的弱稳定点、WQMIX 的最优动作非稳定点）性能不佳的根源，并推导出单轮全局最优的必要条件；其二提出 MRVF，把"唯一强稳定点"这一几乎不可行的要求转化为可行的"逐轮严格改进"，通过动作价值增量裁剪将劣质动作变为不稳定点，在充分轮数下可获得全局最优动作。局限：计算复杂度由 QMIX 的 O(N) 升至 O(K(N+1))；Q_tot 的更新依赖 Q̂_jt 的结果导致相对标准 TD 学习延迟（3s_vs_5z 胜率上升偏慢）；实际可能失败的原因包括 Q̂_jt 的近似误差传播、单调混合网络的表达/优化误差、以及奖励过于稀疏（5智能体8动作场景）。此外方法可解释为并行执行框架下的多轮通信机制，而与 GVR、WQMIX 相比的优势在于无需预知最优动作即可逐轮排除劣解。

Abstract: Value factorization, a popular paradigm in MARL, faces significant theoretical and algorithmic bottlenecks: its tendency to converge to suboptimal solutions remains poorly understood and unsolved. Theoretically, existing analyses fail to explain this due to their primary focus on the optimal case. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel theoretical concept: the stable point, which characterizes the potential convergence of value factorization in general cases. Through an analysis of stable point distributions in existing methods, we reveal that non-optimal stable points are the primary cause of poor performance. However, algorithmically, making the optimal action the unique stable point is nearly infeasible. In contrast, iteratively filtering suboptimal actions by rendering them unstable emerges as a more practical approach for global optimality. Inspired by this, we propose a novel Multi-Round Value Factorization (MRVF) framework. Specifically, by measuring a non-negative payoff increment relative to the previously selected action, MRVF transforms inferior actions into unstable points, thereby driving each iteration toward a stable point with a superior action. Experiments on challenging benchmarks, including predator-prey tasks and StarCraft II Multi-Agent Challenge (SMAC), validate our analysis of stable points and demonstrate the superiority of MRVF over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [180] [Graph of Skills: Dependency-Aware Structural Retrieval for Massive Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05333)
*Dawei Li,Zongxia Li,Hongyang Du,Xiyang Wu,Shihang Gui,Yongbei Kuang,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Graph of Skills (GoS) 是一种用于大规模技能库推理时检索的结构化框架，通过离线构建技能图和在线选择性检索，在保持任务性能的同时显著降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体系统需要管理数千个可复用技能，但完整加载技能库会耗尽上下文窗口，导致成本增加、幻觉风险上升和延迟提高。现有简单向量检索方法无法捕捉技能间的依赖关系，无法在性能和效率间取得平衡。

Method: GoS包含三个核心阶段：①离线阶段从技能包构建可执行技能图；②推理时通过混合语义-词汇播种进行初始召回；③利用反向加权PageRank进行依赖感知排序；④通过上下文预算控制进行技能包填充。

Result: 在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上，GoS相比完整加载基线平均奖励提升43.6%，同时减少37.8%的输入token。在200至2000技能规模库上的消融实验持续验证了其对多种模型（Claude Sonnet、GPT-5.2 Codex、MiniMax）的有效性。

Conclusion: GoS通过结构化检索范式成功解决了大规模技能库的上下文窗口瓶颈问题，在多模型、多规模场景下证明了其在奖励、token效率和运行时性能之间的良好权衡能力。

Abstract: Skill usage has become a core component of modern agent systems and can substantially improve agents' ability to complete complex tasks. In real-world settings, where agents must monitor and interact with numerous personal applications, web browsers, and other environment interfaces, skill libraries can scale to thousands of reusable skills. Scaling to larger skill sets introduces two key challenges. First, loading the full skill set saturates the context window, driving up token costs, hallucination, and latency.
  In this paper, we present Graph of Skills (GoS), an inference-time structural retrieval layer for large skill libraries. GoS constructs an executable skill graph offline from skill packages, then at inference time retrieves a bounded, dependency-aware skill bundle through hybrid semantic-lexical seeding, reverse-weighted Personalized PageRank, and context-budgeted hydration. On SkillsBench and ALFWorld, GoS improves average reward by 43.6% over the vanilla full skill-loading baseline while reducing input tokens by 37.8%, and generalizes across three model families: Claude Sonnet, GPT-5.2 Codex, and MiniMax. Additional ablation studies across skill libraries ranging from 200 to 2,000 skills further demonstrate that GoS consistently outperforms both vanilla skills loading and simple vector retrieval in balancing reward, token efficiency, and runtime.

</details>


### [181] [TRACE: Capability-Targeted Agentic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05336)
*Hangoo Kang,Tarun Suresh,Jon Saad-Falcon,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE是一个端到端的智能体自我改进系统，通过对比成功与失败轨迹来识别LLM的能力缺陷，合成针对性的训练环境，并使用LoRA适配器进行强化学习训练，在客户服务和工具使用任务上分别提升了14.1分和7个满分，超越最强基线7.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在智能体能力训练中存在两个极端：要么依赖与目标环境能力缺陷不匹配的合成训练数据，要么直接在目标环境上训练，导致模型需要隐式学习跨任务能力。这导致训练效率低下且泛化能力不足。

Method: TRACE系统包含三个核心步骤：(1) 对比成功与失败轨迹，自动识别模型缺乏的能力；(2) 为每种能力合成针对性的训练环境，奖励该能力的执行；(3) 在每个合成环境上使用RL训练LoRA适配器，推理时动态路由到相关适配器。

Result: 在客户服务的τ²-bench上提升14.1分，在工具使用的ToolSandbox上获得7个满分，分别超越最强基线7.4分和4个满分。在相同rollout数量下，扩展效率优于GRPO和GEPA，分别超越9.2分和7.4分。

Conclusion: TRACE通过能力导向的合成环境训练，能够高效提升LLM在多种智能体环境中的表现，具有良好的跨环境泛化能力和扩展性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in agentic environments must exercise multiple capabilities across different task instances, where a capability is performing one or more actions in a trajectory that are necessary for successfully solving a subset of tasks in the environment. Many existing approaches either rely on synthetic training data that is not targeted to the model's actual capability deficits in the target environment or train directly on the target environment, where the model needs to implicitly learn the capabilities across tasks. We introduce TRACE (Turning Recurrent Agent failures into Capability-targeted training Environments), an end-to-end system for environment-specific agent self-improvement. TRACE contrasts successful and failed trajectories to automatically identify lacking capabilities, synthesizes a targeted training environment for each that rewards whether the capability was exercised, and trains a LoRA adapter via RL on each synthetic environment, routing to the relevant adapter at inference. Empirically, TRACE generalizes across different environments, improving over the base agent by +14.1 points on $τ^2$-bench (customer service) and +7 perfect scores on ToolSandbox (tool use), outperforming the strongest baseline by +7.4 points and +4 perfect scores, respectively. Given the same number of rollouts, TRACE scales more efficiently than baselines, outperforming GRPO and GEPA by +9.2 and +7.4 points on $τ^2$-bench.

</details>


### [182] [Dynamic Agentic AI Expert Profiler System Architecture for Multidomain Intelligence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05345)
*Aisvarya Adeseye,Jouni Isoaho,Seppo Virtanen,Mohammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于本地 LLaMA v3.1 (8B) 的智能体式"专家画像系统"（Expert Profiler），用模块化分层架构将自然语言回答自动划分为 Novice / Basic / Advanced / Expert 四个专业水平等级，并通过静态（82人转录）与动态（402人实时智能体访谈）两阶段评估验证：与参与者自评的一致率在结构化技术领域达 97–98%，在主观领域（隐私、游戏化、LLM 认知）为 83–95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 虽能自然地与人交流，但若要提供有意义的支持并建立信任，系统必须感知用户的专业水平（expertise level），从而自适应调整沟通风格与内容深度。现有文献中的"专家识别"系统（Borna et al. 的医疗 ML 模型、Bukowski et al. 的文本挖掘、Balog & Rijke 的概率匹配模型、Fazel-Zarand & Fox 的时序推荐系统、Fu & Akbar 的合著图网络）多依赖结构化数据、出版物或企业数据，存在隐私风险、数据完备性依赖、无法覆盖"隐性技能"等局限；尚无面向自然语言对话、能够动态判定专家水平的方法。同时，人工面试的自评存在自评偏差（高估/低估），且面试是"一次性"的，难以刻画专业能力的演变。

Method: 系统采用顺序但模块化的分层架构，可扩展、透明、灵活：①输入层（Transcript Collector 离线转录 + Response Stream 实时响应流）；②预处理层（文本归一化、切分分段、领域词典映射以消解歧义词如 token、fine-tuning）；③特征打分引擎（5 个并行模块——术语准确性 Terminology、深度理解 Depth、实际应用 Application、逻辑严谨 Rigor、不确定性表达 Uncertainty，各由 LLM 打 0–3 分；平均分 Score_avg 后施加惩罚 Penalty(-1，事实错误，下限 0) 与加分 Boost(+0.5，事实正确，上限 3)，调整后各特征分送入聚合层）；④聚合层计算加权维度——Relevancy=avg(Terminology,Application) 权重 0.5、Recency=avg(Terminology,Depth) 权重 0.3、Consistency=avg(Rigor,Uncertainty) 权重 0.2，权重和为 1，故 Score_final∈[0,3]；⑤分类层按阈值分段：Novice 0.0–0.7、Basic 0.8–1.4、Advanced 1.5–2.2、Expert 2.3–3.0，并含 Insufficient Evidence Detection 与 Justification Builder；⑥输出层提供 JSON 结构化结果与面向非技术用户的可读报告。实验实现基于 LLaMA v3.1 (8B)（因前期研究显示其在本地模型中性能可靠且算力需求低）。静态阶段：对 82 名关于"组织中引入游戏化的安全与隐私"的已录访谈转录做分析，参与者先自评，再与画像结果比对；动态阶段：由智能体 AI 面试官对 402 名参与者就 LLM 的认知、隐私与安全进行实时访谈，每答一问即估计一次专业水平并据此自适应选择下一题难度（内部估计不向参与者显示），最终输出各领域分类并与自评比对。另做收敛性分析：统计专业等级首次稳定且与自评一致所需的问题序号，以及首次达到 ±1 级容差与首次精确匹配的问题序号。

Result: 静态阶段（82人）：与自评一致率 Security 98%、Privacy 89%、Gamification 83%，差异以 ±1 级为主（如 Gamification 有 8% 被判更高、5% 判更低）。动态阶段（402人）：Security 97%、Privacy 95%、LLM Awareness 97%，差异几乎全部集中在 L1 级（略高估/略低估）。收敛性：第 1 题几乎无法给出稳定且精确的一致（Security 稳定匹配主要出现在 Q3–Q4，Privacy/LLM 认知需到 Q4–Q5），说明至少需 4 个精心设计的问题才能获得精确稳定结果；但仅需 1 题即有 92–100% 参与者进入 ±1 级"够用"区间，Q2 时所有领域 100% 达到 ±1 级；首次精确匹配方面，Security 全部在 Q2 达成，Privacy 在 Q3、LLM 认知在 Q3 达成。一旦进入 ±1 区间便保持稳定不再回退。动态模式中无人自评为 Novice，提示存在自我认知偏差与"不愿承认无知"倾向；差异来源归纳为自评过高/过低、回答含糊、LLM 对细微专业度的偶发误判。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了一个可解释、透明、模块化的智能体式专家画像架构能以较高准确率从自然语言回答中判定专业水平：在结构化领域（安全）表现最佳，主观领域（隐私、游戏化、LLM 认知）更难、需更多证据；动态自适应提问能快速收敛并保持稳定，最终评分不依赖自述信念而基于回答模式，因此更客观一致。系统适合用于教育、医疗、研究等需要实时感知用户能力的场景，也是快速筛查（1–2 题）与精细评估（4 题以上）的组合工具。局限与未来工作：回答模糊或高度主观时仍不稳定；需进一步精炼打分规则、扩展更多领域、优化模糊回答的评级策略。

Abstract: In today's artificial intelligence driven world, modern systems communicate with people from diverse backgrounds and skill levels. For human-machine interaction to be meaningful, systems must be aware of context and user expertise. This study proposes an agentic AI profiler that classifies natural language responses into four levels: Novice, Basic, Advanced, and Expert. The system uses a modular layered architecture built on LLaMA v3.1 (8B), with components for text preprocessing, scoring, aggregation, and classification. Evaluation was conducted in two phases: a static phase using pre-recorded transcripts from 82 participants, and a dynamic phase with 402 live interviews conducted by an agentic AI interviewer. In both phases, participant self-ratings were compared with profiler predictions. In the dynamic phase, expertise was assessed after each response rather than at the end of the interview. Across domains, 83% to 97% of profiler evaluations matched participant self-assessments. Remaining differences were due to self-rating bias, unclear responses, and occasional misinterpretation of nuanced expertise by the language model.

</details>


### [183] [From Retinal Evidence to Safe Decisions: RETINA-SAFE and ECRT for Hallucination Risk Triage in Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05348)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对医学大语言模型(LLM)在糖尿病视网膜病变(DR)诊断中的幻觉问题，提出RETINA-SAFE证据对齐基准和ECRT两阶段白盒检测框架，实现可解释的风险分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学LLM在证据不足或冲突时产生幻觉构成安全风险，尤其在糖尿病视网膜病变等关键诊断场景中可能危及患者安全。

Method: 1) 构建RETINA-SAFE基准数据集(12,522样本)，包含三类任务：E-Align(证据一致)、E-Conflict(证据冲突)、E-Gap(证据不足)；2) 提出ECRT两阶段白盒检测框架，Stage 1做Safe/Unsafe风险分类，Stage 2将unsafe案例细分为矛盾驱动型和证据缺口型风险；3) 利用CTX/NOCTX条件下的内部表征和logit偏移，结合类别平衡训练。

Result: ECRT在Stage 1风险分类中：(1) 相比外部不确定性和自一致性基线，平衡准确率提升+0.15至+0.19；(2) 相比最强监督基线提升+0.02至+0.07；(3) 一致性优于单阶段白盒消融实验。

Conclusion: 基于视网膜证据的白盒内部信号是实现可解释医学LLM风险分类的实用路径。

Abstract: Hallucinations in medical large language models (LLMs) remain a safety-critical issue, particularly when available evidence is insufficient or conflicting. We study this problem in diabetic retinopathy (DR) decision settings and introduce RETINA-SAFE, an evidence-grounded benchmark aligned with retinal grading records, comprising 12,522 samples. RETINA-SAFE is organized into three evidence-relation tasks: E-Align (evidence-consistent), E-Conflict (evidence-conflicting), and E-Gap (evidence-insufficient). We further propose ECRT (Evidence-Conditioned Risk Triage), a two-stage white-box detection framework: Stage 1 performs Safe/Unsafe risk triage, and Stage 2 refines unsafe cases into contradiction-driven versus evidence-gap risks. ECRT leverages internal representation and logit shifts under CTX/NOCTX conditions, with class-balanced training for robust learning. Under evidence-grouped (not patient-disjoint) splits across multiple backbones, ECRT provides strong Stage-1 risk triage and explicit subtype attribution, improves Stage-1 balanced accuracy by +0.15 to +0.19 over external uncertainty and self-consistency baselines and by +0.02 to +0.07 over the strongest adapted supervised baseline, and consistently exceeds a single-stage white-box ablation on Stage-1 balanced accuracy. These findings support white-box internal signals grounded in retinal evidence as a practical route to interpretable medical LLM risk triage.

</details>


### [184] [ETR: Entropy Trend Reward for Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05355)
*Xuan Xiong,Huan Liu,Li Gu,Zhixiang Chi,Yue Qiu,Yuanhao Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出熵趋势奖励（ETR）方法，通过分析推理过程中不确定性的轨迹而非静态熵值来提升LLM推理效率。ETR鼓励逐步不确定性降低的同时允许有限的局部探索，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时大幅缩短思维链长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT压缩方法使用长度惩罚或全局熵减，隐含假设推理全程低不确定性是理想的。但这种假设过于简化，未考虑推理过程中不确定性的动态变化规律。

Method: 1) 发现推理效率由不确定性轨迹决定，熵趋势以下降为主的CoT更短；2) 提出ETR（熵趋势奖励）目标函数，同时鼓励逐步熵减并允许局部探索；3) 与GRPO（群体相对策略优化）集成训练。

Result: 在多个推理模型和挑战性基准测试中一致性地实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡：DeepSeek-R1-Distill-7B在四个基准上准确率提升9.9%，同时CoT长度减少67%。

Conclusion: ETR通过轨迹感知的方式重新定义CoT压缩范式，验证了不确定性动态变化对推理效率的关键作用，为高效推理研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large language model performance on complex tasks, but often produces excessively long and inefficient reasoning traces. Existing methods shorten CoTs using length penalties or global entropy reduction, implicitly assuming that low uncertainty is desirable throughout reasoning. We show instead that reasoning efficiency is governed by the trajectory of uncertainty. CoTs with dominant downward entropy trends are substantially shorter. Motivated by this insight, we propose Entropy Trend Reward (ETR), a trajectory-aware objective that encourages progressive uncertainty reduction while allowing limited local exploration. We integrate ETR into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and evaluate it across multiple reasoning models and challenging benchmarks. ETR consistently achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency tradeoff, improving DeepSeek-R1-Distill-7B by 9.9% in accuracy while reducing CoT length by 67% across four benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Xuan1030/ETR

</details>


### [185] [LatentAudit: Real-Time White-Box Faithfulness Monitoring for Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Verifiable Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05358)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentAudit通过测量残差流激活的Mahalonobis距离，在无需辅助裁判模型的情况下实时检测RAG系统的幻觉问题，在多个基准和模型家族上达到0.94-0.98 AUROC，并支持Groth16公钥验证部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽能缓解幻觉但无法消除，部署系统需在推理时判断生成答案是否被检索证据支持，但现有方法依赖辅助模型、计算开销大或难以公开验证。

Method: LatentAudit采用白盒审计方式，从开源权重生成器中提取中后期残差流激活，测量其到证据表示的马氏距离(Mahalanobis distance)，形成二次判别规则。该规则无需辅助裁判模型、可在小型留出集上校准、支持Groth16零知识证明实现公开验证。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B的PubMedQA上达0.942 AUROC，延迟仅0.77ms；跨3个QA基准和5个模型家族(Llama-2/3, Qwen-2.5/3, Mistral)表现稳定；四路压力测试(矛盾、检索缺失、部分支持噪声)下达0.9566-0.9815 AUROC(PubMedQA)和0.9142-0.9315 AUROC(HotpotQA)；16位定点精度保留99.8%的FP16性能。

Conclusion: 残差流几何结构携带可用的可信度信号，该信号可跨架构迁移且对检索失败鲁棒，为实时RAG可信度监控和可选的可验证部署提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) mitigates hallucination but does not eliminate it: a deployed system must still decide, at inference time, whether its answer is actually supported by the retrieved evidence. We introduce LatentAudit, a white-box auditor that pools mid-to-late residual-stream activations from an open-weight generator and measures their Mahalanobis distance to the evidence representation. The resulting quadratic rule requires no auxiliary judge model, runs at generation time, and is simple enough to calibrate on a small held-out set. We show that residual-stream geometry carries a usable faithfulness signal, that this signal survives architecture changes and realistic retrieval failures, and that the same rule remains amenable to public verification. On PubMedQA with Llama-3-8B, LatentAudit reaches 0.942 AUROC with 0.77,ms overhead. Across three QA benchmarks and five model families (Llama-2/3, Qwen-2.5/3, Mistral), the monitor remains stable; under a four-way stress test with contradictions, retrieval misses, and partial-support noise, it reaches 0.9566--0.9815 AUROC on PubMedQA and 0.9142--0.9315 on HotpotQA. At 16-bit fixed-point precision, the audit rule preserves 99.8% of the FP16 AUROC, enabling Groth16-based public verification without revealing model weights or activations. Together, these results position residual-stream geometry as a practical basis for real-time RAG faithfulness monitoring and optional verifiable deployment.

</details>


### [186] [TFRBench: A Reasoning Benchmark for Evaluating Forecasting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05364)
*Md Atik Ahamed,Mihir Parmar,Palash Goyal,Yiwen Song,Long T. Le,Qiang Cheng,Chun-Liang Li,Hamid Palangi,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TFRBench是首个用于评估预测系统推理能力的基准测试框架，采用多智能体迭代验证框架生成数值驱动的推理轨迹，在10个数据集5个领域的实验中验证了推理的有效性，并将预测准确率从40.2%提升至56.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测仅以数值准确度评估，将基础模型视为"黑箱"，缺乏对预测系统生成推理能力的系统性评估协议。现有基准测试无法评价模型在跨通道依赖、趋势分析和外部事件方面的推理质量。

Method: 提出TFRBench基准测试，采用系统化多智能体框架，通过迭代验证循环合成数值锚定的推理轨迹。框架在10个数据集、5个领域上评估模型对交叉通道依赖、趋势和外部事件的推理能力，并使用LLM-as-Judge进行推理质量评估。

Result: 实验验证了生成推理的因果有效性和实用性。使用生成的推理轨迹提示LLM可显著提升预测准确率（平均从40.2%提升至56.6%）。现成LLM在推理（LLM-as-Judge评分较低）和数值预测两方面均表现不佳，常无法捕捉领域特定动态。

Conclusion: TFRBench建立了时间序列预测领域可解释、基于推理评估的新标准，填补了传统数值评估的空白，为未来可解释AI预测系统的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce TFRBench, the first benchmark designed to evaluate the reasoning capabilities of forecasting systems. Traditionally, time-series forecasting has been evaluated solely on numerical accuracy, treating foundation models as ``black boxes.'' Unlike existing benchmarks, TFRBench provides a protocol for evaluating the reasoning generated by forecasting systems--specifically their analysis of cross-channel dependencies, trends, and external events. To enable this, we propose a systematic multi-agent framework that utilizes an iterative verification loop to synthesize numerically grounded reasoning traces. Spanning ten datasets across five domains, our evaluation confirms that this reasoning is causally effective; useful for evaluation; and prompting LLMs with our generated traces significantly improves forecasting accuracy compared to direct numerical prediction (e.g., avg. $\sim40.2\%\to56.6\%)$, validating the quality of our reasoning. Conversely, benchmarking experiments reveal that off-the-shelf LLMs consistently struggle with both reasoning (lower LLM-as-a-Judge scores) and numerical forecasting, frequently failing to capture domain-specific dynamics. TFRBench thus establishes a new standard for interpretable, reasoning-based evaluation in time-series forecasting. Our benchmark is available at: https://tfrbench.github.io

</details>


### [187] [LLM-as-Judge for Semantic Judging of Powerline Segmentation in UAV Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05371)
*Akram Hossain,Rabab Abdelfattah,Xiaofeng Wang,Kareem Abdelfatah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用大语言模型(LLM)作为语义裁判，评估无人机轻量级分割模型对电力线路的分割质量可靠性。通过可重复性和感知敏感性两个评估协议，验证LLM在视觉退化条件下能保持一致的判断并适当降低置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机电力线路巡检中，轻量级分割模型虽能实时推理，但在真实复杂环境下性能可能不可预测地退化，威胁安全运行。需要一种可靠的方法来评估分割结果的置信度，而无需引入新的检测系统。

Method: 设计"看门狗"场景，将分割结果叠加图发送给offboard LLM进行评估。建立两个评估协议：(1)可重复性评估——使用相同输入和固定提示词重复查询，测量质量评分和置信度稳定性；(2)感知敏感性评估——引入受控视觉干扰(雾、雨、雪、阴影、眩光)，分析模型对分割质量渐进退化的响应。

Result: LLM在相同条件下产生高度一致的分类判断；当视觉可靠性下降时表现出适当的置信度下降；即使在挑战性条件下，仍能对缺失或误识别的电力线路等感知线索做出响应。

Conclusion: 经过精心约束的LLM可作为安全关键的航空巡检任务中监控分割质量的可靠语义裁判。

Abstract: The deployment of lightweight segmentation models on drones for autonomous power line inspection presents a critical challenge: maintaining reliable performance under real-world conditions that differ from training data. Although compact architectures such as U-Net enable real-time onboard inference, their segmentation outputs can degrade unpredictably in adverse environments, raising safety concerns. In this work, we study the feasibility of using a large language model (LLM) as a semantic judge to assess the reliability of power line segmentation results produced by drone-mounted models. Rather than introducing a new inspection system, we formalize a watchdog scenario in which an offboard LLM evaluates segmentation overlays and examine whether such a judge can be trusted to behave consistently and perceptually coherently. To this end, we design two evaluation protocols that analyze the judge's repeatability and sensitivity. First, we assess repeatability by repeatedly querying the LLM with identical inputs and fixed prompts, measuring the stability of its quality scores and confidence estimates. Second, we evaluate perceptual sensitivity by introducing controlled visual corruptions (fog, rain, snow, shadow, and sunflare) and analyzing how the judge's outputs respond to progressive degradation in segmentation quality. Our results show that the LLM produces highly consistent categorical judgments under identical conditions while exhibiting appropriate declines in confidence as visual reliability deteriorates. Moreover, the judge remains responsive to perceptual cues such as missing or misidentified power lines, even under challenging conditions. These findings suggest that, when carefully constrained, an LLM can serve as a reliable semantic judge for monitoring segmentation quality in safety-critical aerial inspection tasks.

</details>


### [188] [CODESTRUCT: Code Agents over Structured Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05407)
*Myeongsoo Kim,Joe Hsu,Dingmin Wang,Shweta Garg,Varun Kumar,Murali Krishna Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CODESTRUCT通过将代码库重新框架化为结构化动作空间，使LLM智能体操作AST实体而非文本跨度，显著提升代码编辑准确率并降低资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码智能体将代码库视为无结构文本，通过脆弱的字符串匹配应用编辑，常因格式漂移或模糊模式而失败。

Method: CODESTRUCT框架：引入readCode（检索完整语法单元）和editCode（语法验证的语义转换）接口，使智能体基于命名AST实体而非文本跨度操作；评估于SWE-Bench Verified（6个LLM）和CodeAssistBench。

Result: SWE-Bench上Pass@1提升1.2-5.0%，token消耗降低12-38%；GPT-5-nano提升20.8%（空补丁失败率从46.6%降至7.2%）；CodeAssistBench上准确率提升0.8-4.4%，成本降低最多33%。

Conclusion: 结构化接口（AST实体操作）为代码智能体提供了更可靠的基础，能在保持或提升准确率的同时显著降低token消耗和空补丁失败率。

Abstract: LLM-based code agents treat repositories as unstructured text, applying edits through brittle string matching that frequently fails due to formatting drift or ambiguous patterns. We propose reframing the codebase as a structured action space where agents operate on named AST entities rather than text spans. Our framework, CODESTRUCT, provides readCode for retrieving complete syntactic units and editCode for applying syntax-validated transformations to semantic program elements. Evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified across six LLMs, CODESTRUCT improves Pass@1 accuracy by 1.2-5.0% while reducing token consumption by 12-38% for most models. Models that frequently fail to produce valid patches under text-based interfaces benefit most: GPT-5-nano improves by 20.8% as empty-patch failures drop from 46.6% to 7.2%. On CodeAssistBench, we observe consistent accuracy gains (+0.8-4.4%) with cost reductions up to 33%. Our results show that structure-aware interfaces offer a more reliable foundation for code agents.

</details>


### [189] [Towards Effective In-context Cross-domain Knowledge Transfer via Domain-invariant-neurons-based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05383)
*Jianzhi Yan,Zhiming Li,Le Liu,Zike Yuan,Shiwei Chen,Youcheng Pan,Buzhou Tang,Yang Xiang,Danny Dongning Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DIN-Retrieval方法，通过领域不变神经元构建通用表示向量，从跨领域示例中检索结构兼容的推理示范，以提升大语言模型在数学与逻辑推理任务中的性能，平均提升1.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM推理提升策略依赖专家手工制作的领域内示范，在专业数学推理、形式逻辑或法律分析等专业知识稀缺的领域适用性受限。

Method: DIN-Retrieval首先从隐藏表示中总结跨域通用表示向量（领域不变神经元），推理阶段利用该DIN向量检索结构兼容的跨域示范用于上下文学习。

Result: 在数学与逻辑推理的迁移实验中，该方法相比现有最优方法平均提升1.8分。

Conclusion: 跨领域存在可复用的隐式逻辑结构，领域不变神经元可有效桥接不同领域，实现跨域示范的灵活迁移。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made notable progress in logical reasoning, yet still fall short of human-level performance. Current boosting strategies rely on expert-crafted in-domain demonstrations, limiting their applicability in expertise-scarce domains, such as specialized mathematical reasoning, formal logic, or legal analysis. In this work, we demonstrate the feasibility of leveraging cross-domain demonstrating examples to boost the LLMs' reasoning performance. Despite substantial domain differences, many reusable implicit logical structures are shared across domains. In order to effectively retrieve cross-domain examples for unseen domains under investigation, in this work, we further propose an effective retrieval method, called domain-invariant neurons-based retrieval (\textbf{DIN-Retrieval}). Concisely, DIN-Retrieval first summarizes a hidden representation that is universal across different domains. Then, during the inference stage, we use the DIN vector to retrieve structurally compatible cross-domain demonstrations for the in-context learning. Experimental results in multiple settings for the transfer of mathematical and logical reasoning demonstrate that our method achieves an average improvement of 1.8 over the state-of-the-art methods \footnote{Our implementation is available at https://github.com/Leon221220/DIN-Retrieval}.

</details>


### [190] [Neural Assistive Impulses: Synthesizing Exaggerated Motions for Physics-based Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05394)
*Zhiquan Wang,Bedrich Benes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper proposes Assistive Impulse Neural Control, a framework that uses impulse-space assistance instead of force-space to help physics-based character animation achieve exaggerated, stylized motions (like instantaneous dashes) that were previously impossible. It combines analytic inverse dynamics with learned residual corrections via a hybrid neural policy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current data-driven DRL methods struggle to reproduce exaggerated, stylized motions (e.g., instantaneous dashes, mid-air trajectory changes) required in animation but violating physical laws. This limitation arises because characters are modeled as underactuated floating-base systems where internal torques and momentum conservation govern motion. Direct external wrenches cause training instability due to velocity discontinuities producing sparse, high-magnitude force spikes.

Method: The framework reformulates external assistance in impulse space (instead of force space) for numerical stability. The assistive signal is decomposed into: (1) an analytic high-frequency component derived from Inverse Dynamics, and (2) a learned low-frequency residual correction governed by a hybrid neural policy.

Result: The method enables robust tracking of highly agile, dynamically infeasible maneuvers that were previously intractable for physics-based methods.

Conclusion: Assistive Impulse Neural Control successfully enables physics-based characters to track highly agile, dynamically infeasible maneuvers that were previously intractable, providing a numerically stable solution for exaggerated stylized motion synthesis.

Abstract: Physics-based character animation has become a fundamental approach for synthesizing realistic, physically plausible motions. While current data-driven deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods can synthesize complex skills, they struggle to reproduce exaggerated, stylized motions, such as instantaneous dashes or mid-air trajectory changes, which are required in animation but violate standard physical laws. The primary limitation stems from modeling the character as an underactuated floating-base system, in which internal joint torques and momentum conservation strictly govern motion. Direct attempts to enforce such motions via external wrenches often lead to training instability, as velocity discontinuities produce sparse, high-magnitude force spikes that prevent policy convergence. We propose Assistive Impulse Neural Control, a framework that reformulates external assistance in impulse space rather than force space to ensure numerical stability. We decompose the assistive signal into an analytic high-frequency component derived from Inverse Dynamics and a learned low-frequency residual correction, governed by a hybrid neural policy. We demonstrate that our method enables robust tracking of highly agile, dynamically infeasible maneuvers that were previously intractable for physics-based methods.

</details>


### [191] [Reason Analogically via Cross-domain Prior Knowledge: An Empirical Study of Cross-domain Knowledge Transfer for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05396)
*Le Liu,Zhiming Li,Jianzhi Yan,Zike Yuan,Shiwei Chen,Youcheng Pan,Buzhou Tang,Qingcai Chen,Yang Xiang,Danny Dongning Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨域上下文学习的可行性，提出不同领域可能共享底层推理结构，使得源域演示可提升目标域推理性能。通过大量实验验证了跨域知识的条件性正向迁移，发现了"示例吸收阈值"现象，并揭示性能提升源于推理结构修复而非语义线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习依赖领域内专家标注，限制了其在专家标注稀缺场景下的应用。研究者假设不同领域可能共享底层推理结构，从而实现跨域知识迁移。

Method: 采用综合实证研究方法，测试不同检索方法在跨域上下文学习场景下的表现，验证跨域知识迁移的可行性。

Result: 实验证明跨域上下文学习存在条件性正向迁移。发现了明确的"示例吸收阈值"：超过该阈值后，正向迁移更可能发生，且额外演示带来更大收益。进一步分析表明，这些收益源于跨域示例对推理结构的修复，而非语义线索。

Conclusion: 研究验证了利用跨域知识迁移提升跨域上下文学习性能的可行性，为设计更有效的跨域检索方法开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Despite its success, existing in-context learning (ICL) relies on in-domain expert demonstrations, limiting its applicability when expert annotations are scarce. We posit that different domains may share underlying reasoning structures, enabling source-domain demonstrations to improve target-domain inference despite semantic mismatch. To test this hypothesis, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study of different retrieval methods to validate the feasibility of achieving cross-domain knowledge transfer under the in-context learning setting. Our results demonstrate conditional positive transfer in cross-domain ICL. We identify a clear example absorption threshold: beyond it, positive transfer becomes more likely, and additional demonstrations yield larger gains. Further analysis suggests that these gains stem from reasoning structure repair by retrieved cross-domain examples, rather than semantic cues. Overall, our study validates the feasibility of leveraging cross-domain knowledge transfer to improve cross-domain ICL performance, motivating the community to explore designing more effective retrieval approaches for this novel direction.\footnote{Our implementation is available at https://github.com/littlelaska/ICL-TF4LR}

</details>


### [192] [HYVE: Hybrid Views for LLM Context Engineering over Machine Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05400)
*Jian Tan,Fan Bu,Yuqing Gao,Dev Khanolkar,Jason Mackay,Boris Sobolev,Lei Jin,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HYVE 是一个面向"机器数据"提示词的 LLM 上下文工程框架，用数据库管理思想把长、嵌套、重复的 JSON/Python 字面量负载重组成"列视图+行视图"的混合视图，仅向 LLM 暴露经统计排序挑选的子集，同时把全量数据连同 schema 存入请求级内存数据仓库（DuckDB）。被隐藏的信息可在后处理阶段通过 SQL 查询与模板回填精确恢复。实验表明，HYVE 在 11 个真实网络运维基准上把 token 消耗降低 50–90%，质量持平或提升（图表生成准确率最高提升约 132%，延迟最多降低 83%），本质上实现了"可恢复压缩"而非有损压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统的可观测性数据（日志、指标、遥测、配置快照）以自然语言与 JSON/Python AST 字面量混合的形式进入 LLM 提示词，存在三类顽固问题：(1) token 爆炸——嵌套键与重复 schema 挤占上下文并割裂相关证据；(2) context rot——模型在庞大负载中找不到"针"并偏离指令；(3) 数值/类别序列推理薄弱——长序列掩盖异常、趋势与实体关联。现有方案各有缺陷：LLMLingua/Selective Context 等基于语义的有损压缩一旦删除即不可恢复；智能体记忆以摘要压缩历史；约束解码与输出修复只保证语法正确，无法解决"结构合法但数组被截断、数据不完整"这一失效模式。此外，grep/awk 等 shell 工具不擅长对跨段嵌套机器数据做过滤、聚合与连接，而预先并不存在可用数据库。作者的核心诉求是：上下文缩减必须是结构性的（structural）且可恢复的（recoverable），而非语义性的、有损的；同时保证数据保真度（每一部分要么在上下文中逐字可见，要么带显式 schema 存于数据仓库，可被确定性取回）。

Method: 框架围绕一次主 LLM 调用，前置 preprocessor 与后置 postprocessor 协调工作，全部状态限定在单请求作用域内。

**预处理（Preprocessor）**：用弹性解析器将原始字符串 s 分解为交错的自由文本段与 JSON/Python 字面量对象 J（Algorithm 2：去 markdown 围栏→快速解析（canonical JSON、Python literal）→递归 ExpandNestedJSON 展开字符串内嵌负载→失败则修复阶段（控制字符归一、补引号、修复畸形 JSON、解码转义 JSON、抽取并补全内嵌 JSON 跨度），并以字母词重叠覆盖率校验以防静默截断，未恢复残留保留在 unparsed_string）。随后构建 JSON 树、扁平化为 JSONPath 式点路径、按索引抽象后的模式聚类（data.*.x），并自底向上做"schema 一致性"检查（递归深度 schema 签名，int/float 归一为 number；不一致的列表保留显式索引、不做列合并），防止无关对象被错误对齐。

**混合视图（Hybrid View）**：定义 Col(J) 列式表示（按"重复上下文路径 c + 扁平字段路径 p"构成列标识，值按原始遍历顺序成列，利于跨字段抽取与数值分析）与 Row(J) 行式表示（每个 schema 一致的完整记录整体保留，保持字段共现关联，利于实体级关联推理），二者按 t₁⊕Col(J₁)⊕…⊕t_{m+1}⊕Row(J) 拼接。

**截断与排序**：用参考感知的 BM25 排序决定暴露哪些表项——查询串 q 由周边文本段与 Slim(J)（表外所有键值对）拼成；列视图中各 schema 组内 IDF 仅用 value token（schema token 无区分力），取前缀+后缀+排名项并保持原序；行视图候选为跨表并集，使用 schema+value token 的全局 IDF，无序展示代表性记录。

**数据仓库与 SQL**：将未截断数据载入 DuckDB 内存库（每请求一个 :memory: 库，请求结束即销毁、跨请求隔离），嵌套数组按 parent_<id> 外键规范化为父子表，平行列表结构转为带 series_idx 的扁平表；自动生成 schema 感知的工具提示与少样本示例指导 SQL 生成，并按"渐进披露"不对称地暴露 QueryDatastore（模式三，主调用即给全量契约）与 GenTemplateAndBackfill（模式二，主调用仅给入口，选择后才揭示 BackfillData 契约）。

**后处理（Postprocessor）**：模式一直接输出；模式二模板回填——LLM 生成答案模板 + BackfillData 工具调用（SQL + 列到模板路径映射，路径支持 (series_idx) 分组与 (N) 位置重建），由执行器按组/扁平/回退三种情形确定性重建被截断数组，同时保留模型生成的 schema；模式三 SQL 增强综合——执行 QueryDatastore，将结果追加进上下文后发起至多一次有界的额外 LLM 调用做有据综合。默认每请求至多 2 次 LLM 调用。

**算子**：列/行排序选择、n-sigma 异常检测（可作 SQL 函数 DETECT_ANOMALY(data, y) 暴露，配合周期性检查抑制误报，输出紧凑的异常值+上下文+正常统计摘要）、渲染算子（美化 JSON 为默认、raw JSON、TOON 编码可节省最高 31% token 但可能损质）、HTML 算子。

Result: 在 11 个真实网络运维基准（Cert-QA 3524 题、Runbook 260、Line 100、Bar 100、Anom 797、Sum 84、Canvas 4096 槽位填充、RB-Text、RB-JSON、Hard 多跳推理、TOON-QA 154）上对比 GPT-4.1/GPT-5 基线。

质量：Line 图相似度 0.68→0.97（GPT-5）、0.41→0.95（GPT-4.1），Bar 0.85→1.00，Anom 3.28→3.77，Hard 4.33→5.00，TOON-QA 精确匹配 0.47→0.93；Cert-QA/Runbook/Sum/RB-Text 等知识与文本任务质量基本持平无回退；Line 数据集中 76% 样本达到相似度 1.0（基线 44% 完全失败）。
Token：整体降低 50–90%，典型如 Line 4.2M→748K（−82%）、Bar 1.8M→252K（−86%）、Canvas 122.8M→38.2M（−69%）、TOON-QA 1.3M→154K（−88%）。
延迟：Line 125s→40s（−68%）、Bar 75s→13s（−83%）；例外是 Mode 3 额外调用主导的场景——TOON-QA（GPT-4.1 1.35s→2.33s）与 RB-Text（2.74s→4.53s）反而变慢。

消融（GPT-4.1）：关闭后处理最致命——Bar 相似度 0.99→0.06（−94%，证实 LLM 常在生成中截断数据数组），TOON-QA 0.93→0.38（−59%），Hard 5.00→3.93，Anom 4.03→3.41；关闭截断导致 token 激增 3–7 倍（Canvas +239%、Bar +601%、Hard +395%）且质量下降（Bar 0.73、Hard 4.10），印证长上下文引发 context rot；关闭排序使 RB-Text 4.89→4.56、TOON-QA 0.93→0.91 且 token 反增；TOON 编码省 6–18% token 但质量略降，故默认用美化 JSON。与领域模型 DNM 叠加后 Runbook 达 4.61，说明推理期上下文工程与训练期领域后训练互补。

Conclusion: HYVE 表明，针对机器数据主导的提示词，上下文缩减应当是结构性的、可恢复的，而不是语义性的、有损的：把嵌套负载重排为列视图（利于跨字段抽取与统计）与行视图（利于实体关联与多跳推理）的混合视图，把全量保真数据留在带 schema 的请求级数据仓库中，从而在大幅压缩可见提示词的同时不损失底层证据的可达性，形成对"近乎无界上下文窗口"的有效工程近似。其贡献可归纳为：形式化了 schema 一致性、列/行表示与 Rep 算子；提出基于参考感知 BM25 的可恢复截断与渐进披露的 SQL 工具链；并用包含输出修复在内的实证说明——大多数被截断的数组可由确定性回填精确恢复，这对需要数据完整性（图表、报告、结构化产出）的 agentic 工作负载尤其关键。局限方面：HYVE 是请求级的短期记忆，不提供跨会话持久化，与 LongMem/MemGPT/Mem0 等长期记忆正交但可结合；它假定重复结构已表示为 JSON/Python 字面量，对嵌在自由文本中的日志组件（需先自动结构化）尚不适用；此外论文的主要数据集为专有数据，仅 TOON-QA 为公开基准，评测多依赖 LLM-as-a-judge（GenericJudge/ReasoningJudge），存在主观性与数据选择偏差；Mode 3 的额外 LLM 调用在基线已很快时反而增加延迟。

Abstract: Machine data is central to observability and diagnosis in modern computing systems, appearing in logs, metrics, telemetry traces, and configuration snapshots. When provided to large language models (LLMs), this data typically arrives as a mixture of natural language and structured payloads such as JSON or Python/AST literals. Yet LLMs remain brittle on such inputs, particularly when they are long, deeply nested, and dominated by repetitive structure.
  We present HYVE (HYbrid ViEw), a framework for LLM context engineering for inputs containing large machine-data payloads, inspired by database management principles. HYVE surrounds model invocation with coordinated preprocessing and postprocessing, centered on a request-scoped datastore augmented with schema information. During preprocessing, HYVE detects repetitive structure in raw inputs, materializes it in the datastore, transforms it into hybrid columnar and row-oriented views, and selectively exposes only the most relevant representation to the LLM. During postprocessing, HYVE either returns the model output directly, queries the datastore to recover omitted information, or performs a bounded additional LLM call for SQL-augmented semantic synthesis.
  We evaluate HYVE on diverse real-world workloads spanning knowledge QA, chart generation, anomaly detection, and multi-step network troubleshooting. Across these benchmarks, HYVE reduces token usage by 50-90% while maintaining or improving output quality. On structured generation tasks, it improves chart-generation accuracy by up to 132% and reduces latency by up to 83%. Overall, HYVE offers a practical approximation to an effectively unbounded context window for prompts dominated by large machine-data payloads.

</details>


### [193] [Multi-Agent Pathfinding with Non-Unit Integer Edge Costs via Enhanced Conflict-Based Search and Graph Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05416)
*Hongkai Fan,Qinjing Xie,Bo Ouyang,Yaonan Wang,Zhi Yan,Jiawen He,Zheng Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAPFZ，一种在非单位整数成本图上的多智能体路径规划变体，通过CBS-NIC算法和BOGD离散化方法解决传统MAPF的局限性，在基准测试中展现出更优的运行时间和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAPF方法假设单位边成本和单时间步动作，限制了其在现实场景中的应用；现有MAPFR虽处理非单位成本和连续时间动作，但几何碰撞模型导致状态空间无界，影响求解器效率。

Method: 1) 提出MAPFZ变体，保持有限状态空间同时提高真实感；2) 开发CBS-NIC框架，基于时间间隔冲突检测和改进的SIPP算法；3) 提出BOGD方法用于非单位边成本离散化，具有次线性遗憾界。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种基准场景中的运行时间和成功率均优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: MAPFZ结合CBS-NIC和BOGD为非单位成本多智能体路径规划提供了高效且准确的解决方案，兼顾状态空间有限性与求解效率。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Pathfinding (MAPF) plays a critical role in various domains. Traditional MAPF methods typically assume unit edge costs and single-timestep actions, which limit their applicability to real-world scenarios. MAPFR extends MAPF to handle non-unit costs with real-valued edge costs and continuous-time actions, but its geometric collision model leads to an unbounded state space that compromises solver efficiency. In this paper, we propose MAPFZ, a novel MAPF variant on graphs with non-unit integer costs that preserves a finite state space while offering improved realism over classical MAPF. To solve MAPFZ efficiently, we develop CBS-NIC, an enhanced Conflict-Based Search framework incorporating time-interval-based conflict detection and an improved Safe Interval Path Planning (SIPP) algorithm. Additionally, we propose Bayesian Optimization for Graph Design (BOGD), a discretization method for non-unit edge costs that balances efficiency and accuracy with a sub-linear regret bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods in runtime and success rate across diverse benchmark scenarios.

</details>


### [194] [PRISM-MCTS: Learning from Reasoning Trajectories with Metacognitive Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05424)
*Siyuan Cheng,Bozhong Tian,YanChao Hao,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM-MCTS 将过程奖励模型（PRM）与动态双记忆（Heuristics/Fallacies Memory）结合进蒙特卡洛树搜索，使各次 rollout 共享全局经验、复用已验证推理步骤并提前剪枝错误分支；同时提出结合 Step-wise DPO 与五分类的两阶段少样本 PRM 训练法，在 GPQA-Diamond 上以约一半的搜索轨迹数超越 MCTS-RAG 与 Search-o1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型（如 OpenAI o1）将扩展规律从预训练转向测试时计算，MCTS 成为实现"慢思考"的重要路径。但现有方法把每次 rollout 视为孤立轨迹，节点间无信息共享，导致严重的计算冗余：同一逻辑错误被反复探索、已验证的正确步骤被重复推演。在资源受限或时延敏感场景下，如何在搜索质量与效率之间取得平衡是核心瓶颈。同时，高质量 PRM 通常依赖大规模过程标注数据，在数据稀缺领域难以获得，使 MCTS 缺少中间引导而效率低下。已有记忆增强推理方法（MC-DML、I-MCTS、CoAT）又普遍无差别地聚合经验，未显式区分有效逻辑模式与失败模式，易导致错误复现。

Method: 框架层面：PRISM-MCTS（Process-Rewarded Intelligent Shared Memory MCTS）将传统串行 MCTS 改造为支持全局信息共享的并行化搜索，包含三个核心模块：(1) Heuristics Memory，节点价值 v_k 超过阈值 τ_pos 时归档优质中间状态与成功轨迹，供后续节点复用已验证逻辑以加速收敛；(2) Fallacies Memory，v_k 低于 τ_neg 时归档错误模式，作为前置约束避免同类错误重复消耗算力；(3) Memory Manager，蒸馏关键模式、过滤冗余条目，保证记忆的信息简约性与高保真度。在选择阶段使用 UCT 公式变体 argmax(V_C' + ε√(2 ln N_C / N_C'))，扩展阶段由 FindChilds(C, MEM_F, MEM_H) 结合记忆生成子节点，模拟阶段按阈值将节点写入两类记忆，回传阶段更新访问计数与价值。PRM 训练层面：沿用 ReST-MCTS* 的自训练数据构造，对部分解 p_k 计算质量值 v_k = max(v_{k-1}+w_{s_k}, 0)，其中 w_{s_k} = (1-v_{k-1})/(m_k+1)·(1-2r_{s_k})，m_k 为到正确答案的最小步数、r_{s_k} 为步级错误指示；第一阶段用 Step-wise DPO（高价值步 v_k≥0.8 为偏好对，低质量步 v_k≤0.2 为负例）；第二阶段将值估计由回归改为五分类（Perfect/Good/Fair/Poor/Bad，间隔 0.2）的交叉熵训练，以提升少样本泛化。

Result: 在 GPT-4.1-mini 与 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507 两个骨干上与 Zero-shot CoT、ReAct、Search-o1、ReST-MCTS*、MCTS-RAG 对比。GPQA-Diamond 上 EM 分别达 65.08% 与 65.15%，优于 MCTS-RAG（64.65/63.64）与 Search-o1（57.07/61.11）；FMT 上 GPT-4.1-mini 的 EM 70.50/F1 70.07 为最佳，Qwen 骨干略低于 MCTS-RAG；MATH500 与 AIME25 上 GPT-4.1-mini 得 82.09 与 53.33，领先各基线，Qwen 骨干与 MCTS-RAG 相当。效率方面，GPQA 上平均轨迹数由 18.76 降至 8.40（降幅逾 55%），AIME25（Qwen）由 6.73 降至 2.40，深度保持稳定或略降。消融显示去掉全部记忆后 MATH500 分数由约 0.82 降至约 0.77，且 MCTS-RAG 需 2–4 倍轨迹数。双阶段训练的 Light-PRM（Qwen-3-4B 本地微调）在 EM/F1 上几乎与 Gemini-2.5-Pro 的 Oracle-PRM 打平（如 70.50/69.91 vs 70.50/70.07），仅搜索略宽，验证了少样本 PRM 的可行性。

Conclusion: 记忆反思机制可显著提升 MCTS 的推理效率与质量：通过共享已验证的启发式并前置拦截谬误分支，PRISM-MCTS 将"暴力扩展"转为"审慎推理"，在更少搜索步骤下达到更高准确率；配合资源高效的两阶段 PRM 训练，本地轻量模型即可逼近顶级闭源 PRM 的保真度，兼顾成本与数据隐私。局限包括：受资源限制 PRM 训练数据规模偏小，领域适配能力可能未达上限；当前系统仅支持纯文本推理，尚未扩展到视觉、图表等多模态场景。

Abstract: PRISM-MCTS: Learning from Reasoning Trajectories with Metacognitive Reflection Siyuan Cheng, Bozhong Tian, Yanchao Hao, Zheng Wei Published: 06 Apr 2026, Last Modified: 06 Apr 2026 ACL 2026 Findings Conference, Area Chairs, Reviewers, Publication Chairs, Authors Revisions BibTeX CC BY 4.0 Keywords: Efficient/Low-Resource Methods for NLP, Generation, Question Answering Abstract: The emergence of reasoning models, exemplified by OpenAI o1, signifies a transition from intuitive to deliberative cognition, effectively reorienting the scaling laws from pre-training paradigms toward test-time computation. While Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) has shown promise in this domain, existing approaches typically treat each rollout as an isolated trajectory. This lack of information sharing leads to severe inefficiency and substantial computational redundancy, as the search process fails to leverage insights from prior explorations. To address these limitations, we propose PRISM-MCTS, a novel reasoning framework that draws inspiration from human parallel thinking and reflective processes. PRISM-MCTS integrates a Process Reward Model (PRM) with a dynamic shared memory, capturing both "Heuristics" and "Fallacies". By reinforcing successful strategies and pruning error-prone branches, PRISM-MCTS effectively achieves refinement. Furthermore, we develop a data-efficient training strategy for the PRM, achieving high-fidelity evaluation under a few-shot regime. Empirical evaluations across diverse reasoning benchmarks substantiate the efficacy of PRISM-MCTS. Notably, it halves the trajectory requirements on GPQA while surpassing MCTS-RAG and Search-o1, demonstrating that it scales inference by reasoning judiciously rather than exhaustively.

</details>


### [195] [Automated Auditing of Hospital Discharge Summaries for Care Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05435)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用本地部署的大语言模型(LLM)实现出院摘要的大规模自动化审计框架，基于DISCHARGED框架将护理交接核心要求转化为结构化验证清单，在MIMIC-IV数据库上验证了自动化临床审计的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院文档不完整或不一致是护理碎片化和可避免再入院的主要驱动因素，尽管其对患者安全至关重要，但出院摘要的审计高度依赖人工审查，难以规模化扩展。

Method: 将护理交接核心要求（随访指示、用药史及变更、患者信息、临床过程等）基于DISCHARGED框架操作化为结构化验证清单问题，使用隐私保护型LLM在MIMIC-IV成人住院摘要中识别关键文档要素的存在、缺失或模糊。

Result: 研究证明了使用LLM实现可扩展自动化临床审计的可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究为电子病历文档的系统性质量改进提供了基础，未来可进一步扩展至更大规模数据集和多中心验证。

Abstract: Incomplete or inconsistent discharge documentation is a primary driver of care fragmentation and avoidable readmissions. Despite its critical role in patient safety, auditing discharge summaries relies heavily on manual review and is difficult to scale. We propose an automated framework for large-scale auditing of discharge summaries using locally deployed Large Language Models (LLMs). Our approach operationalizes core transition-of-care requirements such as follow-up instructions, medication history and changes, patient information and clinical course, etc. into a structured validation checklist of questions based on DISCHARGED framework. Using adult inpatient summaries from the MIMIC-IV database, we utilize a privacy-preserving LLM to identify the presence, absence, or ambiguity of key documentation elements. This work demonstrates the feasibility of scalable, automated clinical auditing and provides a foundation for systematic quality improvement in electronic health record documentation.

</details>


### [196] [Adaptive Serverless Resource Management via Slot-Survival Prediction and Event-Driven Lifecycle Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05465)
*Zeyu Wang,Cuiqianhe Du,Renyue Zhang,Kejian Tong,Qi He,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应工程框架，通过事件驱动架构和概率建模优化无服务器计算性能，采用双策略机制动态调整空闲持续时间并基于槽位生存预测进行智能请求等待管理，实现冷启动降低51.2%、成本效率提升近2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算虽消除了基础设施管理开销，但冷启动延迟和资源利用率问题突出。传统静态资源分配在可变工作负载下效率低下，导致性能下降或成本过高。

Method: 提出双策略机制：(1)动态调整空闲持续时间；(2)基于槽位生存预测的智能请求等待策略。采用滑动窗口聚合和异步处理技术，实现资源生命周期的主动管理。

Result: 多云环境下，冷启动降低51.2%，成本效率提升近2倍。

Conclusion: 该自适应框架能有效平衡性能与成本，为无服务器计算的资源优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Serverless computing eliminates infrastructure management overhead but introduces significant challenges regarding cold start latency and resource utilization. Traditional static resource allocation often leads to inefficiencies under variable workloads, resulting in performance degradation or excessive costs. This paper presents an adaptive engineering framework that optimizes serverless performance through event-driven architecture and probabilistic modeling. We propose a dual-strategy mechanism that dynamically adjusts idle durations and employs an intelligent request waiting strategy based on slot survival predictions. By leveraging sliding window aggregation and asynchronous processing, our system proactively manages resource lifecycles. Experimental results show that our approach reduces cold starts by up to 51.2% and improves cost-efficiency by nearly 2x compared to baseline methods in multi-cloud environments.

</details>


### [197] [OntoTKGE: Ontology-Enhanced Temporal Knowledge Graph Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05468)
*Dongying Lin,Yinan Liu,Shengwei tang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OntoTKGE框架，利用本体知识图谱（ontology-view KG）中的层级概念关系和概念-实体连接来指导时序知识图谱（TKG）外推任务的学习过程，从而增强实体嵌入表示，缓解稀疏历史交互实体预测困难的问题。该框架具有良好的通用性，可适配多种现有TKG外推模型，并在四个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序知识图谱外推模型面临关键挑战：如何处理历史交互稀疏的实体。这些稀疏实体由于缺乏足够的时序交互信息，难以学习有效的嵌入表示，从而导致预测性能下降。已有研究忽略了本体知识（ontological knowledge）的潜在价值——本体知识可以将具有相同概念的实体联系起来，使稀疏实体能够继承同概念实体的行为模式。

Method: 提出OntoTKGE编码器-解码器框架，核心思想是有效整合本体知识与时序知识。具体包括：（1）利用本体视图知识图谱建模抽象概念间的层级关系以及概念与实体间的连接关系；（2）通过知识引导机制将本体知识有效融合到TKG外推模型的学习过程中；（3）框架设计具有灵活性，可适配多种现有的TKG外推模型作为基础编码器。

Result: 在四个数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明：（1）OntoTKGE能显著提升多种TKG外推模型的性能；（2）与众多当前最优（SOTA）基线方法相比，OntoTKGE表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过引入本体知识并与时序知识有效整合，OntoTKGE成功解决了稀疏实体嵌入学习不充分的问题。该框架不仅具有较强的通用性和适应性，还能与现有模型优势互补，显著提升预测性能，为时序知识图谱外推任务提供了一种有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graph (TKG) extrapolation is an important task that aims to predict future facts through historical interaction information within KG snapshots. A key challenge for most existing TKG extrapolation models is handling entities with sparse historical interaction. The ontological knowledge is beneficial for alleviating this sparsity issue by enabling these entities to inherit behavioral patterns from other entities with the same concept, which is ignored by previous studies. In this paper, we propose a novel encoder-decoder framework OntoTKGE that leverages the ontological knowledge from the ontology-view KG (i.e., a KG modeling hierarchical relations among abstract concepts as well as the connections between concepts and entities) to guide the TKG extrapolation model's learning process through the effective integration of the ontological and temporal knowledge, thereby enhancing entity embeddings. OntoTKGE is flexible enough to adapt to many TKG extrapolation models. Extensive experiments on four data sets demonstrate that OntoTKGE not only significantly improves the performance of many TKG extrapolation models but also surpasses many SOTA baseline methods.

</details>


### [198] [Can We Trust a Black-box LLM? LLM Untrustworthy Boundary Detection via Bias-Diffusion and Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05483)
*Xiaotian Zhou,Di Tang,Xiaofeng Wang,Xiaozhong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GMRL-BD算法，利用强化学习和知识图谱识别大语言模型（LLM）在哪些主题领域可能产生偏见回答，确定模型的"不可信边界"。通过黑盒访问方式，仅需有限查询即可高效检测，并在多个主流LLM上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）在各领域展现强大问答能力，但有时产生偏见、意识形态化或错误的回答。用户难以判断模型在哪些主题上的回答值得信任，限制了LLM的安全可靠应用。目前缺乏有效方法帮助用户识别LLM的不可信边界。

Method: 提出GMRL-BD算法：（1）基于Wikipedia构建通用知识图谱（KG）；（2）采用多智能体强化学习框架；（3）仅需黑盒访问LLM，在特定查询约束下探索KG节点；（4）通过智能体协作高效识别易产生偏见回答的主题节点。

Result: 实验验证了算法的高效性，能够在仅向LLM发出有限查询的条件下准确识别不可信边界。研究还发布了包含Llama2、Vicuna、 Falcon、Qwen2、Gemma2和Yi-1.5等主流LLM的新数据集，并标注了各模型可能存在偏见的主题。

Conclusion: GMRL-BD算法为识别LLM不可信边界提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，对提升LLM应用的安全性和可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown a high capability in answering questions on a diverse range of topics. However, these models sometimes produce biased, ideologized or incorrect responses, limiting their applications if there is no clear understanding of which topics their answers can be trusted. In this research, we introduce a novel algorithm, named as GMRL-BD, designed to identify the untrustworthy boundaries (in terms of topics) of a given LLM, with black-box access to the LLM and under specific query constraints. Based on a general Knowledge Graph (KG) derived from Wikipedia, our algorithm incorporates with multiple reinforcement learning agents to efficiently identify topics (some nodes in KG) where the LLM is likely to generate biased answers. Our experiments demonstrated the efficiency of our algorithm, which can detect the untrustworthy boundary with just limited queries to the LLM. Additionally, we have released a new dataset containing popular LLMs including Llama2, Vicuna, Falcon, Qwen2, Gemma2 and Yi-1.5, along with labels indicating the topics on which each LLM is likely to be biased.

</details>


### [199] [Auditable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05485)
*Yi Nian,Aojie Yuan,Haiyue Zhang,Jiate Li,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper argues that LLM agent systems require auditability (not just prevention) to enable accountability. The authors define five auditability dimensions, three mechanism classes, and provide empirical evidence that basic security prerequisites are widely unmet, implementation is feasible, and partial recovery is possible. They propose an Auditability Card framework and identify six open research problems.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As LLM agent systems gain ability to act in the world through tool calls, database queries, task delegation, and external side effects, the critical question shifts from preventing harmful actions to ensuring post-deployment answerability and responsibility assignment.

Method: Define three core concepts (accountability, auditability, auditing) and five auditability dimensions (action recoverability, lifecycle coverage, policy checkability, responsibility attribution, evidence integrity); identify three mechanism classes (detect, enforce, recover) with temporal constraints; conduct multi-layered empirical evaluation including ecosystem security measurements (617 findings across 6 projects), runtime overhead analysis, and controlled recovery experiments.

Result: Security audit reveals widespread vulnerabilities (617 findings in 6 prominent open-source projects); pre-execution mediation with tamper-evident records adds only 8.3 ms median overhead; responsibility-relevant information can be partially recovered even without conventional logs; Auditability Card proposed as evaluation framework.

Conclusion: No agent system can be accountable without auditability; no single mechanism approach suffices for achieving full auditability; practical implementation is feasible with acceptable overhead (8.3 ms median); the paper proposes an Auditability Card framework and identifies six open research problems organized by mechanism class.

Abstract: LLM agents call tools, query databases, delegate tasks, and trigger external side effects. Once an agent system can act in the world, the question is no longer only whether harmful actions can be prevented--it is whether those actions remain answerable after deployment. We distinguish accountability (the ability to determine compliance and assign responsibility), auditability (the system property that makes accountability possible), and auditing (the process of reconstructing behavior from trustworthy evidence). Our claim is direct: no agent system can be accountable without auditability.
  To make this operational, we define five dimensions of agent auditability, i.e., action recoverability, lifecycle coverage, policy checkability, responsibility attribution, and evidence integrity, and identify three mechanism classes (detect, enforce, recover) whose temporal information-and-intervention constraints explain why, in practice, no single approach suffices. We support the position with layered evidence rather than a single benchmark: lower-bound ecosystem measurements suggest that even basic security prerequisites for auditability are widely unmet (617 security findings across six prominent open-source projects); runtime feasibility results show that pre-execution mediation with tamper-evident records adds only 8.3 ms median overhead; and controlled recovery experiments show that responsibility-relevant information can be partially recovered even when conventional logs are missing. We propose an Auditability Card for agent systems and identify six open research problems organized by mechanism class.

</details>


### [200] [Thinking Diffusion: Penalize and Guide Visual-Grounded Reasoning in Diffusion Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05497)
*Keuntae Kim,Mingyu Kang,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散多模态大语言模型(dMLLMs)在链式思维推理中的两个关键问题：答案过早生成和视觉依赖不足，提出PSP和VRG两种方法，在保持3倍加速的同时提升7.5%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散多模态大语言模型(dMLLMs)结合了LLM的推理能力与并行生成的高速推理优势，但在链式思维(CoT)推理中表现不佳，存在答案过早生成和视觉信息利用不足的问题，需要深入分析并提出改进方案。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) PSP (Position and Step Penalty)：在早期时间步对后续位置token施加惩罚，延缓过早答案生成；(2) VRG (Visual Reasoning Guidance)：借鉴无分类器引导思想，放大视觉定位信号。

Result: 在多种dMLLMs上的广泛实验表明，该方法相比使用4倍扩散步数的推理方式，准确率提升高达7.5%，同时实现超过3倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过PSP延迟过早生成并鼓励渐进式推理，结合VRG增强视觉对齐，有效解决了dMLLMs在CoT推理中的核心问题，证明了该方法在效率和效果上的显著优势。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) are emerging as promising alternatives to autoregressive (AR) LLMs. Recently, this paradigm has been extended to multimodal tasks, leading to the development of diffusion multimodal large language models (dMLLMs). These models are expected to retain the reasoning capabilities of LLMs while enabling faster inference through parallel generation. However, when combined with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning, dMLLMs exhibit two critical issues. First, we observe that dMLLMs often generate the final answer token at a very early timestep. This trend indicates that the model determines the answer before sufficient reasoning, leading to degraded reasoning performance. Second, during the initial timesteps, dMLLMs show minimal dependency on visual prompts, exhibiting a fundamentally different pattern of visual information utilization compared to AR vision-language models. In summary, these findings indicate that dMLLMs tend to generate premature final answers without sufficiently grounding on visual inputs. To address these limitations, we propose Position and Step Penalty (PSP) and Visual Reasoning Guidance (VRG). PSP penalizes tokens in later positions during early timesteps, delaying premature answer generation and encouraging progressive reasoning across timesteps. VRG, inspired by classifier-free guidance, amplifies visual grounding signals to enhance the model's alignment with visual evidence. Extensive experiments across various dMLLMs demonstrate that our method achieves up to 7.5% higher accuracy while delivering more than 3x speedup compared to reasoning with four times more diffusion steps.

</details>


### [201] [OmniDiagram: Advancing Unified Diagram Code Generation via Visual Interrogation Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05514)
*Haoyue Yang,Xuanle Zhao,Xuexin Liu,Feibang Jiang,Yao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniDiagram是一个统一的可编程图表生成框架，支持多种图表代码语言和任务定义，通过创新的视觉反馈策略Viva（Visual Interrogation Verifies All）解决强化学习中代码逻辑与视觉保真度对齐的问题，实现自进化训练，在196k+样本的M3²Diagram数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表生成研究任务定义单一、语言支持受限，难以应用于多样化图表类型；同时在强化学习训练中，代码逻辑与视觉保真度之间存在对齐挑战，传统方法依赖脆弱的语法规则或像素级匹配。

Method: 提出统一框架OmniDiagram整合多种图表代码语言和任务定义；引入创新视觉反馈策略Viva，通过生成式方法主动生成目标视觉查询来审视图表视觉保真度并提供细粒度反馈，实现无需人工标注ground truth代码的自进化训练过程。

Result: 在M3²Diagram数据集（196k+高质量样本）上进行实验，结合SFT与Viva-based RL，OmniDiagram在图表代码生成基准测试上达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: Viva机制通过生成式视觉询问有效解决了图表生成中的视觉保真度对齐问题，为可编程图表生成领域提供了可扩展的统一解决方案。

Abstract: The paradigm of programmable diagram generation is evolving rapidly, playing a crucial role in structured visualization. However, most existing studies are confined to a narrow range of task formulations and language support, constraining their applicability to diverse diagram types. In this work, we propose OmniDiagram, a unified framework that incorporates diverse diagram code languages and task definitions. To address the challenge of aligning code logic with visual fidelity in Reinforcement Learning (RL), we introduce a novel visual feedback strategy named Visual Interrogation Verifies All (\textsc{Viva}). Unlike brittle syntax-based rules or pixel-level matching, \textsc{Viva} rewards the visual structure of rendered diagrams through a generative approach. Specifically, \textsc{Viva} actively generates targeted visual inquiries to scrutinize diagram visual fidelity and provides fine-grained feedback for optimization. This mechanism facilitates a self-evolving training process, effectively obviating the need for manually annotated ground truth code. Furthermore, we construct M3$^2$Diagram, the first large-scale diagram code generation dataset, containing over 196k high-quality instances. Experimental results confirm that the combination of SFT and our \textsc{Viva}-based RL allows OmniDiagram to establish a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) across diagram code generation benchmarks.

</details>


### [202] [UniCreative: Unifying Long-form Logic and Short-form Sparkle via Reference-Free Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05517)
*Xiaolong Wei,Zerun Zhu,Simin Niu,Xingyu Zhang,Peiying Yu,Changxuan Xiao,Yuchen Li,Jicheng Yang,Zhejun Zhao,Chong Meng,Long Xia,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniCreative是一个统一的无参考强化学习框架，通过自适应约束感知奖励模型AC-GenRM和策略优化算法ACPO，解决创意写作中长文本全局连贯性与短文本局部表达性之间的张力问题，无需监督微调或真实标签即可实现人类偏好对齐，并展现出自主区分任务类型的元认知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创意写作面临核心挑战：长文本生成需要显式的宏观规划以保持全局连贯性，而短文本创作则需要自发、无约束的表达以保持局部表达力。现有对齐范式依赖静态奖励信号和高成本高质量监督数据，难以扩展。

Method: 1. 提出AC-GenRM：自适应约束感知奖励模型，动态合成查询特定标准，提供细粒度偏好判断
2. 提出ACPO：策略优化算法，在无监督微调和真实参考条件下对齐人类偏好
3. 采用无参考强化学习框架UniCreative

Result: AC-GenRM与专家评估高度一致；ACPO在多种写作任务中显著提升性能；模型展现出元认知能力——能自主区分需要严谨规划的任务和适合直接生成的任务。

Conclusion: 直接对齐方法有效，模型可自主学习区分不同写作任务的认知需求，为创意写作的对齐研究提供了可扩展的无参考解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in creative writing lies in reconciling the inherent tension between maintaining global coherence in long-form narratives and preserving local expressiveness in short-form texts. While long-context generation necessitates explicit macroscopic planning, short-form creativity often demands spontaneous, constraint-free expression. Existing alignment paradigms, however, typically employ static reward signals and rely heavily on high-quality supervised data, which is costly and difficult to scale. To address this, we propose \textbf{UniCreative}, a unified reference-free reinforcement learning framework. We first introduce \textbf{AC-GenRM}, an adaptive constraint-aware reward model that dynamically synthesizes query-specific criteria to provide fine-grained preference judgments. Leveraging these signals, we propose \textbf{ACPO}, a policy optimization algorithm that aligns models with human preferences across both content quality and structural paradigms without supervised fine-tuning and ground-truth references. Empirical results demonstrate that AC-GenRM aligns closely with expert evaluations, while ACPO significantly enhances performance across diverse writing tasks. Crucially, our analysis reveals an emergent meta-cognitive ability: the model learns to autonomously differentiate between tasks requiring rigorous planning and those favoring direct generation, validating the effectiveness of our direct alignment approach.

</details>


### [203] [Market-Bench: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Economic and Trade Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05523)
*Yushuo Zheng,Huiyu Duan,Zicheng Zhang,Yucheng Zhu,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Market-Bench 是一个将「定量决策」与「自由语言生成」耦合在同一闭环竞争市场中的 LLM 基准：在多智能体供应链经济中，LLM 作为零售商参与预算约束的第一价格多单位拍卖（采购）并定价、撰写营销标语（零售）；核心机制 Persona-Gated Attention（PGA）用标语与隐藏买家画像的余弦相似度决定市场可见性，从而让语言直接影响收益。对 20 个开源与闭源模型的评测显示显著分化与「赢者通吃」：仅少数模型（Gemini 2.5 Pro/Flash）能持续实现资本增值，多数在盈亏平衡线附近徘徊，且语义匹配分数相近，因此单靠语义能力无法预测经济成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 基准通常把「语义/社会角色扮演能力」（AgentSociety、MultiAgentBench）与「定量经济博弈理性」（囚徒困境、信任博弈、ABIDES-Economist）分开评测，缺少一个同时要求数学优化与语言理解、并在硬性稀缺约束（有限资金、破产）下闭环运行的多智能体市场测试床。真实零售中定价与采购离不开有说服力的营销，二者严格共生依赖；且智能体需从有限数值信号中推断隐藏买家偏好，错误会复利式放大直至破产而非仅仅降低一点准确率。由此提出核心问题：当代 LLM 能否在稀缺与竞争的市场中同时完成定量推理与语义适应。

Method: 构建可配置的多智能体供应链经济（POMSG，物理与金融双重约束），20 个 LLM 零售商代理 + 上游供应商 + 每步生成的买家。Stage A 采购：逐商品多单位第一价格拍卖（含保留价），刻意选择第一价格而非第二价格以迫使「出价压价（bid shading）」、显式竞争建模与利润-中标率权衡；硬预算约束，超预算报价得零分配。Stage B 零售：智能体输出各商品零售价与 ≤25 词营销标语；两阶段选择模型——先「注意力」（谁被看见）后「购买」（谁最便宜）：PGA 用 Qwen3-Embedding-8B 嵌入标语与隐藏画像，计算 w_{i,j}=exp(λ_j·Sim/τ)（τ=1.0，K_max=20），按权重采样考虑集 V_j（规模由耐心系数 ρ_j 与 K_max 决定），买家中只考虑 V_j 内有货且报价的最低价卖家，形成局部 Bertrand 价格竞争。观测：自身资金/库存、供应商报价、紧凑的公开市场历史（报价、标语、实际销量），人物画像不可见，输出严格 JSON。默认配置：8 类商品（基础价 50–2000，共 1000 件）、20 代理、6 步、每步 2 轮竞价、供需比 r=0.95、初始资金 22,500、买家 200、持有成本 0、LLM 温度 0、10 次独立重复。指标 9 项：NPM、累计利润 Π、RAR、IEI、缺货率、BidEff、OSI、FillRate、MMS；市场级指标 Gini/Theil/CV/HHI/CR4/Active Ratio。

Result: 1) 闭源领先：Gemini 2.5 Pro（Π=36,589，NPM=0.167）与 Flash（Π=26,104，NPM=0.190）利润最高；O3 服务水平最佳（FillRate=0.418、OSI=1.000）但利润较低，说明拍卖能力与零售变现能力可分离，存在「以利润换销量」的边际-批量权衡。2) 开源：Phi-4 仅 14B 却达 Π=7,565，与 GPT-4o（7,619）相当，优于 ERNIE 4.5 300B（1,360）；模型规模不是可靠预测因子，推理型训练更有效。3) 失败模式：Qwen3 30B-A3B 全程零利润、零 BidEfficiency，因格式/报价错误被完全排除出市场——这是硬约束经济基准特有的「全有全无」式失败。4) 相关性：利润与 BidEfficiency（Spearman ρ=0.68）、FillRate（0.88）强正相关，与 StockoutRate 强负相关（−0.88）；采购能力是一阶决定因素。5) 语义：MMS 波动窄且与利润弱相关（ρ=0.16），标语-画像相似度在 step 0→1 变化最大后趋于稳定，PCA 嵌入簇在 step 5 收敛，模型向「Ethical-Hype 轴」通用口号最优收敛，未形成部落专业化利基，表现为模仿而非买家分析。6) 市场结构：基尼 0.07→0.21、Theil 0.02→0.10、CV 0.13→0.45，CR4 0.23→0.33，但 HHI 约 0.08–0.10 仍属竞争区间，Active Ratio 降至约 0.4——不平等上升但未形成垄断的多赢家寡头格局，且排名流动性高。7) 存在「进入冲击」：步骤 0→1 调整幅度最大，随后策略空间压缩；采购呈现双峰分布，早期成功带来流动性形成自我强化。8) 需注意 IEI/OSI 等有界指标须与参与度（BidEff、FillRate、Active Ratio）联合解读，低活跃代理会因变异小而显得「稳定/高效」。

Conclusion: Market-Bench 提供了首个把硬稀缺的多智能体闭环经济与直接影响收益的自由形式语言统一起来的可复现基准，通过 PGA 把「语言的经济价值」数学化，产出完整轨迹可作为战略行为数据集与生成式数据集。主要发现是「赢者通吃」与双峰分布：模型在孤立任务上表现优异，但库存管理与市场定位的合成仍是独立的困难前沿；语言适应虽快却收敛到通用模仿均衡，无法维持语义差异化，作者将其视为待攻克的开放挑战。局限：仅单一市场配置（20 代理/6 步/8 商品）、买家画像为固定权重的合成分布且无重复购买与学习、持有成本为 0。未来方向：动态随机供给、跨市场套利、更长回合、非英语市场。

Abstract: The ability of large language models (LLMs) to manage and acquire economic resources remains unclear. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Market-Bench}, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates the capabilities of LLMs in economically-relevant tasks through economic and trade competition. Specifically, we construct a configurable multi-agent supply chain economic model where LLMs act as retailer agents responsible for procuring and retailing merchandise. In the \textbf{procurement} stage, LLMs bid for limited inventory in budget-constrained auctions. In the \textbf{retail} stage, LLMs set retail prices, generate marketing slogans, and provide them to buyers through a role-based attention mechanism for purchase. Market-Bench logs complete trajectories of bids, prices, slogans, sales, and balance-sheet states, enabling automatic evaluation with economic, operational, and semantic metrics. Benchmarking on 20 open- and closed-source LLM agents reveals significant performance disparities and winner-take-most phenomenon, \textit{i.e.}, only a small subset of LLM retailers can consistently achieve capital appreciation, while many hover around the break-even point despite similar semantic matching scores. Market-Bench provides a reproducible testbed for studying how LLMs interact in competitive markets.

</details>


### [204] [ActivityEditor: Learning to Synthesize Physically Valid Human Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05529)
*Chenjie Yang,Yutian Jiang,Anqi Liang,Wei Qi,Chenyu Wu,Junbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ActivityEditor，一个基于双LLM智能体的大规模轨迹生成框架，用于解决数据稀缺地区的人类移动性建模问题。该框架通过意图生成智能体和轨迹编辑智能体的协作，实现跨区域零样本轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的人类移动性建模方法存在数据稀缺问题，导致在缺乏历史轨迹数据的地区难以应用。现有方法在跨区域迁移时泛化能力不足，需要一种能够在零样本场景下保持高性能的解决方案。

Method: ActivityEditor采用双智能体协作框架：(1)意图生成智能体利用人口统计先验生成结构化人类意图和粗粒度活动链，确保高层社会语义一致性；(2)编辑智能体通过迭代修订强化人类移动定律约束，生成最终轨迹。该能力通过基于真实世界物理约束的多奖励强化学习获得，使智能体内化移动规律。

Result: 实验表明ActivityEditor在跨城市零样本迁移时表现优异，保持较高的统计保真度和物理有效性。

Conclusion: ActivityEditor为数据稀缺场景下的移动性模拟提供了一个稳健且高度可泛化的解决方案，展示了基于LLM智能体的框架在复杂时空建模任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Human mobility modeling is indispensable for diverse urban applications. However, existing data-driven methods often suffer from data scarcity, limiting their applicability in regions where historical trajectories are unavailable or restricted. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{ActivityEditor}, a novel dual-LLM-agent framework designed for zero-shot cross-regional trajectory generation. Our framework decomposes the complex synthesis task into two collaborative stages. Specifically, an intention-based agent, which leverages demographic-driven priors to generate structured human intentions and coarse activity chains to ensure high-level socio-semantic coherence. These outputs are then refined by editor agent to obtain mobility trajectories through iteratively revisions that enforces human mobility law. This capability is acquired through reinforcement learning with multiple rewards grounded in real-world physical constraints, allowing the agent to internalize mobility regularities and ensure high-fidelity trajectory generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{ActivityEditor} achieves superior zero-shot performance when transferred across diverse urban contexts. It maintains high statistical fidelity and physical validity, providing a robust and highly generalizable solution for mobility simulation in data-scarce scenarios. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ActivityEditor-066B.

</details>


### [205] [Inventory of the 12 007 Low-Dimensional Pseudo-Boolean Landscapes Invariant to Rank, Translation, and Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05530)
*Arnaud Liefooghe,Sébastien Verel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文引入了比传统的rank-invariant更强的"rank landscape invariance"概念，将问题按排序、邻域结构和对称性（平移和旋转）等价分类，并对维度1、2、3的伪布尔函数进行了穷举性分类，共识别出12,007个不变景观类，比单纯rank-invariance大幅减少。非单射函数产生的不变景观类远多于单射函数，且涌现出复杂的拓扑景观特性与算法行为组合（如欺骗性、中立性、爬山策略表现）。该分类体系可用于教学、基准测试设计以及理解景观难度与算法性能关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
