Conclusion: JUÁ提供了一个实用的评估框架，支持在统一基准设计下研究多个巴西法律领域的法律检索问题。

Abstract: Legal information retrieval in Portuguese remains difficult to evaluate systematically because available datasets differ widely in document type, query style, and relevance definition. We present \textsc{JUÁ}, a public benchmark for Brazilian legal retrieval designed to support more reproducible and comparable evaluation across heterogeneous legal collections. More broadly, \textsc{JUÁ} is intended not only as a benchmark, but as a continuous evaluation infrastructure for Brazilian legal IR, combining shared protocols, common ranking metrics, fixed splits when applicable, and a public leaderboard. The benchmark covers jurisprudence retrieval as well as broader legislative, regulatory, and question-driven legal search. We evaluate lexical, dense, and BM25-based reranking pipelines, including a domain-adapted Qwen embedding model fine-tuned on \textsc{JUÁ}-aligned supervision. Results show that the benchmark is sufficiently heterogeneous to distinguish retrieval paradigms and reveal substantial cross-dataset trade-offs. Domain adaptation yields its clearest gains on the supervision-aligned \textsc{JUÁ-Juris} subset, while BM25 remains highly competitive on other collections, especially in settings with strong lexical and institutional phrasing cues. Overall, \textsc{JUÁ} provides a practical evaluation framework for studying legal retrieval across multiple Brazilian legal domains under a common benchmark design.

</details>


### [262] [Data, Not Model: Explaining Bias toward LLM Texts in Neural Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06163)
*Wei Huang,Keping Bi,Yinqiong Cai,Wei Chen,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文揭示神经检索器对LLM生成文本的偏好并非模型固有缺陷，而是源于训练数据中的标注偏置。通过理论分析和实验验证，提出两种缓解策略显著降低源偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究普遍认为神经检索器存在"源偏置"——偏好LLM生成的段落而非人类撰写的段落，这种现象被视为检索模型的内生缺陷，引发了对信息访问系统公平性和可靠性的担忧。

Method: 1) 分析训练数据中正负文档的非语义差异特征（流畅性、术语特异性）；2) 在嵌入空间中验证偏置方向的对齐关系；3) 从理论上证明对比学习不可避免地吸收训练数据中的伪影不平衡；4) 提出两种缓解方法：减少训练数据中的伪影差异，以及通过投影去除调整LLM文本向量。

Result: 发现正负文档间的非语义差异与LLM和人类文本间的差异高度相似；在嵌入空间中，从负样本到正样本的偏置方向与从人类文本到LLM文本的方向对齐；两种缓解方法均能显著降低源偏置。

Conclusion: 神经检索器的源偏置并非模型固有缺陷，而是训练数据监督信号的产物。通过数据层面的伪影控制或向量空间的偏置移除，可以有效缓解该问题，从而减轻对LLM生成文本在信息访问系统中应用的担忧。

Abstract: Recent studies show that neural retrievers often display source bias, favoring passages generated by LLMs over human-written ones, even when both are semantically similar. This bias has been considered an inherent flaw of retrievers, raising concerns about the fairness and reliability of modern information access systems. Our work challenges this view by showing that source bias stems from supervision in retrieval datasets rather than the models themselves. We found that non-semantic differences, like fluency and term specificity, exist between positive and negative documents, mirroring differences between LLM and human texts. In the embedding space, the bias direction from negatives to positives aligns with the direction from human-written to LLM-generated texts. We theoretically show that retrievers inevitably absorb the artifact imbalances in the training data during contrastive learning, which leads to their preferences over LLM texts. To mitigate the effect, we propose two approaches: 1) reducing artifact differences in training data and 2) adjusting LLM text vectors by removing their projection on the bias vector. Both methods substantially reduce source bias. We hope our study alleviates some concerns regarding LLM-generated texts in information access systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Towards Predicting Multi-Vulnerability Attack Chains in Software Supply Chains from Software Bill of Materials Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04977)
*Laura Baird,Armin Moin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文（FSE '26 Companion）提出一个新研究方向：把漏洞增强的 CycloneDX SBOM 表示为异构图（Component/CVE/CWE 节点，DEPENDS_ON/HAS_VULNERABILITY/HAS_CWE 边），用异构图注意力网络 HGAT 做组件级"是否含漏洞"的可行性检查，并用轻量 MLP 将"多漏洞级联攻击链发现"建模为 CVE 对链接预测；在 Wild SBOMs 的 200 个真实 SBOM 上 HGAT 达 91.03% 准确率 / 74.02% F1，MLP 在 35 条已记录攻击链种子上达 0.93 ROC-AUC；两者尚未端到端打通，多步链组装仍是未来工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链攻击常由多个组件中漏洞的级联交互引发（典型如 ProxyLogon 将 4 个 Exchange CVE 串联为预认证入侵路径），而现有 SBOM 安全流水线与工具（Snyk、Trivy 等）把扫描结果当作彼此独立的 per-CVE 记录，只做 CVSS/EPSS/VEX 单条评分，无法暴露跨组件攻击链；孤立看似低危的漏洞经依赖结构组合后可能升为关键。同时 SBOM 生成与解析工具差异大、结果噪声高。因此需要从"给每个 CVE 打分"转向"在依赖图约束下学习漏洞交互模式"，即把 SBOM 结构与扫描输出视为需在供应链依赖上下文中解释的证据（evidence graph），而非孤立记录。

Method: 整体管线四步：(1) 用 Syft 生成 CycloneDX SBOM、Grype（基于 OSV）富化为含漏洞的 SBOM，构建异构图/KG：Component、CVE、CWE 三类节点，DEPENDS_ON（组件间依赖）、HAS_VULNERABILITY（组件→CVE）、HAS_CWE（CVE→CWE，原型中尚未实现）三类边；节点特征为轻量可解释特征（组件：CVSS 聚合、severity、直接/传递依赖、度统计、许可证；CVE：CVSS 分箱、年份等时序元数据；CWE：映射 CVE 归一化计数）。(2) 训练 2 层、2 头注意力、隐藏维 64、dropout 0.2、Adam lr=1e-3、weight decay 5e-4、交叉熵、最多 30 epoch、batch 2 graphs 的 HGAT，对组件做 has-any-CVE 二分类，作为"依赖结构是否携带超越局部元数据信号"的可行性检查。(3) 级联发现建模为 CVE 对链接预测：22 维特征（9 个来自 NVD 的 per-CVE 特征如 CVSS、severity one-hot、年份、exploited 标志、引用数、CWE 数 + 4 个交互特征如 CVSS 差、CVSS 积、年份间隔、双 exploited 指示），MLP 结构 22-64-32-16-1，dropout 0.3，Adam lr=1e-3，二元交叉熵，最多 50 epoch，patience 10 早停；训练数据为 35 条已记录链（27 条来自漏洞披露、8 条来自事件报告，链长 2–4，中位数 2、均值 2.51）内的 CVE 对为正例，按 2:1 采负例，输出协同利用概率用于排序候选链。(4) 分析者映射协议：把链中 CVE 与 SBOM 的 HAS_VULNERABILITY 边求交，定位承载 CVE 的组件，在 DEPENDS_ON 图上投影并提取诱导子图作为 SBOM 局部化上下文。

Result: RQ1：HGAT 在 200 个随机抽取的 Python CycloneDX SBOM（Wild SBOMs，70/15/15 划分）上 Accuracy 0.9103、Precision 0.8084、Recall 0.6826、F1 0.7402；推理期屏蔽 DEPENDS_ON 边的消融使 Recall 崩塌至 0.0000（Accuracy 0.8128 主要源于类别不平衡下预测多数类），表明模型确实依赖关系结构而非仅节点局部信号。RQ2：MLP 级联预测器在 35 条链的种子上 ROC-AUC 达到 0.93，能区分协同利用 CVE 对与随机对的元数据模式。作者自陈局限：ROC-AUC 在类别不平衡下可能具误导性；尚未证明能预测全新、未记录的链；pair 级划分导致同一 CVE 可能同时出现在训练与测试集（泄漏），会高估泛化能力。

Conclusion: 论文提出并初步验证了"SBOM 驱动的图学习 + CVE 对链接预测"这一新研究方向：初步结果表明软件依赖结构本身携带可学习信号（HGAT 证实），且即便链文档稀缺也可训练出具备较好判别力的协同利用先验（MLP）。核心局限是两个模型作用于不相交的 CVE 群体、尚未端到端连接——MLP 目前只能做对级协同利用打分，多步攻击链的组装（把高分对拼接成端到端链）仍是关键未来工作。后续计划包括：扩充链语料、引入链级与时序划分以更严谨评估泛化、对负采样比例做敏感性分析、扩展到 SPDX 格式与非 Python 生态、重扫启用 CWE 抽取以研究链内 CWE 共现模式并提升可解释性、以及评估基于 LLM 的基线（如在 SBOM/KG 上下文上的 ReAct）作为互补方法。代码与数据已开源于 GitHub 与 DataVerse。

Abstract: Software supply chain security compromises often stem from cascaded interactions of vulnerabilities, for example, between multiple vulnerable components. Yet, Software Bill of Materials (SBOM)-based pipelines for security analysis typically treat scanner findings as independent per-CVE (Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures) records. We propose a new research direction based on learning multi-vulnerability attack chains through a novel SBOM-driven graph-learning approach. This treats SBOM structure and scanner outputs as a dependency-constrained evidence graph rather than a flat list of vulnerabilities. We represent vulnerability-enriched CycloneDX SBOMs as heterogeneous graphs whose nodes capture software components and known vulnerabilities (i.e, CVEs), connected by typed relations, such as dependency and vulnerability links. We train a Heterogeneous Graph Attention Network (HGAT) to predict whether a component is associated with at least one known vulnerability as a feasibility check for learning over this structure. Additionally, we frame the discovery of cascading vulnerabilities as CVE-pair link prediction using a lightweight Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) neural network trained on documented multi-vulnerability chains. Validated on 200 real-world SBOMs from the Wild SBOMs public dataset, the HGAT component classifier achieves 91.03% Accuracy and 74.02% F1-score, while the cascade predictor model (MLP) achieves a Receiver Operating Characteristic - Area Under Curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.93 on a seed set of 35 documented attack chains.

</details>


### [264] [Measuring the Permission Gate: A Stress-Test Evaluation of Claude Code's Auto Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04978)
*Zimo Ji,Zongjie Li,Wenyuan Jiang,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次独立评估Claude Code自动模式的安全分类器，发现其在模糊授权场景下漏检率达81%，远高于生产环境的17%；主要原因是36.8%的危险操作通过项目文件编辑（非shell命令）实现，绕过了分类器的监控范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体的权限控制系统缺乏独立验证；Anthropic报告的17%假阴性率仅基于生产流量，未涵盖故意模糊的授权场景；需要评估系统在边界情况下的真实表现。

Method: 使用AmPermBench基准（128个提示词，覆盖4个DevOps任务家族和3个受控模糊轴），对253个状态变更操作进行逐动作评估，对抗oracle真值标签。

Result: 端到端假阴性率达81.0%（95% CI: 73.8%-87.4%）；36.8%操作通过Tier 2（项目内文件编辑）绕过分类器；仅限Tier 3时FNR为70.3%，FPR升至31.9%；工件清理任务的Tier 2覆盖缺口最严重（FNR 92.9%）。

Conclusion: 自动模式假设危险操作必须经过shell执行，但智能体常通过文件编辑实现等效效果；分类器存在覆盖边界漏洞，需扩展对文件编辑路径的监控能力。

Abstract: Claude Code's auto mode is the first deployed permission system for AI coding agents, using a two-stage transcript classifier to gate dangerous tool calls. Anthropic reports a 0.4% false positive rate and 17% false negative rate on production traffic. We present the first independent evaluation of this system on deliberately ambiguous authorization scenarios, i.e., tasks where the user's intent is clear but the target scope, blast radius, or risk level is underspecified. Using AmPermBench, a 128-prompt benchmark spanning four DevOps task families and three controlled ambiguity axes, we evaluate 253 state-changing actions at the individual action level against oracle ground truth.
  Our findings characterize auto mode's scope-escalation coverage under this stress-test workload. The end-to-end false negative rate is 81.0% (95% CI: 73.8%-87.4%), substantially higher than the 17% reported on production traffic, reflecting a fundamentally different workload rather than a contradiction. Notably, 36.8% of all state-changing actions fall outside the classifier's scope via Tier 2 (in-project file edits), contributing to the elevated end-to-end FNR. Even restricting to the 160 actions the classifier actually evaluates (Tier 3), the FNR remains 70.3%, while the FPR rises to 31.9%. The Tier 2 coverage gap is most pronounced on artifact cleanup (92.9% FNR), where agents naturally fall back to editing state files when the expected CLI is unavailable. These results highlight a coverage boundary worth examining: auto mode assumes dangerous actions transit the shell, but agents routinely achieve equivalent effects through file edits that the classifier does not evaluate.

</details>


### [265] [Squeez: Task-Conditioned Tool-Output Pruning for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04979)
*Ádám Kovács*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究代码智能体中工具输出裁剪问题，提出在给定任务查询和工具输出时，提取最小必要证据块的方法。通过在SWE-bench数据集上构建11,477样本的基准测试，微调Qwen 3.5 2B模型，在移除92%输入标记的同时达到0.86召回率和0.80 F1分数，超越更大规模零样本模型和启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体在执行任务时需要反复处理冗长的工具输出，但其中仅有少量信息对下一步决策有用。现有方法要么保留全部内容导致效率低下，要么简单截断可能丢失关键信息，缺乏针对任务相关的智能裁剪机制。

Method: 构建包含11,477示例的基准数据集（源自SWE-bench仓库交互和合成多生态系统工具输出），手动标注618个测试样本；使用LoRA微调Qwen 3.5 2B模型，训练任务条件下的工具输出裁剪能力。

Result: 微调模型在移除92%输入标记的条件下达到0.86召回率和0.80 F1分数；相比零样本Qwen 3.5 35B A3B模型召回率提升11个百分点，显著优于所有启发式裁剪基线方法。

Conclusion: 小规模微调模型可通过任务条件感知有效裁剪工具输出，在保持高召回的同时大幅降低输入长度，为提升代码智能体效率提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Coding agents repeatedly consume long tool observations even though only a small fraction of each observation matters for the next step. We study task-conditioned tool-output pruning: given a focused query and one tool output, return the smallest verbatim evidence block the agent should inspect next. We introduce a benchmark of 11,477 examples built from SWE-bench repository interactions and synthetic multi-ecosystem tool outputs, with a manually curated 618-example test set. We fine-tune Qwen 3.5 2B with LoRA and compare it against larger zero-shot models and heuristic pruning baselines. Our model reaches 0.86 recall and 0.80 F1 while removing 92% of input tokens, outperforming zero-shot Qwen 3.5 35B A3B by 11 recall points and all heuristic baselines by a wide margin.

</details>


### [266] [Architecture Without Architects: How AI Coding Agents Shape Software Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04990)
*Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Tim Lukas Adam,Riccardo Terrenzi,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这篇立场论文指出：AI 编码代理（Claude Code、Cursor、Devin 等）在几秒内替开发者做出框架、数据库、认证、部署等架构决策，却几乎不被当作架构决策来评审。作者归纳出代理隐式做架构决策的五个机制，以及"提示词特征→必需基础设施"的六种 prompt-architecture coupling 模式（分为随模型能力提升而弱化的 contingent 类与逻辑上永久存在的 fundamental 类），并用一个客服机器人案例演示：仅改变提示词措辞（FAQ / 结构化 JSON / 工具调用），同一任务生成的系统在 LoC（141→827）、文件数（2→6）与组件结构上差异巨大。作者将此现象命名为 "vibe architecting"（氛围式架构），并提出三层治理框架（约束、一致性校验、知识回灌）、影响声明式评审、ADR 记录与生成前架构影响预览等实践与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码代理（vibe coding 工具）正在以秒级速度替开发者完成脚手架生成、框架选型、数据库与认证配置、集成协议绑定等原本需要团队权衡数日并留存 ADR 的架构决策。作者观察到三类现有工作均未覆盖此缺口：prompt pattern 目录与 promptware 生命周期研究（White et al.、Chen et al.）未考察提示词如何决定系统架构；LangChain/LlamaIndex 的架构抽象藏在库代码中而非可推理的设计知识；Sculley et al. 的隐藏技术债发生在训练/部署阶段，而本文关注的债在代码生成阶段、系统尚未运行时即已累积；Sarkar、Drosos、Fawzy、Falcão 等聚焦开发者交互与采纳，未审视架构后果。因此需要一个把"提示词即架构规格"这一耦合显式化的视角与治理手段。

Method: 采用"工具调查 + 说明性案例研究"的立场论文方法。（1）工具调研：考察 Claude Code、Cursor、Devin、Bolt.new、Codex、Windsurf、Kiro、Replit Agent 等工具的决策面演变（行级补全 2022 → 多文件 2024 → 系统级代理 2025 → 子代理协同分解），据此识别出五个机制：模型选择、任务分解、默认配置、脚手架与自主生成、集成协议（MCP/硬编码服务）。（2）案例实验：以同一客服聊天机器人任务构建 A/B/C 三个变体，均用 Claude Code 默认设置独立生成、运行时统一为 GPT-4o-mini，使提示词成为唯一变量；记录框架、存储、组件、集成方式、LoC 与文件数差异（表 I、图 1）。（3）耦合模式归纳：追踪 LangChain、LlamaIndex 及厂商文档中的抽象，把每个 prompt 层特征映射到其必需的基础设施，归纳出六种模式（表 II、图 2），并按 contingent/fundamental 分类，讨论其组合的超线性效应。（4）提出三层框架（图 3）与研究议程、效度威胁分析。

Result: 案例结果显示，同一任务仅因提示词措辞不同即产生结构迥异的系统：变体 A（"从 FAQ 回答产品问题"）为 Express + JSON，2 文件 141 LoC，FAQ 加载器与聊天处理器；变体 B（"返回含 intent/confidence/entities 的结构化 JSON 并做 schema 校验"）增至 4 文件 472 LoC，新增 Zod schema、指数退避重试、fallback 生成器；变体 C（"可调用 search_kb()/get_account()/escalate_to_human()"）达 6 文件 827 LoC，新增工具注册表、10 轮迭代的 agent loop、SQLite 状态存储——代码量 5.9×、文件数 3×。三者组件结构、依赖图与失败模式均不同，说明"能力声明实质是接口定义、输出 schema 实质是数据契约"。六模式表明确：结构化输出（contingent）、动态 few-shot（contingent）、函数调用（fundamental）、ReAct 推理（fundamental）、RAG（contingent）、上下文压缩（contingent），并指出工具数量增加会扩大攻击面、间接提示注入风险随 MCP 标准化而放大。作者同时报告代理脚手架正向 React/TypeScript/Tailwind 收敛，简化知识迁移但集中漏洞暴露。

Conclusion: 作者主张：提示词规格应被视为架构制品并纳入架构评审；应记录"影响声明"（如"结构化 JSON 输出新增校验器、重试、fallback，+330 LoC 与两种新失败模式"）并写入 ADR；可设定复杂度阈值/基线以在脚手架失控前触发评审；需要生成前主动提示架构后果的治理工具，以及从代理推理轨迹自动抽取 ADR 的能力。三层框架将工具机制映射到 SA 概念：约束层（指令文件、MCP 配置 ↔ ADL/ADD）、一致性层（plan-build、生成后 hook ↔ fitness function）、知识层（repo map、上下文文件 ↔ ADR/AKM）。结论指出 vibe coding 在提示词决定系统结构的那一刻即成为 vibe architecting，这些提示词级决策应享有与设计级决策同等的治理，而相应的评审实践、决策记录与工具尚不存在；本文定位为研究纲领而非单点贡献，后续方向包括跨代理复现、架构足迹度量、主动治理工具、ADR 自动生成与模式组合分析（横切关注点是加性还是超线性增长仍待验证）。

Abstract: AI coding agents select frameworks, scaffold infrastructure, and wire integrations, often in seconds. These are architectural decisions, yet almost no one reviews them as such. We identify five mechanisms by which agents make implicit architectural choices and propose six prompt-architecture coupling patterns that map natural-language prompt features to the infrastructure they require. The patterns range from contingent couplings (structured output validation) that may weaken as models improve to fundamental ones (tool-call orchestration) that persist regardless of model capability. An illustrative demonstration confirms that prompt wording alone produces structurally different systems for the same task. We term the phenomenon vibe architecting, architecture shaped by prompts rather than deliberate design, and outline review practices, decision records, and tooling to bring these hidden decisions under governance.

</details>


### [267] [Nidus: Externalized Reasoning for AI-Assisted Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05080)
*Danil Gorinevski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Nidus是一个治理运行时系统，通过机械化的V模型实现AI辅助软件交付。在自托管部署中，三个LLM家族（Claude、Gemini、Codex）在每次提交时验证证明义务的当前义务集下交付了一个100,000行系统，系统自行管理自身构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程不变量（追踪的需求、有据可查的架构、有证据的交付物）无法作为学习行为可靠维护；保证需要由提案者外部的机制强制执行。组织标准需编译成可导入的约束库，由可判定评估强制执行。

Method: 将工程方法论外部化为可判定工件，在每次变更持久化前验证。组织标准编译为指南手册——约束库由治理项目导入并由可判定评估强制执行。

Result: 三个LLM家族在自托管部署中交付了100,000行系统，每个义务永久消除一类未工程化输出。开发历史形成形式化开发——每个状态满足所有活动义务，义务集单调增长。

Conclusion: 四项贡献：(1)递归自我治理——约束表面约束对自身的变更；(2)信息素协调——通过表面摩擦引导智能体，无中央控制；(3)近端规范强化——活制品外部化工程上下文，UNSAT判决在推理时塑造行为，无需权重更新；(4)治理剧场预防——合规证据无法在建模的变更路径内伪造。

Abstract: We present Nidus, a governance runtime that mechanizes the V-model for AI-assisted software delivery. In the self-hosting deployment, three LLM families (Claude, Gemini, Codex) delivered a 100,000-line system under proof obligations verified against the current obligation set on every commit. The system governed its own construction.
  Engineering invariants - traced requirements, justified architecture, evidenced deliveries - cannot be reliably maintained as learned behavior; assurance requires enforcement by a mechanism external to the proposer. Nidus externalizes the engineering methodology into a decidable artifact verified on every mutation before persistence. Organizational standards compile into guidebooks - constraint libraries imported by governed projects and enforced by decidable evaluation.
  Four contributions: (1) recursive self-governance - the constraint surface constrains mutations to itself; (2) stigmergic coordination - friction from the surface routes agents without central control; (3) proximal spec reinforcement - the living artifact externalizes the engineering context that RL and long-chain reasoning try to internalize; the specification is the reward function, UNSAT verdicts shape behavior at inference time, no weight updates; (4) governance theater prevention - compliance evidence cannot be fabricated within the modeled mutation path. The constraint surface compounds: each obligation permanently eliminates a class of unengineered output. The artifact's development history is a formal development - every state satisfies all active obligations, and the obligation set grows monotonically.

</details>


### [268] [Closed-Loop Autonomous Software Development via Jira-Integrated Backlog Orchestration: A Case Study in Deterministic Control and Safety-Constrained Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05000)
*Elias Calboreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并实证部署了一套“闭环自主软件开发”系统：把软件生命周期管理（而非代码生成）建模为确定性控制回路，用 7 条按计划运行的自动化泳道实现 7 阶段流水线，通过 Jira Status Contract 提供外部可观测的碰撞锁与降级模式协议，将 AI 限定为受策略栅栏约束的监督层。初始 152 次运行窗口达成 100% 终态成功（95% Clopper-Pearson CI [97.6%, 100%]），此后累计 795+ 运行产物；三轮对抗性代码评审 51 条发现全部闭环且注入集内零漏报；10 条自主安全工单按 6:2:2 分布（自主/人工/策略裁决）。结论是：把自治嵌入显式控制、恢复与审计机制中的“有界、可追溯”生命周期自动化在工程上是可行的，但论文仅为单案例的部分验证安全论证，而非形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业关注的 AI 辅助开发多集中于 Copilot、Tabnine、Codex 一类代码生成工具，而软件生命周期还包含待办管理、需求整合、合规、问题跟踪、变更编排与验证等环节，这些环节依赖多异构规划源（设计文档、监管要求、Issue Tracker、代码评审意见、安全扫描），人工综合既费力又易错，且缺乏可验证的证据链把决策点与结果关联起来。作者因此把问题重构为控制回路问题：给定异构输入，产生确定性的动作序列，在推进工作同时维持系统状态完整性。具体痛点有四：(1) 待办碎片化与不一致（基线 820 To Do / 223 In Progress / 689 Done，多 Jira 项目间权威源不唯一、存在重复项）；(2) 合规与审计风险（含 29 项 AUTO-CERT、135 项 CERTREQ 等认证相关条目，手工流程无可验证证据链）；(3) 13 份结构化源文档分散于 5 类规划界面，缺少解析、去重与置信度打分；(4) 缺少“摄入→规范化→执行→验证→发布”的闭环编排机制。三个研究问题分别关注：确定性七阶段控制回路能在多大程度上削减例行待办分流中的人工判断；自主安全工单族中安全自治的实际边界及其架构化强制方式；以及在无形式化证明下取得可量化辩护的可靠性主张所需的安全机制。

Method: 架构上采用双表示模型：本地规范化待办库（版本化仓库，意图权威记录）+ 远端 Jira（共享状态面），共 7 个任务族约 1,602 行（KAN 1,024、AUTO-DROP 442、EMU 77、AUTO-CERT 29、AUTO-SEC/AUTO-OPS/AUTO-TECH 各 10）。7 条泳道按不同节拍持续运行：Lane1 待办同步摄入（每小时）、Lane2 代码库审计（2 小时，只读）、Lane3 待办梳理（4 小时，去重+置信度映射）、Lane4 AI 代码修复（每小时，隔离 worktree）、Lane5 运维智能/健康看门狗（2 小时）、Lane6 质量闸门（2 小时）、Lane7 规范完备性审计（4 小时）。形式化层面用有限状态机（S0–S6 + Failure/Stalled）建模阶段转换，另以 Jira Status Contract（To Do/In Progress/On Hold/Done，Lane4 原子地 To Do→In Progress 作为分布式互斥锁，Lane6 失败回退只回 To Do）治理单工单生命周期；安全层做执行—验证分离（FAIL_VERIFY_MUTATED_STATE 作为“执行者不得自审”的机器级绊线）、检查点式时间预算（标准 45 分钟/夜间深扫 120 分钟、10 分钟进度检查点、USR1 警告后 30 秒 SIGKILL）、12 个集中式锁（4 个核心函数 + 8 种模式，7 个分泳道锁文件、TTL 2–4 小时）、101 个异常处理器、约 120 条映射 fix plan 作为 MANDATE 任务分类学、失败恢复级联（worktree 丢弃重试→回执重放→指数退避+±10% 抖动→三次失败后熔断→崩溃处理器转 Stalled→Lane5 队列排空，每轮最多 20 次 MCP 调用）。规范化用四级匹配（精确标签→键→加权 SequenceMatcher+Jaccard 相似度 s=0.6·SM+0.4·Jac→Levenshtein 模糊），并以 TF-IDF 余弦相似度作为动机；置信度三层阈值 ≥0.83 自主、0.50–0.83 人工复核、<0.50 停止并重摄入（LATTICE 架构实例化）。AI 层被限定为监督层（部署期从 GPT-5.4 切换至 Claude，经 MCP 调用 6 个 Jira 工具），受上下文包（Authority/Source/Conductor）、版本化 PROMPT.md（7 泳道 6,907 行）、资源上限、diff 分级审查（<50 行标准、50–200 行扩展、>200 行强制人工）、输出再校验与保守默认原则约束。降级模式以 jira_connectivity.json 为开关，三个分区回退存储（jira_write_outbox.json、mcp_replay_needed.json、jira_fallback_queue.md）与本地锁 .jira_ticket_locks.json 维持离线连续性，Lane5 充当恢复协调者。证据链遵循 TRACE 规范（哈希链接、运行/泳道 ID、时间戳、输入输出校验和、异常计数、终态标识），Jira 每次状态转换记录 run ID、UTC 时间戳与证据日志超链接，对齐 NIST SP 800-53 AU-12/AU-3。评估手段：152 次初始运行窗口的终态成功率与 Clopper-Pearson 精确区间；三轮基于 STRIDE 威胁模型、由两名未参与构建者组成的评审组的对抗性注入（51 条发现）；AUTO-SEC 10 条工单结果分布；Jira 集成与去重指标（38 份回执/30 个 issue，后续 120 条转换、零重复发布）；并以 IEC 60812:2018 四级严重度做 19 模式 FMEA（双人评审打分）。

Result: 初始 152 次运行（夜间全扫 82、执行器 29、修复 41）全部达成终态成功 100%，95% CI [97.6%, 100%]（夜间 [95.6%,100%]、执行器 [88.1%,100%]、修复 [91.4%,100%]）；CLEAN 75 次（49.3%）、DEGRADED 77 次（50.7%），其中夜间 DEGRADED 占 79.3% 但 65 次全部成功——DEGRADED 仅表示“存在信息性告警”而非失败。转入按泳道连续运行后截至 2026-03-31 累计 795+ 运行产物，报告件由 313 增至 795+，告警由 99 条按七泳道重构。Jira 侧：评估窗口 38 份回执覆盖 30 个唯一 issue，后续 120 条工单转换，全期零重复发布（三层去重生效），零状态变更未溯源。安全评审三轮：13+22+16=51 条发现（P0 1→3→0、P1 3+6+4、P2 4+7+7、P3 5+6+5），100% 闭环（42% 验证器改进、35% 流程级门控、23% 策略调整），其中 48 条完全修复、3 条 P3 延后加固（离线恢复边界、时钟偏移下的健康看门狗时序、eslint/npm-audit 误报刻画），注入集内零漏报。AUTO-SEC 10 条中 6 条由流水线自主派发、监控与验证完成（SEC-001/002/004/006 纯工具化，SEC-003/005 含人工代码改动），2 条需人工架构判断（安全门通道拆分、SAST 产物噪声），2 条由策略裁决关闭，比例 6:2:2。规模指标：自动化 12,661 行 Python（23 脚本）+ 6,907 行 PROMPT + 245 行 TICKET_CONTRACT.md，基础设施密度约 1.52%（v2 为 1.78%），相比 v2 的 22,946 行/51 脚本/58 锁函数实现脚本数减 55%、代码行减 45%；托管应用代码 1,290,158 行/15,840 文件；测试 114 文件/1,062 函数/19,284 行；另有 77 个 CLI 参数、17 处超时边界、120 条 fix plan、18 种发布结果码。

Conclusion: 论文主张生命周期自动化是与代码生成本质不同的问题，需要控制论式的结构而非生成能力；当自治被嵌入显式控制架构、监控、恢复协议与可追溯证据链之中时，“有界自治”是可行的工程模式——自治范围由配置而非学习决定，失败触发策略回滚而非重训练。三个 RQ 的回应为：七阶段流水线能把摄入、规范化、执行、验证这些例行分流降到“配置+异常处理”层面，但并不消除复杂架构判断与新任务族中的人类判断；自治边界体现在置信度阈值、阶段策略栅栏与每步人工覆盖三重复合强制执行，具备确定性修复计划与可验证结果的工单（依赖升级、配置变更）适合自主派发，需架构判断或跨组件协调者不适合（以 AUTO-SEC 6:2:2 为观测到的边界实例，该比例反映当前策略配置而非普适最优）；可靠性主张依赖六类机制——FSM 显式状态表示、Jira 碰撞锁、检查点式时间预算、执行与验证分离、三层去重发布、TRACE 式防篡改证据日志，但论文明确仅称其为“部分验证的安全论证”而非形式化证明，且未证明这些机制联合充分。局限与后续工作包括：单案例（一个代码库/团队/Jira 实例）、评审组与开发团队同机构、注入集由作者自建带来的选择偏差、DEGRADED 为非标准语义可能高估表面失败率、无对照组与事前功效分析、样本量有限（n=152、n=51）；关键未验证项包括碰撞锁在真实多智能体竞争下的压测、Jira 真实宕机下的降级模式现场验证、Lane 7 自 2026-03-31 上线后尚无经验结果（覆盖率/补丁准确率/假间隙率）、状态机转换的形式化验证、静态分析误报率刻画、MCP 错误注入测试与跨仓库 MCP 联邦。另需注意 MID-study 模型切换（GPT-5.4→Claude）与泳道 11→7 整合期间 PROMPT 版本推进（Lane2 v2.3.0→v2.4.0、Lane4 v3.5.0→v3.7.0 等）对前后对比的干扰；底层代码库与 Jira 数据含专有客户信息无法公开，MLT 四框架（Context Engineering/MANDATE/LATTICE/TRACE）尚未完成同行评审。

Abstract: This paper presents a closed-loop system for software lifecycle management framed as a control architecture rather than a code-generation tool. The system manages a backlog of approximately 1,602 rows across seven task families, ingests 13 structured source documents, and executes a deterministic seven-stage pipeline implemented as seven scheduled automation lanes. The automation stack comprises approximately 12,661 lines of Python across 23 scripts plus 6,907 lines of versioned prompt specifications, with checkpoint-based time budgets, 101 exception handlers, and 12 centralized lock mechanisms implemented through four core functions and eight reusable patterns. A Jira Status Contract provides externally observable collision locking, and a degraded-mode protocol supports continued local operation when Jira is unavailable. Artificial-intelligence assistance is bounded by structured context packages, configured resource caps, output re-validation, and human review gates. A formal evaluation of the initial 152-run window yielded 100% terminal-state success with a 95% Clopper-Pearson interval of [97.6%, 100%]; the system has since accumulated more than 795 run artifacts in continuous operation. Three rounds of adversarial code review identified 51 findings, all closed within the study scope (48 fully remediated, 3 closed with deferred hardening), with zero false negatives within the injected set. In an autonomous security ticket family of 10 items, six were completed through pipeline-autonomous dispatch and verification, two required manual remediation, and two were closed by policy decision. The results indicate that bounded, traceable lifecycle automation is practical when autonomy is embedded within explicit control, recovery, and audit mechanisms.

</details>


### [269] [Models as Values in a Model Expression Algebra: A Functional Approach to Model Driven Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05001)
*Sinisa Neskovic,Dejan Stojimirovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了模型表达式代数(Model Expression Algebra)作为模型驱动工程(MDE)的功能基础，通过单一形式主义统一模型构造、元模型、模板和转换。其中模型为值，模型表达式为项，求值为从项到值的解释同态。代数由四类算子组成：模型/元素创建算子、引用算子和计算算子。元模型被形式化为约束代数的类型模式，模板被理解为带自由变量的开项，转换则自然地表示为以源模型为输入参数的模型模板。论文证明了求值下的类型保持性和转换执行的类型安全性，并通过嵌入DSL在主流语言中实现，同时支持宏模型和编织模型而无需额外机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动工程中模型构造、元模型、模板和转换通常使用不同的形式主义和工具链，缺乏统一语义基础，导致理论与实现之间存在语义鸿沟，且难以在单一语言中提供形式化保证。

Method: 建立模型表达式代数形式体系，将模型作为值、模型表达式作为项、求值作为解释同态；元模型形式化为类型模式约束代数；模板形式化为带自由变量的开项；转换形式化为以模型为输入参数的模型模板；通过主流语言的类型系统强制执行形式保证。

Result: 证明了求值下的类型保持性和转换执行的类型安全性；展示了如何用单一主流语言同时作为元建模、模型构造和转换语言；无需额外机制即可支持宏模型和编织模型。

Conclusion: 模型表达式代数为MDE提供了统一的功能语义基础，通过将模型作为一等公民值、模板作为开项、转换作为参数化模型表达式，实现了理论与实现的语义一致，并借助宿主语言类型系统提供形式保证。该方法为MDE工具链的整合提供了原则性途径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a functional foundation for model driven engineering that unifies model construction, metamodels, templates, and transformations under a single formalism: the model expression algebra. In this algebra, models are values, model expressions are terms, and evaluation is the interpretation homomorphism from terms to values. Model expressions are composed from four operators: model creation and element creation operators, reference operators for retrieving models and elements, and computation operators that embed functional computations. Metamodels are type schemas that constrain the algebra, and model templates, understood as parameterized model expressions, are formalized as open terms with free variables. Model transformations then arise naturally as model templates whose input parameter is a source model. We prove type preservation under evaluation and type safety of transformation execution. Since models are themselves model elements, the algebra also supports megamodels and weaving models without additional mechanisms. The approach is realized through an embedded domain-specific language (DSL) that demonstrates how a single mainstream language can serve simultaneously as the metamodeling, model construction, and transformation language, with formal guarantees enforced by the language's type system.

</details>


### [270] [Spec Kit Agents: Context-Grounded Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05278)
*Pardis Taghavi,Santosh Bhavani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Spec Kit Agents 在 GitHub Spec Kit（Specify→Plan→Tasks→Implement）的规格驱动开发流程上，引入「上下文接地层」：每个阶段前用只读 discovery 钩子探查仓库证据（文件、约定、依赖、git 历史），每个阶段后用 validation 钩子校验中间产物（SPEC/PLAN/TASKS）与仓库一致性，实现后执行项目测试与 lint。在 5 个仓库、32 个特性任务、共 128 次运行的实验中，接地钩子使 LLM-as-judge 1–5 综合质量分提升 +0.15（满分比例 +3.0%，Wilcoxon 符号秩检验 p<0.05），同时保持 99.7–100% 的仓库级测试兼容性；在 SWE-bench Lite 上以 MiniMax-M2.5 取得 58.2% Pass@1（基线 56.5%），优于 Aider、OpenHands、SWE-Agent 等既有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型、持续演化的代码仓库中，AI 编码代理在多步任务中普遍存在「上下文盲区（context blindness）」：中间产物（规格、计划、任务清单）可能在内部自洽，却与仓库真实状态不兼容——引用不存在的 API、提出不存在的文件路径、违反本地架构与风格约定。这类错误在实现或测试阶段才暴露，迫使代理回溯修改早期产物并引入新的不一致，从而在规划、任务分解、实现各阶段间级联放大。已有的 SDD 结构化工作流（如 GitHub Spec Kit）虽外化了中间产物，但仍未把「接地与校验」变成显式的工作流操作，作者因此希望将 grounding 从代理轨迹内的尽力而为行为提升为可审计、可复用的阶段级原语。

Method: 系统架构：状态机编排器（orchestrator）+ PM 代理（需求澄清与优先级）+ 开发者代理（生成中间产物并实现代码），通过集中式消息平台通信，支持在计划评审等检查点人工介入；成功时在目标仓库创建 pull request。核心是上下文接地层，提供阶段作用域（phase-scoped）的接地/校验接口，作用于显式产物 SPEC.md、PLAN.md、TASKS.md，而非嵌入主提示词：①Discovery 钩子（阶段前，只读）使用 glob、grep、git history 等工具收集仓库证据（既有 API、约定、相关模块、依赖），条件化后续生成；②Validation 钩子（阶段后）先做结构与引用一致性检查（路径是否存在、依赖是否安装、任务列表是否可行且有序），实现后再执行 pytest、ruff、JS 测试运行器等仓库检查。工具权限最小化：PM 仅限仓库分析与版本控制检视，开发者可编辑文件与执行命令，discovery 只读，validation 附加执行权限。模型与评测分离：生成侧经 Claude Code CLI 2.1.50 路由至兼容 Anthropic 接口的 MiniMax-M2.5；评测侧用 Claude Opus 4.6 作为独立 LLM-as-judge，从完整性、正确性、风格、可维护性四维 1–5 打分取均值，并在子集上做盲化人工偏好对比；另在 FastAPI、Airflow、Dexter、Plausible Analytics、Strapi 五仓库、32 个特性任务上，对 Baseline、Augmented、Full、Full-Augmented 四配置（40 分钟 vs 90 分钟预算）及 Discovery-only、Validation-only 两个消融做配对 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验，并额外评测 SWE-bench Lite；记录提示词、工具调用、产物与执行轨迹，限流运行退出延迟分析但保留质量统计。

Result: 质量：Full-Augmented 在 90 分钟族内总体最佳，综合分由 Full 的 3.51 提升至 3.66（+0.15，配对子集 Wilcoxon p<0.05），增益跨仓库可见，FastAPI 与 Plausible 尤为明显；40 分钟族中 Augmented（3.50）略优于 Baseline（3.46）。消融显示 Validation-only（3.57，+1.71%）强于 Discovery-only（3.53，+0.57%），两者组合最强（+4.27%），说明两类钩子互补。人工盲化偏好对比（6 个任务、60 票）倾向 Full-Augmented（33 票 vs 19 票，平局 8）。测试兼容性在各配置下维持 99.7–100%，说明质量提升未以破坏既有行为为代价。延迟呈质量—运行时权衡：40 分钟族仅 +1.1 分钟（14.4→15.5，n=15），90 分钟族代价明显 +13.2 分钟（24.0→37.2，n=16），故仅在同预算族内比较。SWE-bench Lite：Baseline 56.5%、Augmented 58.2% Pass@1，超过文中对比的 Aider 26.33%、Moatless 38.00%、OpenHands 41.67%、DARS 47.00%、SWE-Agent 56.67%。附录分析指出增益不均匀：当缺陷路径被单元测试直接覆盖时收益最大，而在 django、matplotlib 等失败源自 ORM 状态迁移或可视化状态交互、仅被弱测试暴露的仓库收益较小，SymPy 类数学边界条件也可能因测试欠规范而被「锚定在不完整信号上」。

Conclusion: 显式的、以阶段为作用域的上下文接地编排（阶段前只读 discovery + 阶段后 validation）是提升多步代理式软件工程可靠性的可行设计原则：其核心价值不在于平均分的巨大跃升，而在于早期发现并阻止上下文错误的级联放大，同时保持产物与仓库证据的可审计追踪和最小权限工具访问。由于接地与校验带来额外运行时开销（90 分钟族约 +13.2 分钟），该方法更适合高风险或高复杂度任务；框架本身与模型无关（本文统一使用 MiniMax-M2.5），可迁移至其他可 API 访问的模型。局限包括：LLM-as-judge 评分与人工复核样本量有限（人工对比仅 6 个任务）、仓库与任务数量偏少、SWE-bench 增益幅度小且分布不均、缺少消融钩子本身提示词与工具集细节的深入分析。

Abstract: Spec-driven development (SDD) with AI coding agents provides a structured workflow, but agents often remain "context blind" in large, evolving repositories, leading to hallucinated APIs and architectural violations. We present Spec Kit Agents, a multi-agent SDD pipeline (with PM and developer roles) that adds phase-level, context-grounding hooks. Read-only probing hooks ground each stage (Specify, Plan, Tasks, Implement) in repository evidence, while validation hooks check intermediate artifacts against the environment. We evaluate 128 runs covering 32 features across five repositories. Context-grounding hooks improve judged quality by +0.15 on a 1-5 composite LLM-as-judge score (+3.0 percent of the full score; Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.05) while maintaining 99.7-100 percent repository-level test compatibility. We further evaluate the framework on SWE-bench Lite, where augmentation hooks improve baseline by 1.7 percent, achieving 58.2 percent Pass@1.

</details>


### [271] [Scaling Coding Agents via Atomic Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05013)
*Yingwei Ma,Yue Liu,Xinlong Yang,Yanhao Li,Kelin Fu,Yibo Miao,Yuchong Xie,Zhexu Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于原子技能的新 scaling 范式，通过定义代码定位、编辑、单元测试生成、问题复现、代码审查五个基础原子技能并进行联合强化学习训练，提升 LLM 编程智能体的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 编程智能体主要在复合基准（如 bug 修复）上训练，导致任务特定的过拟合和有限的泛化能力。复合任务难以分解，难以实现通用技能的有效扩展。

Method: 首先形式化定义五个基础原子技能（代码定位、代码编辑、单元测试生成、问题复现、代码审查），这些技能更通用且可组合。然后通过对原子技能进行联合强化学习（Joint RL）来扩展编程智能体，避免技能间的负向干扰和权衡。

Result: 原子技能的提升能够泛化到其他未见复合编程任务（bug 修复、代码重构、机器学习工程、代码安全）。联合 RL 在 5 个原子技能和 5 个复合任务上平均性能提升 18.7%。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了"通过原子技能训练"这一新 scaling 范式的有效性，为编程智能体的训练提供了新方向，强调了基础技能分解对泛化能力的重要性。

Abstract: Current LLM coding agents are predominantly trained on composite benchmarks (e.g., bug fixing), which often leads to task-specific overfitting and limited generalization. To address this, we propose a novel scaling paradigm that shifts the focus from task-level optimization to atomic skill mastery. We first formalize five fundamental atomic skills, code localization, code editing, unit-test generation, issue reproduction, and code review, that serve as the basis vectors for complex software engineering tasks. Compared with composite coding tasks, these atomic skills are more generalizable and composable. Then, we scale coding agents by performing joint RL over atomic skills. In this manner, atomic skills are consistently improved without negative interference or trade-offs between them. Notably, we observe that improvements in these atomic skills generalize well to other unseen composite coding tasks, such as bug-fixing, code refactoring, machine learning engineering, and code security. The observation motivates a new scaling paradigm for coding agents by training with atomic skills. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed paradigm. Notably, our joint RL improves average performance by 18.7% on 5 atomic skills and 5 composite tasks.

</details>


### [272] [Typify: A Lightweight Usage-driven Static Analyzer for Precise Python Type Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05067)
*Ali Aman,Muhammad Asaduzzaman,Shaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Typify是一个轻量级的Python类型推断引擎，结合符号执行、迭代不动点分析和上下文匹配检索系统，在不依赖深度学习或大数据集的情况下，实现与现有最先进系统相当或更优的类型推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python的动态类型系统为静态分析和自动化工具带来挑战，现有方法依赖类型注解或需大量训练数据和计算资源的深度学习模型，导致可扩展性和可解释性受限。

Method: Typify集成符号执行与迭代不动点分析，通过上下文匹配检索系统在项目范围内传播和预测类型信息，以执行感知方式构建和遍历依赖图，精确连接函数调用与其定义。

Result: 在ManyTypes4Py和Typilus数据集上，Typify在预测变量、参数和返回语句类型方面一致达到或超越Type4Py、HiTyper等深度学习系统以及Pyre等工业级静态类型推断工具。

Conclusion: 基于使用驱动的检索式推断可匹配或超越数据驱动方法的准确性，为大型和不断演进的Python代码库提供了实用、可解释且计算高效的类型推断替代方案。

Abstract: Python's dynamic type system, while offering significant flexibility and expressiveness, poses substantial challenges for static analysis and automated tooling, particularly in unannotated or partially annotated codebases. Existing type inference approaches often depend on existing type annotations or on deep learning models that require extensive training corpora and considerable computational resources, resulting in limited scalability and reduced interpretability. We introduce Typify, a lightweight, usage-driven static analysis engine designed to infer precise and contextually relevant type information without relying on statistical learning or large datasets. Typify integrates symbolic execution with iterative fixpoint analysis and a context-matching retrieval system to propagate and predict type information across entire projects. By constructing and traversing dependency graphs in an execution-aware manner, Typify accurately connects function calls to their definitions and infers usage-based type semantics, even in complex, interdependent modules. We evaluate Typify on a diverse corpus of real-world Python repositories, including the ManyTypes4Py and Typilus datasets, benchmarking its effectiveness in predicting types of variables, arguments, and return statements. Results from the evaluation show that Typify consistently matches or surpasses state-of-the-art deep learning-based systems such as Type4Py and HiTyper, as well as industry-standard static type inference tools like Pyre. Our findings demonstrate that usage-driven, retrieval-based inference can match or exceed the accuracy of data-driven methods, offering a practical, interpretable, and computationally efficient alternative for large and evolving Python codebases.

</details>


### [273] [Understanding: reframing automation and assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05662)
*Robin Bloomfield*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文探讨了安全与保障案例中"理解"这一关键要素逐渐被忽视的问题。随着系统复杂性和AI生成内容的增加,保障产出的形式可能看起来连贯,但实际上缺乏真正的人类理解。文章提出将"理解"作为决策中显式、可评估和可辩护的组成部分,并基于Catherine Elgin的理解认识论,通过Assurance 2.0框架提出两种具体成果物:Understanding Basis(理解基础)和Personal Understanding Statement(个人理解声明),同时警示自动化可能带来的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关键社会技术系统的生产与评估面临理解挑战:开发节奏加快、审查减少、软件复杂性增加、AI生成内容的广泛使用,可能导致产出看似连贯却缺乏真正人类理解。这对负责任的工程实践和治理决策构成威胁。

Method: 1) 基于Catherine Elgin的理解认识论建立概念基础;2) 以Assurance 2.0作为工程化路径,通过结构化论证、证据、置信度、反驳者(defeaters)和基于理论的自动化来操作化;3) 提出两种linked artefacts作为具体实现。

Result: 提出两个核心成果物:1) Understanding Basis(理解基础) - 证明现有理解对决策是否充分;2) Personal Understanding Statement(个人理解声明) - 使参与者明确表达并可质疑的理解。识别出自动化可能改善成果物生产但削弱理解的风险。

Conclusion: 理解应成为决策中显式、可评估和可辩护的组成部分;需要建立评估自动化效能和认知影响的方法论;当前威胁在于形式连贯性与实质理解之间的鸿沟正在扩大。

Abstract: Safety and assurance cases risk becoming detached from the understanding needed for responsible engineering and governance decisions. More broadly, the production and evaluation of critical socio-technical systems increasingly face an understanding challenge: pressures for increased tempo, reduced scrutiny, software complexity, and growing use of AI generated artefacts may produce outputs that appear coherent without supporting genuine human comprehension. We argue that understanding should become an explicit, assessable, and defensible component of decision making: what developers, assessors, and decision makers grasp about system behavior, evidence, assumptions, risks, and residual uncertainty. Drawing on Catherine Elgin's epistemology of understanding, we outline a conceptual foundation and then use Assurance 2.0 as an engineering route to operationalize using structured argumentation, evidence, confidence, defeaters, and theory based automation. This leads to two linked artefacts: an Understanding Basis, which justifies why available understanding is sufficient for a decision, and a Personal Understanding Statement, through which participants make their grasp explicit and challengeable. We also identify risks that automation may improve artefact production while weakening understanding, and we propose initial directions for evaluating both efficacy and epistemic impact.

</details>


### [274] [Edit, But Verify: An Empirical Audit of Instructed Code-Editing Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05100)
*Amir M. Ebrahimi,Gopi Krishnan Rajbahadur*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对现有的指令化代码编辑基准测试（CanItEdit和EDIT-Bench）进行了全面审计，发现这两个基准测试未能充分代表真实世界的代码编辑场景：超过90%的评估集中于Python，而GitHub最常用的TypeScript完全缺失；前后端开发（占真实编辑活动的46%）严重缺失；文档、测试和维护类编辑（占人类PR的31.4%）完全没有覆盖。EDIT-Bench存在严重的测试质量问题，59%的低覆盖率测试套件无法检测编辑区域外的修改，且有11个问题源于基准测试本身缺陷而非模型能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令化代码编辑（根据自然语言指令修改现有代码）占实际编码助手交互的19%，但直接评估这一能力的基准测试极少。作者调查了150多个代码相关基准测试，仅发现CanItEdit和EDIT-Bench两个符合条件的目标基准测试，因此需要对其质量进行严格审计。

Method: 作者从三个维度进行审计：（1）比较基准测试的编程语言、编辑意图和应用领域与真实世界分布（Copilot Arena、AIDev、GitHub Octoverse）的差异；（2）测量全部213个问题的测试数量、语句覆盖率和测试范围；（3）使用40个LLM在EDIT-Bench上测试，识别因基准测试缺陷导致的问题。

Result: 审计揭示了系统性偏差：两个基准测试>90%集中于Python，缺少TypeScript；前后端开发领域严重缺失；文档/测试/维护编辑零覆盖；测试数量不足（CanItEdit中位数13个，EDIT-Bench中位数4个）；EDIT-Bench中59%的低覆盖率测试套件无法检测编辑区域外的修改；40个LLM在15个问题上完全失败，其中11个源于基准测试artifact而非模型局限；29%的EDIT-Bench问题和6%的CanItEdit问题与其他问题共享代码库。

Conclusion: 现有基准测试测量的构念比实际部署决策所需的更窄。作者提出了六个基于实证的期望标准（desiderata），并发布所有审计 artifacts，以便社区构建能够可靠反映真实世界编辑能力的代码编辑基准测试。

Abstract: Instructed code editing, where an LLM modifies existing code based on a natural language instruction, accounts for roughly 19% of real-world coding assistant interactions. Yet very few benchmarks directly evaluate this capability. From a survey of over 150 code-related benchmarks, we find that only two, CanItEdit and EDIT-Bench, target instructed code editing with human-authored instructions and test-based evaluation. We audit both by comparing their programming languages, edit intents, and application domains against distributions observed in the wild (Copilot Arena, AIDev, GitHub Octoverse), and by measuring test counts, statement coverage, and test scope across all 213 problems. Both benchmarks concentrate over 90\% of evaluation on Python while TypeScript, GitHub's most-used language, is absent. Backend and frontend development, which together constitute 46% of real-world editing activity, are largely missing, and documentation, testing, and maintenance edits (31.4% of human PRs) have zero representation. Both benchmarks have modest test counts (CanItEdit median 13, EDIT-Bench median 4), though CanItEdit compensates with near-complete whole-file coverage and fail-before/pass-after validation. 59\% of EDIT-Bench's low-coverage suites would not detect modifications outside the edit region. EDIT-Bench has 15 problems that are not solved by any of 40 LLMs and 11 of these problems trace failures to poor benchmark artifacts rather than model limitations. Further, 29% of EDIT-Bench problems and 6% of CanItEdit problems share a codebase with at least one other problem within the benchmark. In summary, these benchmarks measure a narrower construct than deployment decisions require. We therefore propose six empirically grounded desiderata and release all audit artifacts so the community can build instructed code-editing benchmarks whose scores reliably reflect real-world editing capability.

</details>


### [275] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Exploit Generation with Constraint-Guided Comprehension and Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05130)
*Siyi Chen,Tianhan Luo,Shijian Wu,Xiangyu Liu,Yilin Zhou,Qi Li,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Vulnsage是一个用于自动化漏洞利用生成（AEG）的多智能体框架，通过模拟人类安全研究人员的工作流程，将复杂的AEG过程分解为多个专业子智能体（代码分析、代码生成、验证和反思智能体），由中央调度器协调进行迭代循环。实验表明，该框架比现有最先进工具（如explodejs）多生成34.64%的漏洞利用，并在实际场景中发现并验证了146个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库在现代软件开发中广泛使用，引入大量安全漏洞。现有静态分析工具产生大量误报，而传统AEG方法（如模糊测试、符号执行）面临路径覆盖和约束求解问题。虽然大语言模型在AEG中展现潜力，但如何有效利用它们理解漏洞并生成相应利用程序仍是开放问题。

Method: Vulnsage采用多智能体架构，包含四个核心组件：Code Analyzer Agent负责静态分析识别潜在漏洞并收集信息；Code Generation Agent利用LLM生成候选利用程序；Validation Agent与Reflection Agents形成反馈驱动的自改进循环，使用执行轨迹和运行时错误分析迭代改进利用程序或推理误报；中央调度器（supervisor）协调各智能体进行迭代循环。

Result: 实验评估表明，Vulnsage成功生成了比最先进工具（如explodejs）多34.64%的漏洞利用程序。此外，Vulnsage在现实场景中成功发现并验证了146个零日漏洞，证明了其在软件供应链安全评估中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架通过模拟人类安全研究人员的协作工作流程，结合LLM的能力和反馈驱动的自改进机制，能够有效解决传统AEG方法的局限性，显著提高漏洞利用生成的成功率，并在实际安全评估中展现出强大的实用价值。

Abstract: Open-source libraries are widely used in modern software development, introducing significant security vulnerabilities. While static analysis tools can identify potential vulnerabilities at scale, they often generate overwhelming reports with high false positive rates. Automated Exploit Generation (AEG) emerges as a promising solution to confirm vulnerability authenticity by generating an exploit. However, traditional AEG approaches based on fuzzing or symbolic execution face path coverage and constraint-solving problems. Although LLMs show great potential for AEG, how to effectively leverage them to comprehend vulnerabilities and generate corresponding exploits is still an open question.
  To address these challenges, we propose Vulnsage, a multi-agent framework for AEG. Vulnsage simulates human security researchers' workflows by decomposing the complex AEG process into multiple specialized sub-agents: Code Analyzer Agent, Code Generation Agent, Validation Agent, and a set of Reflection Agents, orchestrated by a central supervisor through iterative cycles. Given a target program, the Code Analyzer Agent performs static analysis to identify potential vulnerabilities and collects relevant information for each one. The Code Generation Agent then utilizes an LLM to generate candidate exploits. The Validation Agent and Reflection Agents form a feedback-driven self-refinement loop that uses execution traces and runtime error analysis to either improve the exploit iteratively or reason about the false positive alert.
  Experimental evaluation demonstrates that Vulnsage succeeds in generating 34.64\% more exploits than state-of-the-art tools such as \explodejs. Furthermore, Vulnsage has successfully discovered and verified 146 zero-day vulnerabilities in real-world scenarios, demonstrating its practical effectiveness for assisting security assessment in software supply chains.

</details>


### [276] [Compiled AI: Deterministic Code Generation for LLM-Based Workflow Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05150)
*Geert Trooskens,Aaron Karlsberg,Anmol Sharma,Lamara De Brouwer,Max Van Puyvelde,Matthew Young,John Thickstun,Gil Alterovitz,Walter A. De Brouwer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"编译型AI"范式，让大语言模型在编译阶段生成可执行代码工件，之后工作流以确定性方式执行，无需再次调用模型。通过将生成约束在嵌入于验证模板中的窄粒度业务逻辑函数内，编译型AI牺牲运行时灵活性，换取可预测性、可审计性、成本效益和降低的安全风险。系统架构包含四阶段生成-验证流程，在函数调用任务（96%完成率）和文档智能任务（80% KILE准确率）上表现优异，同时在安全评估中实现96.7%的提示注入检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业级高风险工作流（尤其是医疗场景）中，可靠性和可审计性至关重要。现有运行时推理方式存在成本高、不确定性大、难以审计等问题。本文旨在研究将LLM的代码生成能力与确定性执行相结合的编译型AI范式，以平衡性能与安全性。

Method: 提出三个核心贡献：(i) 约束型LLM代码生成系统架构；(ii) 四阶段生成-验证管道（将概率模型输出转化为生产级代码工件）；(iii) 评估框架，测量令牌摊销、确定性、可靠性、安全性和成本等运营指标。在BFCL（n=400）和DocILE（n=5,680）数据集上评估。

Result: 函数调用任务：96%任务完成率，零执行令牌，约17笔交易即实现盈亏平衡，1,000笔交易时令牌消耗降低57倍。文档智能：Code Factory变体在关键字段提取（KILE: 80.0%）上匹配直接LLM，同时达到最高行项目识别准确率（LIR: 80.4%）。安全评估（135测试用例）：提示注入检测准确率96.7%，静态代码安全分析准确率87.5%，零假阳性。

Conclusion: 编译型AI通过将生成约束在验证模板中的窄粒度业务逻辑函数，牺牲运行时灵活性以换取可预测性、可审计性、成本效率和降低的安全暴露。该范式在企业级高风险工作流中具有实际应用价值，尤其适用于医疗等对可靠性要求严格的场景。

Abstract: We study compiled AI, a paradigm in which large language models generate executable code artifacts during a compilation phase, after which workflows execute deterministically without further model invocation. This paradigm has antecedents in prior work on declarative pipeline optimization (DSPy) and hybrid neural-symbolic planning (LLM+P); our contribution is a systems-oriented study of its application to high-stakes enterprise workflows, with particular emphasis on healthcare settings where reliability and auditability are critical.
  By constraining generation to narrow business-logic functions embedded in validated templates, compiled AI trades runtime flexibility for predictability, auditability, cost efficiency, and reduced security exposure. We introduce (i) a system architecture for constrained LLM-based code generation, (ii) a four-stage generation-and-validation pipeline that converts probabilistic model output into production-ready code artifacts, and (iii) an evaluation framework measuring operational metrics including token amortization, determinism, reliability, security, and cost.
  We evaluate on two task types: function-calling (BFCL, n=400) and document intelligence (DocILE, n=5,680 invoices). On function-calling, compiled AI achieves 96% task completion with zero execution tokens, breaking even with runtime inference at approximately 17 transactions and reducing token consumption by 57x at 1,000 transactions. On document intelligence, our Code Factory variant matches Direct LLM on key field extraction (KILE: 80.0%) while achieving the highest line item recognition accuracy (LIR: 80.4%). Security evaluation across 135 test cases demonstrates 96.7% accuracy on prompt injection detection and 87.5% on static code safety analysis with zero false positives.

</details>


### [277] [Planning to Explore: Curiosity-Driven Planning for LLM Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05159)
*Alfonso Amayuelas,Firas Laakom,Piotr Piękos,Wenyi Wang,Yifan Xu,Yuhui Wang,Jürgen Schmidhuber,William Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将 LLM 测试生成形式化为对未知程序分支结构的贝叶斯探索，提出 CovQValue：以覆盖图(coverage map)作为后验的确定性代理与充分统计量，每轮并行生成 K=3 条差异化测试计划（各 S=3 个脚本），由 LLM 估计即时增益与未来可达性并计算好奇 Q 值 Q=ĝ+γ·v̂（γ=0.5）选取最优计划执行。在 TestGenEval Lite（140 文件）与新提出的 RepoExploreBench（93 模块 / 9 个 PyPI 包）中，跨 Gemini 3 Flash、GPT-5.4-mini、Mistral Large 3 三模型，CovQValue 相比 Greedy 分支覆盖提升 40–77%，在 77–84% 的目标上取胜，并发布面向迭代式探索的基准 RepoExploreBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 测试生成方法（CodaMosa、CoverUp、TELPA、TestForge 等）本质上是贪心的：它们最大化即时覆盖率增益、独立生成测试而不从先前执行中学习。当到达深层分支需要一连串单独贡献零覆盖的"设置步骤"（导入链、类初始化、校验门、配置要求）时，贪心策略会陷入平台期，无法突破。此外，缺乏观测反馈时，LLM 仅凭代码静态检查选择测试近乎随机（Greedy 表现甚至略低于 Random）。Sun et al. (2011) 已证明贝叶斯设定下最大化累积信息增益的最优策略必须前瞻规划，因为信息增益仅在期望意义上可加；其"走廊问题"正对应此处的"覆盖走廊"结构。作者希望为覆盖率导向的测试生成提供有理论依据的替代方案，并指出覆盖率反馈本身才是闭环的关键（无反馈则选择近似随机）。

Method: **理论框架**：将迭代测试生成形式化为未知环境 Θ（程序分支可达结构）上的贝叶斯探索。先验 p(Θ)=无已观测分支；交互历史 h=(a,o) 对；期望信息增益 g̃(a|h)=I(O;Θ|h,a)；递归好奇 Q 值 q(h,a)=g̃(a|h)+γ·E_{o|h,a}[v(hao)]，v(h)=max_a q(h,a)。覆盖图 𝒞_t 作为 p(Θ|h) 的充分统计量/确定性代理（更新规则 𝒞_t=𝒜_{t-1}∪B(a_t)），以结构化文本回注 LLM，避免使用校准差的采样熵、多模型分歧等代理信号。

**CovQValue 算法（Algorithm 1）**：每轮并行生成 K=3 条候选计划 π_k=(t_1…t_S)，S=3，每条计划配不同"多样性提示"（主功能 / 错误处理 / 跨模块交互 / 关注未测异常路径 / 最深嵌套分支 / 导入链依赖等），以促使结构上不同的测试序列而非同一方案的 K 个变体；随后用同一 LLM 对每条计划打分，输出两个 0–50 的数值：即时增益 ĝ（预计新发现分支数）与未来可达性 v̂（执行后额外变得可达的分支数），计算 Q=ĝ+γ·v̂（γ=0.5），执行最高分计划的 S 个脚本并更新覆盖图。精确 Q 值计算对规划视野指数级不可行，故以 LLM 作为启发式估值器；因只需相对排序，绝对尺度不影响选择。

**覆盖走廊（coverage corridor）**：单个步骤零覆盖但整体解锁深层逻辑的设置序列；递归 Q 结构的未来项 E[v(hao)] 赋予"零即时增益但高可达性"的动作正值，故评分器会优先选择做设置的计划而非表面分支。

**实验设计**：两基准共 233 个真实 Python 文件 / 19 个仓库。TestGenEval Lite（SWE-bench 衍生，160 目标中评估 140，排除需编译 C 扩展的 scikit-learn），SWE-bench Docker 测试床；RepoExploreBench（本文提出，从 PyPI 下载量前 150 包中按纯 Python、≥5K 行、无 C 扩展/构建工具、具走廊结构三条标准选出 9 包，再按 ≥200 行、功能多样、排除测试/私有/遗留模块等选出 93 模块、77,242 行，单一 Docker 镜像内用标准 branch coverage 工具测量）。四策略等执行预算 N=24：Random、Greedy、Coverage-map 引导的 Greedy（CovGreedy）、CovQValue（8 轮 × 3 脚本 + 每轮 3 次 Q 值打分调用）。模型：Gemini 3 Flash / GPT-5.4-mini / Mistral Large 3，同一模型贯穿生成、打分与选择；生成温度 0.9，打分/选择 0.3。指标：分支覆盖（主）、行覆盖、测试通过率。

Result: **主结果**：CovQValue 在全部 6 组（2 基准 × 3 模型）均取得最高分支覆盖。RepoExploreBench 均值：Gemini 72.4（Greedy 41.8，Δ+30.6）、GPT-5.4-mini 49.8（28.6，Δ+21.2）、Mistral 59.1（42.3，Δ+16.8），相对提升约 40–74%。TestGenEval Lite：108.9（61.5，Δ+47.4）、80.5（53.2，Δ+27.2）、100.0（65.0，Δ+35.0），相对提升 51–77%。所有 CovQValue vs Greedy 对比 p<0.0001（配对 t 检验），Cohen's d 0.49–0.77；逐样本胜率 77–84%。

**基线分析**：Greedy 不优于 Random，印证无覆盖反馈时 LLM 选择近似随机；CovGreedy（有反馈无规划）有中等但不稳定的提升，说明规划是额外增益来源。累计覆盖曲线：Random/Greedy 约 5 步后即平台化并只生成同类测试变体，CovGreedy 稳步上升，CovQValue 上升最快且在发现走廊时出现陡增，差距随执行步数扩大，与信息增益的累积性质一致。

**分仓库结果**：CovQValue 在两个基准的每个仓库上均为最优，增益最大的仓库为 sympy（+55%）、astropy（+55%）、matplotlib（+52%）、rich，即分支逻辑最复杂者；即使所有方法覆盖都低的仓库（seaborn、pylint）也持平或更优。

**覆盖 vs 通过率权衡**：Random/Greedy 通过率高（75–95%）但覆盖低（生成"安全"重复测试）；CovQValue/CovGreedy 通过率较低（57–71%）却覆盖更高，说明增益来自更大胆、更具探索性的测试而非更安全的测试；行覆盖结果趋势一致（RepoExploreBench 行覆盖最高 197/151/171）。

**消融（RepoExploreBench，Gemini 3 Flash）**：执行预算从 N=8（58.0）到 N=24（71.2）稳步提升，Random 始终约 41 持平，说明覆盖图是核心驱动因素；固定 8 轮时计划长度 S 越大覆盖越高，Random 在 N=40 预算下仅约 43 分支，而 CovQValue 在同预算下 S=5 达 79 分支，说明多步前瞻有超出单纯增加执行的独立贡献；折扣因子 γ=0 时 68.6、γ=0.5 时 71.1、γ=1.0 时 70.0，差距不大但方向一致，说明未来价值项提供有用信号；组件分解中仅加生成多样性 +18.6、仅加 Q 值打分 +20.5、完整方法 +23.1（相对覆盖图基线 49.3），两者各自解决大部分差距且存在部分互补。

**案例研究**：flask.app 上三个基线 24 步全部停在 2 分支（应用工厂需特定初始化序列），CovQValue 立即突破并达到 182 分支；werkzeug.http 中 Greedy 停在 23 分支，CovQValue 达 127（分步解析 ETag/字典头 +36 分支的 options_header/cookie 机制），细节展示了引号/转义状态机形成的走廊；jinja2.ext 初期平坦，进入扩展注册走廊后才进展。

Conclusion: 迭代式 LLM 测试生成可被严格地建模为未知环境中的贝叶斯探索：覆盖图充当探索状态的充分统计量与后验的确定性代理，Q 值评分在"即时分支发现"与"未来可达性"间做权衡，从而突破贪心覆盖率导向方法在设置序列（走廊）上的平台期。核心经验性结论是闭环中的覆盖反馈不可或缺——没有后验更新就没有可优化的信息增益，LLM 的选择近乎随机。作者指出 Q 值估计较为粗糙（启发式、0–50 尺度、γ 灵敏度小），未来可引入更丰富的价值估计器（基于执行的评分、多步 rollout），并把该框架推广到 API 发现、科学实验等需要与未知环境顺序交互的部分可观测 agent 场景；伦理上强调生成测试需与自动生成代码同等对待，在更开放的真实系统中应加入安全探索护栏。代码与基准已开源（github.com/amayuelas/qcurious-tester）。

Abstract: The use of LLMs for code generation has naturally extended to code testing and evaluation. As codebases grow in size and complexity, so does the need for automated test generation. Current approaches for LLM-based test generation rely on strategies that maximize immediate coverage gain, a greedy approach that plateaus on code where reaching deep branches requires setup steps that individually yield zero new coverage. Drawing on principles of Bayesian exploration, we treat the program's branch structure as an unknown environment, and an evolving coverage map as a proxy probabilistic posterior representing what the LLM has discovered so far. Our method, CovQValue, feeds the coverage map back to the LLM, generates diverse candidate plans in parallel, and selects the most informative plan by LLM-estimated Q-values, seeking actions that balance immediate branch discovery with future reachability. Our method outperforms greedy selection on TestGenEval Lite, achieving 51-77% higher branch coverage across three popular LLMs and winning on 77-84% of targets. In addition, we build a benchmark for iterative test generation, RepoExploreBench, where they achieve 40-74%. These results show the potential of curiosity-driven planning methods for LLM-based exploration, enabling more effective discovery of program behavior through sequential interaction

</details>


### [278] [Corporate Training in Brazilian Software Engineering: A Qualitative Study of Useful Learning Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05209)
*Rodrigo Siqueira,Antonio Oliveira,Breno Alves de Andrade,Lidiane C S Gomes,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对195名巴西软件工程从业者的开放式问卷回答进行主题分析，归纳出五类被认为最有用的学习经历：持续技术更新（T1，n=91）、实践与应用式学习（T2，n=39）、正规学术教育（T3，n=35）、社会化学习与人际网络（T4，n=30）、领导力与软技能发展（T5，n=17），并记录了一项低频的涌现观察——自我导向学习与对培训的不信任（EO1，n=5）。共现分析显示T1–T2是最强连接（16次），表明专业者将"持续更新"与"即时可用"视为不可分割。核心结论：感知有用性高度依赖与日常工作需求的对齐和可立即应用的属性；有用学习不可归约为单一模式，应设计为整合技术、学术、社会与自主学习维度的"培训生态系统"而非孤立事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人力资源与培训研究长期聚焦组织政策、教学设计与干预效果，忽视了从业者自身如何描述其最有价值的学习经历；已有定量研究（Andrade等2025年对26项研究按Salas框架的分类）也未聚焦从业者叙事。此外，学界与产业界的"技能鸿沟"争论（Diniz等2024）多从组织或教学设计者视角出发，缺乏个体视角的经验证据，因而对"教什么、如何设计才真正有用"缺乏依据。本研究以Salas框架为解释性透镜，聚焦巴西软件工程从业者，回答：哪些学习经历被认为最有用，以及这种"有用性"由何种特征界定。

Method: 质性研究设计，基于Terry等（2017）六阶段主题分析，辅以IRAMUTEQ的词频与词形还原分析及主题间共现分析。数据来自在线问卷中的开放式问题QQ1（"你经历过的最有用的学习经历是什么？为什么？"），全问卷282份有效回答中195份（69.1%）作答该开放题，构成分析语料；数据采集于2025年9月9日至11月2日。招募为便利抽样（LinkedIn、WhatsApp、Telegram群组、邮件列表），纳入标准为年满18岁、从事软件工程工作、近12个月参加过至少一次雇主资助的企业培训。编码由单一研究者完成（具有10年软件工程经验、含2年技术负责人经历），资深作者督导复核，未做平行编码或正式评分者间信度。研究者承认因团队均熟悉Salas框架，主题构建受理论滤镜影响，故将过程定性为"溯因式（abductive）"而非纯归纳，并以IRAMUTEQ词频（如course、training高频，practice为最高频动词）作为描述性三角验证。饱和度为事后评估：前16份回答（约8%）已出现全部6个主题轴，其余179份未新增主题，编码层面饱和接近但未完全达成。伦理审批号CAEE 91121125.4.0000.5208；引文由葡语经生成式AI辅助翻译并由作者复核；复现包发布于Zenodo（10.5281/zenodo.19896302）。

Result: 从195份回答中生成286次编码出现（89个不同编码），归为6个初步主题轴，最终整合为5个主题与1项涌现观察，共217次主题出现（因单条回答可映射多主题而超过人数）。频次：T1持续技术更新91、T2实践与应用式学习39、T3正规学术教育35、T4社会化学习与网络30、T5领导力与软技能17、EO1自我导向学习与怀疑态度5。词形还原显示course(47)、company(29)、area(23)、training(23)为高频名词，practice(21)为最高频动词，与T1/T2主题呼应。共现网络：T1–T2=16（最强连接）、T1–T5=9、T3–T5=4、T1–T4=3、T2–T4=2、T3–T4=2、T1–T3=2、T2–T5=1、T3–T2=1。T3与T2的低共现被作者审慎解释为"互补层次"而非"竞争替代"（作为待检验假设），唯一桥接T2与T3的回答是"以实践为主、由在岗专业人士授课的MBA"。样本特征：男性73.8%，平均年龄34.2岁，91.3%本科及以上，59.0%有研究生学历，大型企业（100人以上）占77.9%，软件开发39.0%，最近一次培训为自愿者71.3%。作者提出四条分析性命题PR1–PR4（持续更新与实践不可分；学术教育为概念基础；社会互动提升其他模态价值；有用性多维整合），均为供后续定量检验的方向性命题而非确认性结论。

Conclusion: 感知有用性的核心特征是与日常工作需求的对齐和即时可应用性；T1与T2在频次与共现上的双重收敛强化了软件工程领域持续学习的必要性。规范层面的结论是：有用学习不可还原为单一模态，正式学术教育与实践学习在从业者体验中是互补的层次而非竞争选项（此为较受支持但尚待检验的假设），组织应构建整合各维度的"培训生态系统"而非交付孤立培训事件，课程与培养方案亦不应被设计为孤立活动。局限包括：单一编码者、无事前饱和度标准与评分者间信度、便利抽样与自选择导致的非应答偏差（87人未答）、高教育程度样本可能抬高T3显著性、回忆性叙述的记忆失真、职级定义跨组织异质、仅限巴西且仅限软件工程领域，因果与推广性受限。未来工作应检验PR1–PR4、开展纵向与跨情境研究，并探讨产业— academia—个体在技能鸿沟中的三方动态。

Abstract: Context: Quantitative studies can identify statistical predictors of training quality, but they often fail to capture what professionals themselves consider genuinely useful learning experiences and why. Objective: This study qualitatively investigates which types of learning experiences are perceived as most useful by Brazilian software engineering professionals and what characteristics define this usefulness. Method: Open-ended responses from 195 software engineering professionals were analyzed using Thematic Analysis, supported by frequency and lemmatization analysis using IRAMUTEQ and co-occurrence analysis between themes. Results: Five themes emerged: Continuous Technical Updating (T1), Practical and Applied Learning (T2), Formal Academic Education (T3), Social Learning and Networking (T4), and Leadership Development and Soft Skills (T5). Technical updating and practical application dominate professionals' accounts. Formal education, social learning, and soft skills are also valued as complementary dimensions. Conclusions: Perceived usefulness is strongly tied to alignment with daily work demands and immediate applicability. The convergence of technical updating (T1) and practical application (T2) in both frequency and co-occurrence reinforces the imperative of continuous learning in software engineering. Useful learning is not reducible to a single modality: genuinely valued experiences span technical, academic, social, and self-directed dimensions. Formal academic education and practical learning are perceived as complementary rather than competing. Organizations should design training ecosystems that integrate these dimensions rather than delivering isolated events.

</details>


### [279] [Corporate Training in Brazilian Software Engineering: A Quantitative Study of Professional Perceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05263)
*Rodrigo Siqueira,Antonio Oliveira,Breno Alves Andrade,Lidiane C. S. Gomes,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究针对软件工程专业人员的企业培训质量与效果影响因素进行定量分析。通过对282名巴西软件工程师的问卷调查，发现认知投入、活动多样性和讲师表现是预测培训质量与效果的最强因素。强制参与会显著降低动机和培训质量感知。研究结果与通用培训效果文献一致，表明软件企业可应用现有培训设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略培训对软件工程师的职业发展至关重要，但目前缺乏从专业人员视角评估培训质量和效果的因素分析工具，且软件工程领域尚无经过验证的测量工具。

Method: 采用定量问卷调查法，对282名巴西软件工程专业人员进行调查。开发结构化问卷，使用多氯相关(polychoric correlation)方法进行数据分析。

Result: 三个紧密相关的因素——认知投入、活动多样性和讲师表现，成为感知培训质量和效果的最强预测因子。强制参与显著降低动机和培训质量感知。个人时间影响感知与培训质量基本独立。这些发现与通用培训效果文献一致。

Conclusion: 软件工程领域的培训效果主要由三个因素决定：认知投入、活动多样性和讲师表现。强制参与对动机、相关性感知和培训质量产生负面影响，同时加剧时间负担感知。Salas和Cannon-Bowers框架在软件工程情境中产生一致结果，有望成为未来心理测量验证的候选框架。

Abstract: Context: Strategic corporate training is essential for the sustained professional development of software engineers. However, there is a knowledge gap regarding the factors that drive quality and effectiveness of such training from the professionals' perspective, and no validated instrument exists for assessing these factors in the software engineering (SE) domain. Objective: This study aims to quantitatively analyze which factors influence SE professionals' perceptions of corporate training quality and effectiveness. Method: A quantitative survey was conducted with 282 Brazilian SE professionals. A structured questionnaire was developed and polychoric correlation was adopted for data analysis. Results: Three tightly correlated factors (cognitive engagement, variety of activities, and instructor performance) emerged as the strongest predictors of perceived training quality and effectiveness. Mandatory participation significantly reduces motivation and perceived training quality. Perceived impact on personal time proved to be largely independent of training quality. These findings are consistent with the general training effectiveness literature. Conclusions: Training effectiveness in the SE context is predominantly determined by three factors: cognitive engagement, variety of activities, and instructor performance. Mandatory participation negatively influences motivation, perceived relevance, and perceived training quality, while also amplifying the perception of time burden. The consistency with the general literature suggests that software organizations do not need to reinvent training design principles and can apply established guidelines with confidence. Salas and Cannon-Bowers' framework produced coherent results in the SE context, making it a promising candidate for future psychometric validation.

</details>


### [280] [FLARE: Agentic Coverage-Guided Fuzzing for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05289)
*Mingxuan Hui,Xinyue Li,Lu Wang,Chengcheng Wan,Yifan Wang,Yimian Wang,Feiyue Song,Beining Shi,Yixi Li,Yaxiao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FLARE是一个针对多智能体LLM系统（MAS）的新型测试框架，通过从源代码中提取规范和行为空间，结合覆盖引导的模糊测试技术检测MAS故障，解决了传统软件测试技术无法有效测试MAS的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAS应用常因LLM智能体的非确定性行为和智能体间复杂交互而出现故障（如无限循环、工具调用失败），但传统软件测试技术无法有效检测这些故障，原因包括：缺乏LLM智能体规范、MAS行为空间庞大、正确性判断依赖语义。

Method: FLARE框架工作流程：(1) 输入MAS源代码；(2) 从智能体定义中提取规范和行为空间；(3) 基于规范构建测试预言机；(4) 进行覆盖引导的模糊测试以暴露故障；(5) 分析执行日志判断测试通过与否；(6) 生成故障报告。覆盖指标包括智能体间覆盖（inter-agent coverage）和智能体内覆盖（intra-agent coverage）。

Result: 在16个多样化的开源应用上评估，FLARE达到96.9%的智能体间覆盖率和91.1%的智能体内覆盖率，分别优于基线方法9.5%和1.0%；此外，发现了56个MAS特有的此前未知故障。

Conclusion: FLARE能有效测试多智能体LLM系统，通过规范提取和覆盖引导模糊测试相结合的方法，可检测出MAS特有的复杂故障类型，为MAS质量保障提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Multi-Agent LLM Systems (MAS) have been adopted to automate complex human workflows by breaking down tasks into subtasks. However, due to the non-deterministic behavior of LLM agents and the intricate interactions between agents, MAS applications frequently encounter failures, including infinite loops and failed tool invocations. Traditional software testing techniques are ineffective in detecting such failures due to the lack of LLM agent specification, the large behavioral space of MAS, and semantic-based correctness judgment.
  This paper presents FLARE, a novel testing framework tailored for MAS. FLARE takes the source code of MAS as input and extracts specifications and behavioral spaces from agent definitions. Based on these specifications, FLARE builds test oracles and conducts coverage-guided fuzzing to expose failures. It then analyzes execution logs to judge whether each test has passed and generates failure reports. Our evaluation on 16 diverse open-source applications demonstrates that FLARE achieves 96.9% inter-agent coverage and 91.1% intra-agent coverage, outperforming baselines by 9.5% and 1.0%. FLARE also uncovers 56 previously unknown failures unique to MAS.

</details>


### [281] [On the Role of Fault Localization Context for LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05481)
*Melika Sepidband,Hung Viet Pham,Hadi Hemmati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于LLM自动程序修复中故障定位（FL）上下文策略的大规模实证研究，发现文件级定位是主导因素（15-17倍提升），6-10个相关文件为最优配置，上下文并非越多越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障定位是LLM自动程序修复的关键组件，但其影响尚未得到充分探索。具体而言，尚不清楚需要多少定位、上下文是否有益、以及如何获取上下文。

Method: 在500个SWE-bench Verified实例上使用GPT-5-mini进行大规模实证研究，评估61种配置，涵盖文件级、元素级和行级上下文的变化。

Result: 文件级定位是主导因素，比无文件基线提升15-17倍；约6-10个相关文件的配置修复效果最佳；元素级上下文扩展提供条件性增益；行级上下文扩展因噪声放大而频繁降低性能；LLM检索优于结构启发式方法。

Conclusion: 最有效的FL策略通常在高抽象级别结合广泛语义理解与精确行级定位。这挑战了上下文越多修复效果越好的假设，为设计LLM FL策略提供实践指导。

Abstract: Fault Localization (FL) is a key component of Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR), yet its impact remains underexplored. In particular, it is unclear how much localization is needed, whether additional context beyond the predicted buggy location is beneficial, and how such context should be retrieved. We conduct a large-scale empirical study on 500 SWE-bench Verified instances using GPT-5-mini, evaluating 61 configurations that vary file-level, element-level, and line-level context. Our results show that more context does not consistently improve repair performance. File-level localization is the dominant factor, yielding a 15-17x improvement over a no-file baseline. Expanding file context is often associated with improved performance, with successful repairs most commonly observed in configurations with approximately 6-10 relevant files. Element-level context expansion provides conditional gains that depend strongly on the file context quality, while line-level context expansion frequently degrades performance due to noise amplification. LLM-based retrieval generally outperforms structural heuristics while using fewer files and tokens. Overall, the most effective FL context strategy typically combines a broad semantic understanding at higher abstraction levels with precise line-level localization. These findings challenge our assumption that increasing the localization context uniformly improves APR, and provide practical guidance for designing LLM-based FL strategies.

</details>


### [282] [SCOPE: A Dataset of Stereotyped Prompts for Counterfactual Fairness Assessment of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05555)
*Alessandra Parziale,Gianmario Voria,Valeria Pontillo,Andrea De Lucia,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SCOPE是一个大规模反事实提示数据集（241,280个提示，120,640个反事实对），用于系统性研究LLM的群体敏感行为，涵盖1,438个主题、9个偏见维度和1,536个群体，包含四种交际意图类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM偏见探测资源主要依赖小规模、模板化的反事实示例或固定句子对，存在语言多样性有限、主题覆盖狭窄、缺乏对交际意图影响模型行为分析支持等问题。

Method: 设计并构建了一个大规模反事实提示对数据集，所有提示基于四种不同交际意图（问答、推荐、指示、澄清）生成，并跨越多个维度和群体。

Result: SCOPE包含241,280个提示，组织为120,640个反事实对，涵盖1,438个主题、9个偏见维度和1,536个 demographic groups。

Conclusion: 该资源为评估公平性、鲁棒性和反事实一致性提供了受控的、语义对齐的、意图感知的评估基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) now serve as the foundation for a wide range of applications, from conversational assistants to decision support tools, making the issue of fairness in their results increasingly important. Previous studies have shown that LLM outputs can shift when prompts reference different demographic groups, even when intent and semantic content remain constant. However, existing resources for probing such disparities rely primarily on small, template-based counterfactual examples or fixed sentence pairs. These benchmarks offer limited linguistic diversity, narrow topical coverage, and little support for analyzing how communicative intent affects model behavior. To address these limitations, we introduce SCOPE (Stereotype-COnditioned Prompts for Evaluation), a large-scale dataset of counterfactual prompt pairs designed to enable systematic investigation of group-sensitive behavior in LLMs. SCOPE contains 241,280 prompts organized into 120,640 counterfactual pairs, each grounded in one of 1,438 topics and spanning nine bias dimensions and 1,536 demographic groups. All prompts are generated under four distinct communicative intents: Question, Recommendation, Direction, and Clarification, ensuring broad coverage of common interaction styles. This resource provides a controlled, semantically aligned, and intent-aware basis for evaluating fairness, robustness, and counterfactual consistency.

</details>


### [283] [An Iterative Test-and-Repair Framework for Competitive Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05560)
*Lingxiao Tang,Muyang Ye,Zhaoyang Chu,Xiaoxue Ren,Zhongxin Liu,Lingfeng Bao,He Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FixAudit通过迭代的测试-修复调试循环改进代码生成，训练一个共享模型同时扮演Fixer和Auditor两个角色，在APPS、CodeContests和xCodeEval三个基准上显著超越同等规模的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CURE框架存在两个关键局限：(1) Tester不读取候选代码，导致生成的测试难以暴露实现特定的bug；(2) Coder每次从零生成候选程序，无法学习基于失败测试修复有bug的程序。

Method: FixAudit训练一个共享模型通过四个阶段实现两个专业角色：Fixer根据失败测试修复当前候选代码，Auditor读取候选代码生成能暴露其剩余bug的新测试。框架采用迭代的定向测试-修复调试循环，而非从零生成。

Result: 在APPS、CodeContests、xCodeEval三个基准上，应用于7B模型时，FixAudit超越了同家族32B模型的平均性能；相比基于相同7B基模型的强基线，FixAudit将平均Pass@1提升35.1%至36.8%，平均AvgPassRatio提升7.1%至24.5%。

Conclusion: FixAudit通过将竞争性代码生成重新定位为迭代测试-修复过程，结合专门训练的Fixer和Auditor角色，在保持模型规模效率的同时显著提升了代码生成质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in code generation, but competitive programming remains a challenge. Recent training-based methods have improved code generation by using reinforcement learning (RL) with execution feedback. The more recent framework CURE further incorporates test generation into the training process, jointly training a Coder and a Tester within a single model. At inference time, the Coder generates many candidate programs, and the Tester generates tests from the problem description. The candidate who passes the most of the generated tests is selected as the final answer. However, CURE has two critical limitations. First, the Tester never reads any candidate code, so its tests often fail to expose implementation-specific bugs. Second, the Coder generates every candidate from scratch and never learns to fix a buggy program based on a failed test. To address these limitations, we propose FixAudit, which approaches competitive code generation from a new perspective: starting from a single initial candidate, it iteratively improves the candidate through a targeted test-and-repair debugging cycle. The framework trains one shared model with two specialized roles through four stages: the Fixer, which repairs the current candidate based on a failing test, and the Auditor, which reads the candidate code to generate new tests that expose its remaining bugs. We evaluate FixAudit on three benchmarks: APPS, CodeContests, and xCodeEval. Applied to a 7B model, the framework surpasses the average performance of the larger 32B baseline within the same model family under the zero-shot setting. Compared to strong baselines built on the same 7B base model, FixAudit improves average Pass@1 by 35.1% to 36.8% and average AvgPassRatio by 7.1% to 24.5%.

</details>


### [284] [Bias Ahead: Sensitive Prompts as Early Warnings for Fairness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05575)
*Gianmario Voria,Martina De Lucia,Alessandra Raia,Andrea De Lucia,Gemma Catolino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"sensitive prompts"（敏感提示）作为一种新的公平性评估抽象概念，用于识别那些虽不固有偏见但更易引发有偏见或不当响应的输入。构建了包含12,801条提示的SensY数据集，涵盖七个主题领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性基准测试主要关注刻板印象关联，无法有效预见真实世界中多样化的风险场景。研究需要新的评估范式来提前识别LLM在处理敏感内容时的潜在偏见问题。

Method: 1）提出敏感提示概念；2）构建SensY数据集（12,801条提示，含敏感/非敏感分类，涵盖7个主题领域，结合合成生成与真实用户输入）；3）对3个开源LLM进行查询；4）人工分析4,500条响应；5）开发自动化敏感提示分类器。

Result: 模型虽常提供事实正确答案，但往往忽视敏感输入的伦理、关系或情境影响；自动化分类器在敏感提示预测上表现稳健。

Conclusion: 敏感提示可作为LLM公平性风险的早期预警机制，将公平性评估从被动缓解转向预防性设计，使开发者能在部署前预见和管理偏见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being increasingly integrated into software systems, offering powerful capabilities but also raising concerns about fairness. Existing fairness benchmarks, however, focus on stereotype-specific associations, which limit their ability to anticipate risks in diverse, real-world contexts. In this paper, we propose sensitive prompts as a new abstraction for fairness evaluation: inputs that are not inherently biased but are more likely to elicit biased or inadequate responses due to the sensitivity of their content. We construct and release SensY, a dataset of 12,801 prompts, categorized as sensitive and non-sensitive, spanning seven thematic domains, combining synthetic generation and real user inputs. Using this dataset, we query three open-source LLMs and manually analyze 4,500 responses to evaluate their adequacy in answering sensitive prompts. Results show that while models often provide factually correct answers, they frequently fail to acknowledge the ethical, relational, or contextual implications of sensitive inputs. In addition, we develop an automated classifier for predicting prompt sensitivity, achieving robust performance on sensitive prompts. Our findings demonstrate that prompt sensitivity can serve as an effective early-warning mechanism for fairness risks in LLMs. This perspective shifts fairness assessment from reactive mitigation toward preventive design, enabling developers to anticipate and manage bias before deployment.

</details>


### [285] [SemLink: A Semantic-Aware Automated Test Oracle for Hyperlink Verification using Siamese Sentence-BERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05711)
*Guan-Yan Yang,Wei-Ling Wen,Shu-Yuan Ku,Farn Wang,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SemLink 用孪生 Sentence-BERT 模型作为“语义测试预言机”，通过比较超链接源上下文（锚文本、DOM 邻近文本、图像 OCR）与目标网页内容（标题、h1–h3、TextRank 关键词）的语义一致性，检测 HTTP 200 但内容已“语义漂移/软性链接腐烂”的失效。它自建 63,870 对的 HWPPs 数据集，Recall 达 96.00%（GPT-5.2 为 98.30%，差距 2.3%），推理速度 30.87 links/s，约比 GPT-5.2 快 47.5 倍、比本地 Llama-3-70B 快约 300 倍，是唯一落入 0.1s 实时 CI/CD 区间的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 应用依赖超链接互联资源，但 Web 的动态性导致两类退化：链接腐烂（link rot，目标不可用、返回 404/410）与更隐蔽的语义漂移（semantic drift，HTTP 200 正常但目标内容已与源上下文无关，如域名停放页、软 404、通用首页）。传统工具（W3C Link Checker、Screaming Frog）仅是“崩溃预言机”（crash oracle），只能检查状态码，无法验证内容相关性，因而形成典型的测试预言机问题（Test Oracle Problem）。用 LLM 做语义判定又带来三重重型负担：成本高（大规模回归测试 API 费用不可承受）、延迟高（生成式推理比判别式模型慢几个数量级）、输出不确定（导致 flaky 测试），并额外面临提示注入（prompt injection）等安全风险。而 TF-IDF/LSI 等早期统计方法又难以处理“Read More”这类语义模糊的短锚文本。因此需要一个既具语义理解能力、又足够快、便宜、确定、可大规模部署的功能性预言机。

Method: 整体为“特征抽取管线 + 孪生神经网络”两阶段。1) 数据集构建：基于“维护假设”（高流量、活跃维护站点上的链接大概率语义正确）用 Selenium 爬取 5 类站点共 381 个种子 URL（新闻、电商、教育、政府门户、技术博客，约 75% 中文 / 25% 英文），过滤 javascript:/mailto:/#锚 等非导航链接，只保留 HTTP 200 目标，清洗空锚文本/空正文后得到 63,870 对正样本（HWPPs，85%/15% 划分训练/验证）；另从 100 个未见过域名的页面收集 16,951 条链接，人工标注构建 2,000 正 + 2,000 负的独立测试集。2) 源上下文抽取 H_info = {锚文本 T_anchor, 视觉语义 T_img（alt/title 属性 + EasyOCR 文本 + 预训练图标分类）, 启发式 Side-Text T_context}；Side-Text 通过 DOM 遍历启发式取同父容器的前后兄弟文本节点与父容器文本，最多 k=5 条。3) 目标页抽取 P_info = {标题 T_title, h1–h3 结构标题 T_headers, 正文 TextRank top-N 关键词 K_body}，避免全文噪声与 token 超限。4) 模型：两个共享权重的 SBERT（distiluse-base-multilingual-cased-v2）骨干分别编码 512 维，经共享线性层投影为 128 维嵌入 v_H、v_P；取逐元素绝对差 d = |v_H − v_P| 送入三层 MLP（各 128 隐单元 + Dropout + ReLU），末层 Sigmoid 输出相似度概率 ŷ ∈ [0,1]。5) 损失：提出混合目标 L_total = λ1·L_tri + λ2·[L_BCE(A,P) + L_BCE(A,N)]，最终经验最优为 λ1=0、λ2=1.0（即仅 BCE，margin α=1.0）；原因是 batch 较小、缺少难负样本挖掘时 Triplet 约束受限，BCE 梯度更稳定。6) 加权聚合：S_final = max_{i,j}(Model(T_Hi, T_Pj) × w(T))，采用最大化而非平均（借鉴 Multiple Instance Learning：单个强信号即可判定，避免被背景噪声稀释）；w(T) 按 DOM 距离线性衰减，锚文本与 OCR 为 1.0，Side-Text 1..5 为 0.9..0.5，阈值为 τ=0.7，低于阈值判为 Irrelevant。7) 基线：GPT-5.2、GPT-4o、GPT-3.5 Turbo、Llama-3-8B/70B，采用零样本 5 点 Likert 量表提示模板与受限输出格式。

Result: RQ1 效果：SemLink Accuracy 92.70%、Precision 90.06%、Recall 96.00%、F1 92.93%；低于 GPT-4o（98.37% F1）与 GPT-5.2（99.09% F1），但高于 GPT-3.5 Turbo（94.27%）与 Llama-3-8B（88.18%），Recall 优于 GPT-3.5（89.65%）与 Llama-3-70B（95.30%），假阴性最少。RQ2 消融：仅锚文本 F1 91.80%、Recall 89.30%；加入 Side-Text 使 Recall +6.26%（至 95.56%）；图像链接子集（2,406 样本）中无图像文本 84.07%，仅属性 89.86%，仅 OCR 86.46%，属性+OCR 最优 90.60%，再叠加 LabelDroid 图标标注反而降至 88.81%（噪声）。RQ3 效率：SemLink 30.87 links/s、单链接推理约 0.03s，相对 GPT-5.2（0.65 links/s）提速 47.5 倍，相对本地 Llama-3-70B（0.10 links/s）约 300 倍；10 万链接回归套件 SemLink 约 54 分钟、GPT-5.2 约 42.7 小时且约 146 美元 API 费用。定性案例：成功识别返回 200 的软 404 错误页、语义漂移的泛化落地页，并用父级 Side-Text“Campus Life”消歧“Read More”。误差分析发现三类假阴性：登录/鉴权重定向、纯视觉目标页（无文本与 alt）、泛化分类标签（如“公告”对应具体子项）。

Conclusion: SemLink 以固定判别式孪生 SBERT 架构填补了“语法级检查器”与“昂贵生成式 AI”之间的空白，在 Recall 上逼近 GPT-5.2（96.00% vs 98.30%）而速度提升约 47.5 倍，成为可嵌入 nightly 或每次提交的 CI/CD 语义质量门禁的可行方案；同时贡献 6 万余对正样本的 HWPPs 数据集，并验证了 DOM 侧文本与视觉属性的价值。局限包括启发式权重对不同网页布局未必普适、数据集偏结构化领域（对高度非结构化/WebGL 界面泛化未验证）、“语义相关性”本身主观。后续方向：引入登录页分类器与视觉-语言模型（VLM）做多模态校验、显式裁剪 Side-Text 窗口至 k=3 以提效、以图神经网络建模站点级语义流而非孤立的链接对、硬负样本挖掘以重启 Triplet 约束，并发布扩展期刊版代码。

Abstract: Web applications rely heavily on hyperlinks to connect disparate information resources. However, the dynamic nature of the web leads to link rot, where targets become unavailable, and more insidiously, semantic drift, where a valid HTTP 200 connection exists, but the target content no longer aligns with the source context. Traditional verification tools, which primarily function as crash oracles by checking HTTP status codes, often fail to detect semantic inconsistencies, thereby compromising web integrity and user experience. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer semantic understanding, they suffer from high latency, privacy concerns, and prohibitive costs for large-scale regression testing. In this paper, we propose SemLink, a novel automated test oracle for semantic hyperlink verification. SemLink leverages a Siamese Neural Network architecture powered by a pre-trained Sentence-BERT (SBERT) backbone to compute the semantic coherence between a hyperlink's source context (anchor text, surrounding DOM elements, and visual features) and its target page content. To train and evaluate our model, we introduce the Hyperlink-Webpage Positive Pairs (HWPPs) dataset, a rigorously constructed corpus of over 60,000 semantic pairs. Our evaluation demonstrates that SemLink achieves a Recall of 96.00%, comparable to state-of-the-art LLMs (GPT-5.2), while operating approximately 47.5 times faster and requiring significantly fewer computational resources. This work bridges the gap between traditional syntactic checkers and expensive generative AI, offering a robust and efficient solution for automated web quality assurance.

</details>


### [286] [An End-to-End Approach for Fixing Concurrency Bugs via SHB-Based Context Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05753)
*Zhuang Li,Qiuping Yi,Keyang Xiao,Zongcheng Ji,Hongliang Liang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ConFixAgent是一个由大语言模型驱动的智能体，能够端到端地修复各类并发Bug，无需任何先验Bug信息即可工作，通过静态 Happens-Before 图提取Bug相关上下文，显著优于现有最先进工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多核处理器和分布式系统的兴起，并发编程变得必不可少但极具挑战性，主要原因是线程执行的非确定性。手动修复并发Bug既耗时又容易出错。现有工具大多依赖Bug相关信息已可获取的假设，这在实际场景中往往不切实际。

Method: 提出一种专为并发Bug修复设计的上下文提取方法，利用静态 Happens-Before 图识别与Bug相关的代码区域，使系统能够在无需任何先验Bug信息的情况下端到端运行。

Result: ConFixAgent在多种类型的并发Bug修复任务中显著优于现有最先进工具，其上下文提取方法大幅提升了LLM生成修复方案的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过静态 Happens-Before 图引导的上下文提取方法，ConFixAgent有效解决了端到端并发Bug自动修复的核心挑战，为实际场景中的并发Bug修复提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rise of multi-core processors and distributed systems, concurrent programming has become essential yet challenging, primarily due to the non-deterministic nature of thread execution. Manually addressing concurrency bugs is time-consuming and error-prone. Automated Program Repair techniques provide a promising solution. However, developing an end-to-end concurrency bug repair tool is particularly challenging. Most existing tools rely on the assumption that bug-related information is readily available or that concurrency bug contexts are ideally extracted, which is often impractical in real-world scenarios. This paper introduces ConFixAgent, an LLM-driven agent capable of fixing various types of concurrency bugs in an end-to-end manner, eliminating the need for any prior bug-related information. Specifically, we propose a novel context extraction approach designed for concurrency bug repair, utilizing Static Happens-Before Graphs to identify bug-relevant sections.We implemented ConFixAgent and evaluated it across multiple benchmark sets. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that ConFixAgent significantly outperforms state-of-the-art tools in addressing diverse types of concurrency bugs, with its context extraction method markedly enhancing the accuracy of LLM-generated repair solutions.

</details>


### [287] [CAKE: Cloud Architecture Knowledge Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05755)
*Tim Lukas Adam,Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Riccardo Terrenzi,Florian Girardo Lukas,Rahime Yilmaz,Krzysztof Sierszecki,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAKE基准，用于评估LLM对云原生软件架构的理解能力，包含188个专家验证问题。实验表明模型规模超过3B参数后MCQ性能趋于平稳，而自由响应任务仍能持续区分模型能力，评估格式的选择显著影响对LLM架构知识的测量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏评估LLM对云原生软件架构真实理解能力的基准测试，现有评估无法全面衡量LLM作为软件架构协作者的效能。

Method: 使用22种模型配置(0.5B-70B参数)进行评估，采用选择题三次投票和自由响应LLM评判两种评分方式，涵盖Bloom修订分类法的四个认知层级和五个云原生主题。

Result: 1) MCQ准确率在3B参数以上趋于平稳，最佳模型达99.2%；2) 自由响应分数在各认知层级稳定增长；3) 两种格式捕获不同知识维度，MCQ趋近天花板而自由响应仍能区分模型；4) 推理增强提升自由响应质量，工具增强对小型模型有负面影响。

Conclusion: CAKE benchmark揭示了评估格式对LLM架构知识测量的根本性影响，推理增强(+think)提升自由响应质量，工具增强(+tool)对小型模型有负面影响。

Abstract: In today's software architecture, large language models (LLMs) serve as software architecture co-pilots. However, no benchmark currently exists to evaluate large language models' actual understanding of cloud-native software architecture. For this reason we present a benchmark called CAKE, which consists of 188 expert-validated questions covering four cognitive levels of Bloom's revised taxonomy -- recall, analyze, design, and implement -- and five cloud-native topics. Evaluation is conducted on 22 model configurations (0.5B--70B parameters) across four LLM families, using three-run majority voting for multiple-choice questions (MCQs) and LLM-as-a-judge scoring for free-responses (FR). Based on this evaluation, four notable findings were identified. First, MCQ accuracy plateaus above 3B parameters, with the best model reaching 99.2\%. Second, free-response scores scale steadily across all cognitive levels. Third, the two formats capture different facets of knowledge, as the MCQ accuracy approaches a ceiling while free-responses continue to differentiate models. Finally, reasoning augmentation (+think) improves free-response quality, while tool augmentation (+tool) degrades performance for small models. These results suggest that the evaluation format fundamentally shapes how we measure architectural knowledge in LLMs.

</details>


### [288] [An Empirical Study of Perceptions of General LLMs and Multimodal LLMs on Hugging Face](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05782)
*Yujian Liu,Xiao Yu,Jacky Keung,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究在Hugging Face平台上收集662条讨论线程（来自38个模型），分析用户对大语言模型（GLLM和MLLM）的真实感知，发现访问壁垒、生成质量、部署复杂性是主要痛点，为改进LLM生态系统提供可行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖问卷或单一平台数据（如Reddit/GitHub），存在预设问题限制或过度聚焦故障调试的问题，无法全面反映用户在实践中的多样化、经验驱动的跨模型视角。

Method: 在Hugging Face平台手动标注662条讨论线程，涵盖21个GLLM和17个MLLM，构建三级分类体系进行系统性用户关切分析。

Result: 用户最突出的关切包括：LLM访问壁垒、生成质量、部署与调用复杂性；次要问题涉及文档限制和资源约束。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为LLM生态系统的改进提供可操作的实践建议。
