Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have rapidly evolved from general-purpose systems to multimodal models capable of processing text, images, and audio. As both general-purpose LLMs (GLLMs) and multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) gain widespread adoption, understanding user perceptions in real-world settings becomes increasingly important. However, existing studies often rely on surveys or platform-specific data (e.g., Reddit or GitHub issues), which either constrain user feedback through predefined questions or overemphasize failure-driven, debugging-oriented discussions, thus failing to capture diverse, experience-driven, and cross-model user perspectives in practice. To address this issue, we conduct an empirical study of user discussions on Hugging Face, a major model hub with diverse models and active communities. We collect and manually annotate 662 discussion threads from 38 representative models (21 GLLMs and 17 MLLMs), and develop a three-level taxonomy to systematically characterize user concerns. Our analysis reveals that LLM access barriers, generation quality, and deployment and invocation complexity are the most prominent concerns, alongside issues such as documentation limitations and resource constraints. Based on these findings, we derive actionable implications for improving LLM ecosystem.

</details>


### [289] [Reinforcement Learning with Negative Tests as Completeness Signal for Formal Specification Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05820)
*Zhechong Huang,Zhao Zhang,Zeyu Sun,Huifeng Sun,Yingfei Xiong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecRL使用强化学习框架在Dafny中合成程序规范，通过生成"负测试用例"（程序无法产生的输入-输出对）来衡量规范完整性，从而改进规范强度和验证成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范综合任务对于模块化推理和代码库复用验证至关重要，但现有验证器反馈存在根本性缺陷：验证通过只能证明声音性，无法区分弱规范和强规范，缺乏规范完整性的细粒度信号。

Method: 提出SpecRL框架，包含自包含流水线：生成负测试用例（即程序无法产生的输入-输出对），并利用候选规范拒绝这些负测试的比例作为规范完整性的信号，集成到RL训练的奖励中。实验覆盖四种模型规模。

Result: SpecRL在规范强度和验证成功率上均优于SFT和二元规范强度奖励的RL方法，能够泛化到分布外基准测试，且相比更大的通用LLM仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 负测试用例可作为规范完整性的有效信号；SpecRL为程序规范综合提供了一种基于细粒度反馈的强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: The specification synthesis task aims to automatically generate specifications, together with any necessary auxiliary verification annotations, for existing programs. This task is important because such specifications serve as behavioral contracts that support modular reasoning and reusable verification across a codebase. At the same time, it remains challenging because verifier-only feedback is fundamentally incomplete: passing verification establishes soundness, but cannot distinguish weak specifications from strong ones. What is missing is a fine-grained signal for specification completeness.
  We present SpecRL, a reinforcement learning framework for specification synthesis in Dafny. SpecRL introduces a self-contained pipeline that generates negative tests, i.e., input-output pairs that can never be produced by the program. We use the fraction of these negative tests rejected by a candidate specification as a signal of specification completeness, which is integrated into the reward for RL training. Experiments across four model sizes show that SpecRL improves both specification strength and verification success over SFT and RL with a binary specification-strength reward, generalizes to an out-of-distribution benchmark, and remains competitive on that unseen benchmark compared to much larger general-purpose LLMs.

</details>


### [290] [Proof of Concept as a First-Class Architectural Decision Instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05835)
*Bruno Fernando Antognolli,Fabio Petrillo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述研究软件工程中的概念验证（PoC），发现PoC虽然应用广泛但在学术和工业界都缺乏清晰定义和方法论。研究提出了精炼的PoC定义和轻量级三阶段框架（规划、执行、决策），并识别出"未记录的建筑实验"反模式，强调将PoC提升为一级架构决策工具的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念验证（PoC）在软件工程中广泛采用，但无论在学术界还是工业界都存在概念定义模糊、方法论不够系统的问题，其定义和实施方法缺乏清晰性和一致性。

Method: 对学术文献和灰色文献进行系统综述，识别PoC的关键特征、相关流程，并通过综合不同来源的见解来构建框架。

Result: 识别了PoC的关键特征和流程；发现学术文献描述了PoC结果但很少涉及其过程；提出了精炼的PoC定义和轻量级三阶段框架（规划、执行、决策）；引入了"未记录的建筑实验"反模式，当PoC影响高影响力架构决策却未留下持久架构知识时会产生此反模式。

Conclusion: 将PoC提升为一级架构决策工具可以提高决策质量、增强可追溯性，并支持架构实践中的更系统性学习。

Abstract: Proofs of Concept (PoCs) are widely adopted practices in software engineering. Despite their relevance, PoCs remain conceptually underdefined and methodologically ad hoc in both research and industry, with definitions and implementation approaches that often lack clarity and consistency. This paper investigates the concept of PoCs with two complementary goals: (1) to provide a refined definition and astructured framework for PoC development grounded in a systematic review of academic and grey literature; and (2) to position PoCs as first-class architectural decision instruments rather than informal experiments or disposable artifacts. Through a systematic review of academic and grey literature we identify the key characteristics, processes, associated with PoCs and expose a significant gap the academic literature describes PoC outcomes but rarely its process. By synthesizing insights from diverse sources we propose a refined definition and a lightweight, three-phase framework (planning, execution, decision-making) that encompasses technical validation and explicit decision traceability. We also introduce the Undocumented Architectural Experiment anti-pattern, arising when PoCs influence high-impact architectural decisions without leaving durable architectural knowledge. We argue that elevating PoCs to first-class status improves decision quality, enhances traceability, and supports more systematic learning in architectural practice.

</details>


### [291] [Evolutionary Optimization of AI-Collapsed Software Development Stacks: Labor Tipping Points and Workforce Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05948)
*Matthew H. Kilbane*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个量化框架，通过形式化基线和AI崩溃劳动力模型，结合NSGAII多目标进化优化，确定软件团队中安全削减人力的"临界点"，实现成本降低同时保持质量和稳定工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具（如代码生成助手）在软件开发中的应用日益广泛，企业面临如何科学平衡人力成本削减与项目质量、团队稳定性的挑战，缺乏系统性的量化决策框架。

Method: 1) 形式化基线劳动力模型和AI崩溃劳动力模型；2) 推导安全削减人力的临界点方程；3) 将临界点约束嵌入NSGAII多目标进化优化框架，同时优化成本、质量和工作负载稳定性。

Result: NSGAII实验揭示了可复现的、分阶段的自动化策略，能够在保持质量和工作负载稳定性的前提下降低人力成本。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI辅助软件开发的劳动力配置决策提供了可量化的科学依据，具有向其他劳动类别迁移的通用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a quantitative framework for optimizing human AI workforce allocation in software development, translatable to other labor categories. I formalize baseline and AI-collapsed labor models, derive tipping point equations for safe headcount reduction, and embed them in a multi objective evolutionary optimization setup. NSGAII experiments reveal reproducible, phase specific automation strategies that reduce cost while maintaining quality and stable workloads.

</details>


### [292] [Does Pass Rate Tell the Whole Story? Evaluating Design Constraint Compliance in LLM-based Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05955)
*Kai Yu,Zhenhao Zhou,Junhao Zeng,Ying Wang,Xueying Du,Zhiqiang Yuan,Junwei Liu,Ziyu Zhou,Yujia Wang,Chong Wang,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"设计感知的问题修复（design-aware issue resolution）"范式与基准SWE-Shield：从真实PR的代码评审讨论中挖掘并验证项目级隐式设计约束，再通过LLM-as-a-Judge补丁验证器自动检查补丁合规性；最终得到495个issue、1787条人工验证约束（源自6个仓库，对齐SWE-bench-Verified/Pro）。实验显示测试通过率严重高估补丁质量——通过测试的修复中不足一半完全满足设计约束，功能正确性与设计满足之间统计关联近乎为零（Cramer's V ≤ 0.11），即使给出设计约束提示，违规率仍高于30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级问题修复基准（SWE-bench、SWE-bench Pro、SWE-Lancer等）几乎仅以测试用例通过率（Pass Rate）衡量智能体成功，但真实开发中补丁能否被合并还取决于架构约定、错误处理策略、API一致性、可维护性等"设计约束"。这类约束既难被测试覆盖，也很少被显式文档化，多隐含在PR的代码评审讨论中，且存在两个关键难点：(1) 设计知识呈"纠缠（tangling）与散落（scattering）"状态——单条评论可能同时涉及性能、模块化、API一致性，同一约束又分散在多条评论/PR中；(2) 约束是"不可执行、上下文相关"的，无法用预定义ground-truth的oracle方式验证，需基于语义推理判定。文中以Django一个数据库重复查询issue为例：Live-SWE-agent配合Gemini 3 Pro生成的distinct()补丁通过全部SWE-bench测试，但该项目历史评审明确因PostgreSQL兼容性与向后不兼容风险否决了该方案，说明仅凭测试判定成功会掩盖真实不可接受的补丁。

Method: 基准构建（多阶段流水线）：(1) DesignHunter——LLM两阶段设计约束抽取。第一阶段对PR代码评审线程做滑窗（w=6，步长=s=w）分析，抽取"原子设计建议"，并结合初始补丁与最终补丁的diff做基于行的代码对应追踪 + LLM语义校验判断建议是否被采纳（未采纳者保留为备选设计选项的证据来源）；第二阶段以"问题描述权重0.8 + 建议文本0.2"的sentence-transformer语义相似度与结构性依赖（同线程1.0/同评审0.7/同PR0.3，同文件或时间邻近+0.2）加权融合（ws=0.8, wt=0.2），用层次聚类（阈值τ=0.6）成树，再后序遍历由LLM逐层合并/拆分合成结构化设计约束（问题描述 + 多个设计选项，每个选项含选项描述、适用条件、参考代码片段，并保持溯源）。共抽取10,885条约束。(2) Issue–约束关联：Channel A通过建议ID倒排索引做显式溯源（高精度）；Channel B用LLM从diff提取"变更意图"，与约束表示在共享嵌入空间做余弦相似度最大匹配。(3) LLM-as-Judge补丁验证器：输入issue上下文、约束选项与补丁，先判定适用性再输出Satisfied/Violated/Neutral三态，3个LLM并行投票（≥2票一致才判定Satisfied/Violated），输出JSON含推理与置信度。(4) 人工验证：约束抽取可靠性（Cohen's κ=0.74，90.4%有效）、关联标注（κ=0.7783，保留1787条）、验证器人机一致性（80.8%，κ=0.7934；三模型内部一致率95.25%）。评测指标：Pass Rate、DSR（所有适用约束均满足）、DVR（违反至少一条）、DNR（无适用约束），并按P/F×S/V正交分解为P&S、P&V、F&S、F&V；用χ²独立性检验与Cramer's V衡量关联。实验对象为SWE-agent、Live-SWE-agent、Lingxi-v1.5、Sonar Foundation Agent，底座模型含Kimi-K2、GPT-5、Claude-Sonnet-4.5、Gemini-2.5/3.0-Pro；RQ4中向智能体提供issue特定约束提示让其迭代精修补丁。

Result: RQ1：SWE-Shield_pro上Pass Rate为13.44%–42.69%，SWE-Shield_verified为70.25%–76.95%，但DSR仅39.13%–50.20%与32.64%–42.80%，DVR高达36.21%–45.85%，P&S远低于Pass（如SWE-agent/Claude-Sonnet-4.5在pro上42.69%→22.53%），说明设计满足是与功能正确性正交且基本未解的维度。RQ2：功能正确性与设计满足关联可忽略（Cramér's V ≤ 0.1157，多数设置χ²检验p>0.05），大量"通过测试但违反约束"的补丁集中在安全检查、错误处理协议与可维护性边界等高影响约束上。RQ3：同一swe-agent框架下四模型DSR仅在约12个百分点内波动（Pass Rate差异却高达13.83%–43.87%）；被违反约束并集377条，其中35条（9.3%）为所有模型共同违反的"共享核心"，最大模型特有集合48条（12.7%），体现系统性盲区与模型特有盲点（如Django #13410应只捕获BlockingIOError而模型一律捕获OSError）。RQ4：提供设计约束提示后DVR一致下降（最多降6.35个百分点，Claude-Sonnet-4.5由37.15%降至30.80%，Gemini-2.5-Pro由39.92%降至35.27%），但残余违规率仍>30%，且除GPT-5外各模型Pass Rate反而回退，显示设计合规与功能正确存在权衡（如Ansible #50909中未优先复用jinja2的min/max实现）。

Conclusion: 测试通过率显著高估补丁质量，仅凭功能正确性评估LLM智能体的真实可用性不足，评测应从"test-passing"转向"design-respecting"。SWE-Shield使隐式、项目原生的设计约束变得显式、可溯源、可度量，支持对设计失败做细粒度诊断，也揭示当前模型难以将隐含设计理由"操作化"到补丁生成中：即便显式给出约束，模型仍存在系统性误解、部分遵循与以牺牲功能为代价的倾向。未来方向包括：面向大型代码库的可扩展约束抽取（层次聚合、增量抽取、选择性采样）、评估并改进既有"gold patch"本身的设计合规性、用静态检查器/类型推理/模型检查等形式化验证补强LLM评判、以及设计感知的修复技术（结合约束语义推理与结构化知识表示）。论文亦坦承局限：设计满足度为近似度量、只覆盖2个基准/6个仓库与有限智能体、LLM同时承担抽取与评判角色带来内部效度风险。

Abstract: Repository-level issue resolution benchmarks have become a standard testbed for evaluating LLM-based agents, yet success is still predominantly measured by test pass rates. In practice, however, acceptable patches must also comply with project-specific design constraints, such as architectural conventions, error-handling policies, and maintainability requirements, which are rarely encoded in tests and are often documented only implicitly in code review discussions. This paper introduces \textit{design-aware issue resolution} and presents \bench{}, a benchmark that makes such implicit design constraints explicit and measurable. \bench{} is constructed by mining and validating design constraints from real-world pull requests, linking them to issue instances, and automatically checking patch compliance using an LLM-based verifier, yielding 495 issues and 1,787 validated constraints across six repositories, aligned with SWE-bench-Verified and SWE-bench-Pro. Experiments with state-of-the-art agents show that test-based correctness substantially overestimates patch quality: fewer than half of resolved issues are fully design-satisfying, design violations are widespread, and functional correctness exhibits negligible statistical association with design satisfaction. While providing issue-specific design guidance reduces violations, substantial non-compliance remains, highlighting a fundamental gap in current agent capabilities and motivating design-aware evaluation beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [293] [QiMeng-PRepair: Precise Code Repair via Edit-Aware Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05963)
*Changxin Ke,Rui Zhang,Jiaming Guo,Yuanbo Wen,Li Ding,Shuo Wang,Xuyuan Zhu,Xiong Peng,Di Huang,Zidong Du,Xing Hu,Qi Guo,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在程序修复中的"过度编辑"问题，提出精确修复任务和PRepair框架，通过自破坏生成多样化buggy程序，并使用编辑感知的组相对策略优化训练模型，实现更精确的代码修复，修复精度提升31.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在程序修复中常出现过度编辑问题，过多的代码修改覆盖正确代码，阻碍bug定位，导致修复效率低下。

Method: 提出精确修复任务概念；构建PRepair框架包含两部分：(1) Self-Breaking通过受控bug注入和min-max采样生成多样化buggy程序；(2) Self-Repairing使用编辑感知组相对策略优化(EA-GRPO)训练，编辑感知奖励鼓励最小但正确的编辑。

Result: 在fix_1@1指标下修复精度提升31.4%；结合推测编辑显著提高解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: PRepair有效缓解过度编辑问题，在修复精度和效率上均表现优异，为精确实用的代码修复提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong program repair performance but often suffer from over-editing, where excessive modifications overwrite correct code and hinder bug localization. We systematically quantify its impact and introduce precise repair task, which maximizes reuse of correct code while fixing only buggy parts. Building on this insight, we propose PRepair, a framework that mitigates over-editing and improves repair accuracy. PRepair has two components: Self-Breaking, which generates diverse buggy programs via controlled bug injection and min-max sampling, and Self-Repairing, which trains models with Edit-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization (EA-GRPO) using an edit-aware reward to encourage minimal yet correct edits. Experiments show that PRepair improves repair precision by up to 31.4% under $\mathrm{fix}_1@1$, a metric that jointly considers repair correctness and extent, and significantly increases decoding throughput when combined with speculative editing, demonstrating its potential for precise and practical code repair.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [294] [Analyzing Persistent Alltoallv RMA Implementations for High-Performance MPI Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05099)
*Evelyn Namugwanya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对HPC应用中的MPI_Alltoallv集体通信操作，设计了基于fence和lock同步的持久化MPI RMA变体，通过将一次性初始化阶段与每次迭代执行分离，实现通信元数据和窗口状态的复用。实验表明fence持久化变体在大消息场景下性能提升显著，最高可达44%运行时降低，448进程下从2.49s降至1.54s（快38%）。盈亏平衡点为32,768字节，适用于大消息工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPI_Alltoallv等集体通信操作是许多HPC应用的核心，尤其对于不规则消息大小的应用。现有的非持久化实现需要在每次迭代中重复处理通信元数据，导致性能开销。通过将初始化阶段与执行阶段分离，实现元数据和窗口状态的复用，有望显著提升重复通信场景的性能。

Method: 设计并实现了两种持久化MPI RMA Alltoallv变体：基于fence同步和基于lock同步的方案。关键创新在于将通信元数据创建和窗口状态配置分离到一次性初始化阶段，使后续迭代可直接复用这些数据结构。实验在LLNL Dane超级计算机上进行，包含消息大小扫描、不规则稀疏通信模式测试，以及fence与lock方案的分层扩展比较。

Result: Fence持久化变体在大消息场景下始终优于非持久化基线，最高实现44%运行时降低。在448进程规模下，运行时间从2.49s降至1.54s（提速38%），且可扩展性随进程数增加而改善。盈亏平衡分析表明，当消息大小≥32,768字节时持久化立即产生收益；小消息因元数据摊销成本限制，收益有限。

Conclusion: 持久化RMA Alltoallv对于大消息工作负载是实用方案，消除了重复元数据处理开销后，运行时间主要由数据移动主导（表现为消息越大节省越多）。研究明确了现代HPC系统上fence同步与lock同步的权衡取舍，为高性能计算应用的通信优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Collective communication operations such as MPI_Alltoallv are central to many HPC applications, particularly those with irregular message sizes. We design, implement, and evaluate persistent MPI RMA variants of Alltoallv based on fence and lock synchronization, separating a one time initialization phase from per iteration execution to enable reuse of communication metadata and window state across repeated epochs.
  Our benchmarks tested on LLNL's Dane supercomputer show that the fence-persistent variant consistently outperforms the non-persistent baseline for large message sizes, achieving up to 44% reduction in runtime and improving scalability with increasing process counts; at 448 processes the runtime decreases from 2.49s to 1.54s (38% faster). We further evaluate the algorithms under irregular sparse communication patterns and compare fence- and lock-based designs, including hierarchical extensions.
  Message-size sweeps and a break-even model demonstrate that persistence provides immediate payoff for messages greater or equal to 32,768 bytes, while smaller messages show limited benefit due to metadata amortization costs. These results indicate that persistent RMA Alltoallv is a practical approach for workloads with large messages, where removing repeated metadata processing leaves runtime dominated by data movement, as evidenced by the increasing time savings with message size, and they clarify the trade-offs between fence and lock synchronization on modern HPC systems.

</details>


### [295] [Optimizing OpenFaaS on Kubernetes: Comparative Analysis of Language Runtimes and Cluster Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05496)
*Ehsan Ataie,Mohammadreza Pooshani,Hossein Aqasizade*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在CloudLab平台上评估了OpenFaaS在四种Kubernetes发行版（Kubeadm、K3s、MicroK8s、K0s）上的性能表现，并比较了Python、Go和Node.js三种编程语言开发函数时的性能差异。研究表明Go在吞吐量和CPU使用率方面优于其他语言；K3s和Kubeadm整体性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless FaaS平台虽已广泛部署，但其在不同Kubernetes发行版上的兼容性与性能表现缺乏系统性对比研究，且编程语言对平台性能的影响尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 实验基于CloudLab基础设施构建，使用Kubeadm、K3s、MicroK8s和K0s四种Kubernetes发行版部署OpenFaaS，采用Python、Go和Node.js开发函数，在不同并发调用等级下测量吞吐量、CPU使用率和延迟等指标。

Result: Go语言在吞吐量和CPU使用率上始终优于Python和Node.js；K3s和Kubeadm表现最优，其中Kubeadm延迟低且CPU效率高，K3s吞吐量更高。

Conclusion: Go是Serverless应用的最佳运行时选择；K3s和Kubeadm是部署Kubernetes环境下OpenFaaS的推荐方案。研究为优化OpenFaaS平台部署提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Serverless computing, particularly Function-as-a-Service (FaaS), has revolutionized cloud computing by abstracting infrastructure management and enabling dynamic resource allocation. This paper examines the performance and compatibility of OpenFaaS, an open-source serverless platform, when deployed on various Kubernetes distributions, including Kubeadm, K3s, MicroK8s, and K0s. Moreover, leveraging the CloudLab infrastructure, this study examines the impact of Python, Go, and Node.js programming languages on the performance of Kubernetes-enabled OpenFaaS, specifically when these languages are used to develop functions deployed on the platform. The performance is evaluated and analyzed under various levels of concurrent invocations using several usage-level metrics, such as throughput and CPU usage, as well as responsiveness metrics, such as delay. According to our findings, Go consistently outperforms Python and Node.js in terms of throughput and CPU usage, making it the ideal runtime for serverless applications. Among the Kubernetes distributions, K3s and Kubeadm exhibit superior performance, with Kubeadm maintaining low latency and efficient CPU usage, and K3s demonstrating high throughput. This study provides valuable insights into optimizing the Kubernetes-enabled OpenFaaS platform, highlighting the strengths and trade-offs of different Kubernetes distributions and language runtimes.

</details>


### [296] [Communication Requirements for Linearizable Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05862)
*Raïssa Nataf,Yoram Moses*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在异步消息传递系统中，线性化原子寄存器的实现必须产生大量的消息链。研究表明，由于进程无法确定操作的精确时序和实时顺序，消息链成为确保线性化的必要手段，且所有类型的操作都需要消息链连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性化是分布式系统的基本正确性条件，但确保实现的线性化非常复杂。在异步系统中，进程无法确定操作的精确时序和实时顺序，只能依赖消息发送与接收的顺序关系。本文旨在研究异步消息传递系统中线性化原子寄存器实现的通信需求。

Method: 首先证明两个通用定理，将消息链与异步系统执行中操作延迟和重排序的能力联系起来；然后应用这些定理分析线性化寄存器实现的通信模式。

Result: 证明线性化寄存器实现必须在所有类型的操作之间创建大量的消息链连接。

Conclusion: 结果表明异步系统中的线性化实现必然是昂贵的且非平凡的，并揭示了线性化实现的基本结构特征。

Abstract: While linearizability is a fundamental correctness condition for distributed systems, ensuring the linearizability of implementations can be quite complex. An essential aspect of linearizable implementations of concurrent objects is the need to preserve the real-time order of operations. In many settings, however, processes cannot determine the precise timing and relative real-time ordering of operations. Indeed, in an asynchronous system, the only ordering information available to them is based on the fact that sending a message precedes its delivery. We show that as a result, message chains must be used extensively to ensure linearizability. This paper studies the communication requirements of linearizable implementations of atomic registers in asynchronous message passing systems. We start by proving two general theorems that relate message chains to the ability to delay and reorder actions and operations in an execution of an asynchronous system, without the changes being noticeable to the processes. These are then used to prove that linearizable register implementations must create extensive message chains among operations of \emph{all} types. In particular, our results imply that linearizable implementations in asynchronous systems are necessarily costly and nontrivial, and provide insight into their structure.

</details>


### [297] [JZ-Tree: GPU friendly neighbour search and friends-of-friends with dual tree walks in JAX plus CUDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05885)
*Jens Stücker,Oliver Hahn,Lukas Winkler,Adrian Gutierrez Adame,Thomas Flöss*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对GPU架构优化的Morton（z-order）平面树层次结构，解决空间树算法在GPU上因线程分化和不规则内存访问导致的性能瓶颈问题。基于该框架实现了k近邻搜索和Friends-of-Friends聚类算法，在大规模数据（N≥10^7）上实现超过10倍的性能提升，并支持多GPU分布式扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间树遍历算法在CPU高性能计算中高效灵活，但直接迁移到GPU架构时，由于树算法的分支特性和不规则内存访问模式导致严重的线程分化和低效内存访问，无法充分发挥GPU高计算吞吐量的优势。

Method: 提出Morton（z-order）"平面树层次结构"：采用扁平化数据布局实现高效双向树遍历，支持线程组协作执行，获得高度合并的内存访问模式。基于该框架实现两个重要空间算法：精确k近邻搜索和FoF聚类。

Result: 大规模问题规模（N≥10^7）下，性能提升超过一个数量级，支持分布式多GPU系统的强扩展性。

Conclusion: 开源实现'JZ-Tree'（JAX z-order tree），为广泛的树基算法提供高效GPU实现基础。

Abstract: Algorithms based on spatial tree traversal are widely regarded as among the most efficient and flexible approaches for many problems in CPU-based high-performance computing (HPC). However, directly transferring these algorithms to GPU architectures often yields substantially smaller performance gains than expected in light of the high computational throughput of modern GPUs. The branching nature of tree algorithms leads to thread divergence and irregular memory access patterns -- both of which may severely limit GPU performance. To address these challenges, we propose a Morton (z-order) 'plane-based tree hierarchy' that is specifically designed for GPU architectures. The resulting flattened data layout enables efficient dual-tree traversal with collaborative execution across thread groups, leading to highly coalesced memory access patterns. Based on this framework we present implementations of two important spatial algorithms -- exact $k$-nearest neighbour search and friends-of-friends (FoF) clustering. For both cases, we observe more than an order-of-magnitude performance improvement over the closest competing GPU libraries for large problem sizes ($N \gtrsim 10^7$), together with strong scaling to distributed multi-GPU systems. We provide an open-source implementation, 'JZ-Tree' (JAX z-order tree), which serves as a foundation for efficient GPU implementations of a broad class of tree-based algorithms.

</details>


### [298] [GTaP: A GPU-Resident Fork-Join Task-Parallel Runtime with a Pragma-Based Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05982)
*Yuki Maeda,Kenjiro Taura*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GTaP是一个GPU-resident运行时，支持fork-join任务并行。它基于persistent kernel模型，提供thread block和thread两级worker粒度，使用continuation表示join，state machine执行任务，扩展Clang前端支持pragma编程模型，采用work stealing负载均衡和EPAQ减少warp divergence。在不规则应用上优于72核CPU上的OpenMP和naive GPU方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU擅长规则的数据并行负载，但不规则应用通常表现为fork-join结构的任务并行。现有CPU上的fork-join运行时已成熟，但在GPU上高效支持仍具挑战性。

Method: 基于persistent kernel模型，支持thread block和thread两级worker粒度；用continuation表示join，将每个task实现为可分割的state machine；扩展Clang前端添加pragma编程模型；采用work stealing负载均衡；对thread-level worker引入EPAQ按用户定义标准分区任务队列以减少warp divergence。

Result: 在代表性不规则应用上，GTaP在计算密集型任务和大数据量场景下优于72核CPU上的OpenMP。EPAQ效果因工作负载而异，在Fibonacci上可达1.8倍加速。

Conclusion: GTaP成功将fork-join任务并行引入GPU，其设计选择优于naive GPU替代方案，为GPU上的不规则应用提供了高效的运行时支持。

Abstract: Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) excel at regular data-parallel workloads where massive hardware parallelism can be readily exploited. In contrast, many important irregular applications are naturally expressed as task parallelism with a fork-join control structure. While CPU runtimes for fork-join task parallelism are mature, it remains challenging to efficiently support it on GPUs.
  We propose GTaP, a GPU-resident runtime that supports fork-join task parallelism. GTaP is based on the persistent kernel model, and supports two worker granularities: thread blocks and individual threads. To realize fork-join on GPUs, GTaP represents joins as continuations and executes each task as a state machine that can be split into multiple execution segments. We also extend Clang's frontend with a pragma-based programming model that enables programmers to express fork-join without exposing low-level mechanisms. GTaP employs work stealing for load balancing, providing better scalability than a global-queue approach. For thread-level workers, we further introduce Execution-Path-Aware Queueing (EPAQ), which allows programmers to partition task queues using user-defined criteria, reducing warp divergence caused by mixing heterogeneous control flows within a warp.
  Across representative irregular applications, GTaP outperforms OpenMP task-parallel execution on a 72-core CPU in many cases, especially for large problem sizes with compute-intensive tasks. We also show that GTaP's design choices outperform naive GPU alternatives. The benefit of EPAQ is workload-dependent: it can improve performance for some benchmarks while having little effect on others; on Fibonacci, EPAQ achieves up to a 1.8$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [299] [CoStream: Codec-Guided Resource-Efficient System for Video Streaming Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06036)
*Yulin Zou,Yan Chen,Wenyan Chen,JooYoung Park,Shivaraman Nitin,Luo Tao,Francisco Romero,Dmitrii Ustiugov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CoStream是一个基于编解码器引导的流式视频分析系统，利用视频编解码器提取的元数据作为低开销运行时信号，统一优化视频解码、视觉处理和LLM预填充，相比现有方法实现3倍吞吐量提升和87%的GPU计算减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流分析是视觉-语言模型服务的重要工作负载，但多模态推理的高成本限制了可扩展性。现有系统通过利用视频流中的时间和空间冗余来降低推理成本，但它们要么针对ViT，要么针对LLM，视角有限，未能充分利用端到端优化机会。此外，现有方法在识别冗余时会产生显著开销，无论是通过离线分析和训练还是昂贵的在线计算，都不适合动态实时流场景。

Method: CoStream基于一个关键观察：视频编解码器在压缩过程中已经提取了每个流的时间和空间结构作为副产品。CoStream将这种编解码器元数据作为低开销运行时信号，用于：1) ViT编码前的编解码器引导补丁剪枝；2) LLM预填充期间的选择性键值缓存刷新。这两种优化都是完全在线的，不需要离线训练。

Result: 实验表明，CoStream相比最先进的基线方法实现了高达3倍的吞吐量提升和高达87%的GPU计算减少，同时保持具有竞争力的准确性，F1分数仅下降0-8%。

Conclusion: CoStream通过直接操作压缩比特流，将传输减少作为内在优势，展示了编解码器引导的优化在视频分析系统中的潜力，为视觉-语言模型的高效流式服务提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Video streaming analytics is a crucial workload for vision-language model serving, but the high cost of multimodal inference limits scalability. Prior systems reduce inference cost by exploiting temporal and spatial redundancy in video streams, but they target either the vision transformer (ViT) or the LLM with a limited view, leaving end-to-end opportunities untapped. Moreover, existing methods incur significant overhead to identify redundancy, either through offline profiling and training or costly online computation, making them ill-suited for dynamic real-time streams.
  We present CoStream, a codec-guided streaming video analytics system built on a key observation that video codecs already extract the temporal and spatial structure of each stream as a byproduct of compression. CoStream treats this codec metadata as a low-cost runtime signal to unify optimization across video decoding, visual processing, and LLM prefilling, with transmission reduction as an inherent benefit of operating directly on compressed bitstreams. This drives codec-guided patch pruning before ViT encoding and selective key-value cache refresh during LLM prefilling, both of which are fully online and do not require offline training. Experiments show that CoStream achieves up to 3x throughput improvement and up to 87% GPU compute reduction over state-of-the-art baselines, while maintaining competitive accuracy with only 0-8% F1 drop.

</details>


### [300] [Fine-Grained Power and Energy Attribution on AMD GPU/APU-Based Exascale Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06056)
*Adam McDaniel,Michael Jantz,Ashesh Sharma,Steve Abbott,Steven Martin,Shreyas Khandekar,Brandon Neth,Bruno Villasenor Alvarez,Aditya Kashi,Wael Elwasif,Oscar Hernandez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对现代百亿亿次级GPU/APU系统（AMD Instinct MI250X和MI300A）中的功率和能耗传感器差异问题，提出了一套完整的特性分析和校正方法学。通过在多达512块GPU和480块APU上使用受控方波工作负载，量化了传感器更新间隔、延迟、混叠和变异性，并将重构功率流整合到Score-P/PAPI工具中实现时间对齐的相位级归因。该方法应用于混合精度HPL基准测试，证明了不同精度级别可显著降低节点能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代百亿亿次级GPU和APU系统配备了多种功率和能耗传感器，但这些传感器在范围、更新频率、时序和滤波等方面存在差异，导致短时加速器活动的归因变得复杂，亟需系统性方法来解决传感器特性差异对精确功耗分析的影响。

Method: 采用受控方波工作负载进行传感器特性分析；在大规模系统（最多512块GPU和480块APU）上量化更新间隔、延迟、混叠和变异性；利用片上（rocm-smi/amd-smi）和片外Cray电源管理传感器进行对比测试；通过累积能耗计数器重构功率以获得更快响应时间；将校正后的数据流集成到Score-P/PAPI工具中实现时间对齐的相位级归因。

Result: 方法成功应用于rocHPL、rocHPL-MxP和HPG-MxP基准测试。混合精度计算在Frontier系统上显著降低节点能耗：rocHPL-MxP降低79%，HPG-MxP降低31%；Portage系统上呈现类似趋势。该方法有效区分了因运行时间缩短和功率变化带来的能耗节省。

Conclusion: 本研究为当前和未来百亿亿次级系统提供了可移植的传感器验证和功率感知优化指导框架，能够支持精确的能耗分析和性能优化决策。

Abstract: Modern exascale GPU- and APU-based systems provide multiple power and energy sensors, but differences in scope, update rate, timing, and filtering complicate the attribution of short-lived accelerator activity. This paper presents a methodology to characterize and correct these effects on Cray EX systems with AMD Instinct MI250X GPUs (Frontier) and MI300A APUs (Portage). Using controlled square-wave workloads, we quantify update intervals, delay, aliasing, and variability across up to 512 GPUs and 480 APUs with on-chip (rocm-smi/amd-smi) and off-chip Cray Power Management sensors. We reconstruct power from cumulative energy counters to achieve faster response times, validate it against on-chip, off-chip, and node-level sensors, and integrate the resulting streams into a Score-P/PAPI-based tool for time-aligned, phase-level attribution. Applied to rocHPL, rocHPL-MxP, and HPG-MxP, the method separates energy savings due to reduced runtime from changes in power. Mixed precision reduces node energy on Frontier by 79% for rocHPL-MxP and 31% for HPG-MxP, with similar trends on Portage. These results provide portable guidance for sensor validation and power-aware optimization on current and future exascale systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [EduIllustrate: Towards Scalable Automated Generation Of Multimodal Educational Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05005)
*Shuzhen Bi,Mingzi Zhang,Zhuoxuan Li,Xiaolong Wang,keqian Li,Aimin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: EduIllustrate是一个用于评估LLM生成K-12 STEM多模态教学内容的基准测试，包含230道题目、5个学科、3个年级水平，以及8维评估体系和顺序锚定生成协议。Gemini 3.0 Pro Preview表现最佳(87.8%)，Kimi-K2.5成本效益最高($0.12/题)。顺序锚定策略可将视觉一致性提升13%且降低成本94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM教育能力评估主要集中于问答和辅导任务，缺乏对多模态教学内容生成能力的系统评估。关键缺口在于：LLM能否生成结合几何精确视觉与逐步推理的图文交织解释。

Method: 构建EduIllustrate基准测试：(1)230道K-12 STEM题目，涵盖5学科3年级；(2)顺序锚定生成协议确保跨图视觉一致性；(3)基于多媒体学习理论的8维评估标准；(4)对10个LLM进行评估，并进行工作流消融实验和20位专家的人类评估验证。

Result: Gemini 3.0 Pro Preview以87.8%的准确率领先；Kimi-K2.5达到80.8%且成本效益最佳($0.12/题)。顺序锚定使视觉一致性提升13%，成本降低94%。LLM作为评判者在客观维度上与人类评估高度一致(ρ≥0.83)，但在主观视觉评估上存在局限。

Conclusion: EduIllustrate填补了LLM多模态教育内容生成评估的空白，揭示了当前模型在图文一致性方面的差距，为未来教育AI系统的开发提供了标准化评估框架和成本效益分析。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as educational assistants, yet evaluation of their educational capabilities remains concentrated on question-answering and tutoring tasks. A critical gap exists for multimedia instructional content generation -- the ability to produce coherent, diagram-rich explanations that combine geometrically accurate visuals with step-by-step reasoning. We present EduIllustrate, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on interleaved text-diagram explanation generation for K-12 STEM problems. The benchmark comprises 230 problems spanning five subjects and three grade levels, a standardized generation protocol with sequential anchoring to enforce cross-diagram visual consistency, and an 8-dimension evaluation rubric grounded in multimedia learning theory covering both text and visual quality. Evaluation of ten LLMs reveals a wide performance spread: Gemini 3.0 Pro Preview leads at 87.8\%, while Kimi-K2.5 achieves the best cost-efficiency (80.8\% at \\$0.12/problem). Workflow ablation confirms sequential anchoring improves Visual Consistency by 13\% at 94\% lower cost. Human evaluation with 20 expert raters validates LLM-as-judge reliability for objective dimensions ($ρ\geq 0.83$) while revealing limitations on subjective visual assessment.

</details>


### [302] [Context Collapse: Barriers to Adoption for Generative AI in Workplace Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05151)
*Emanuel Moss,Elizabeth Watkins,Christopher Persaud,Dawn Nafus,Passant Karunaratne,Mona Sloane*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过专家访谈研究生成式AI在工作场景中如何处理用户上下文的失败及其应对策略。研究发现计算方法在处理上下文时存在根本性缺陷：多个上下文相互混淆或随时间"腐化"退化。作者呼吁从无差别的上下文数据收集转向更具交互性的实践方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI工具在工作场景中对用户上下文的处理方式未能满足用户期望和需求，需要深入理解这些失败的表现形式及其根本原因。

Method: 专家访谈（empirical expert interviews）

Result: 识别出计算式上下文处理的多个缺陷：上下文相互侵蚀或随时间退化；工具开发者、用户和社会科学家对"上下文"的概念化存在差异，导致系统性错位。

Conclusion: 建议从无差别的上下文数据收集转向更具交互性的实践方式，以更恰当地将生成式AI嵌入用户的使用情境中。

Abstract: As generative AI technologies are pressed into service in workplace settings, current approaches to account for the contexts in which such technologies are used fall short of users' expectations and needs. This paper empirically demonstrates, through expert interviews, both how these tools fail to account for users' context and how users deploy concrete strategies address such failures. The paper analyzes how context is variously conceptualized by tool developers, users, and social scientists to identify specific pitfalls inherent in computational approaches to context. Multiple distinct contexts tend to collapse into one another or rot, degrading over time, reducing the utility of any efforts to account for context. The paper concludes with a provocation to shift from an indiscriminate collection of context-relevant data toward a more interactional set of practices to embed GenAI systems more appropriately into users' contexts of use.

</details>


### [303] [A Multi-Agent Approach to Validate and Refine LLM-Generated Personalized Math Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05160)
*Fareya Ikram,Nischal Ashok Kumar,Junyang Lu,Hunter McNichols,Candace Walkington,Neil Heffernan,Andrew S. Lan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，通过生成-验证-修订的迭代流程，利用LLM为学生生成个性化数学题目。该框架包含四个专业验证智能体（可解性、现实性、可读性、真实性），在600道来自ASSISTments平台的题目上进行评估。研究发现真实性和现实性是LLM生成题目最常见的失败模式，单次修订迭代即可显著减少这些失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成的个性化数学题目存在数量不现实、上下文不合理、可读性差、真实性不足以及数学不一致等问题，需要一个系统化的方法来提升生成质量。

Method: 设计多智能体框架，将个性化过程形式化为迭代的生成-验证-修订流程，使用四个专门验证智能体分别针对可解性、现实性、可读性、真实性和数学一致性进行检验，评估三种不同的反馈协调修订策略。

Result: 真实性和现实性是个性化题目最常见的失败模式；单次修订迭代可显著减少这些失败；不同修订策略在不同评估标准上各有优势；验证器的可靠性在现实性上最高，在真实性上最低。

Conclusion: 多智能体验证-修订框架能有效提升LLM生成个性化数学题目的质量；需要更好的评估协议来考虑教师和学生的个人特征，以提升真实性验证的可靠性。

Abstract: Students benefit from math problems contextualized to their interests. Large language models (LLMs) offer promise for efficient personalization at scale. However, LLM-generated personalized problems may often have problems such as unrealistic quantities and contexts, poor readability, limited authenticity with respect to students' experiences, and occasional mathematical inconsistencies. To alleviate these problems, we propose a multi-agent framework that formalizes personalization as an iterative generate--validate--revise process; we use four specialized validator agents targeting the criteria of solvability, realism, readability, and authenticity, respectively. We evaluate our framework on 600 problems drawn from a popular online mathematics homework platform, ASSISTments, personalizing each problem to a fixed set of 20 student interest topics. We compare three refinement strategies that differ in how validation feedback is coordinated into revisions. Results show that authenticity and realism are the most frequent failure modes in initial LLM-personalized problems, but that a single refinement iteration substantially reduces these failures. We further find that different refinement strategies have different strengths on different criteria. We also assess validator reliability via human evaluation. Results show that reliability is highest on realism and lowest on authenticity, highlighting the need for better evaluation protocols that consider teachers' and students' personal characteristics.

</details>


### [304] [AI-Augmented Peer Review and Scientific Productivity: A Cross-Country Panel and SEM Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05463)
*Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究首次跨国实证验证了AI辅助同行评审系统对国家层面科学生产力的影响，通过构建AI评审能力指数(AIRC)，发现其可使科学生产力提升18-25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同行评审存在效率低下问题，但缺乏对AI整合在国家层面系统性影响的实证研究。

Method: 构建了AI评审能力指数(AIRC)，采用OECD国家面板数据，运用固定效应回归和结构方程模型(SEM)进行分析。

Result: AI辅助评审显著提升科学生产力并降低研究质量差异。AIRC每增加一个标准差，科学生产力提升18-25%，该效应通过改善评审效率和可重复性来实现。

Conclusion: 本研究为AI增强型科学评价系统提供了首个跨国实证验证，表明AI可作为知识生产的结构性驱动因素，对推进科研评价体系改革具有政策参考价值。

Abstract: This study empirically investigates the impact of AI-augmented peer review systems on scientific productivity using panel data from OECD countries. While prior research has highlighted inefficiencies in traditional peer review, little empirical work has quantified the systemic impact of AI integration at the national level. We construct a novel AI Review Capability Index (AIRC) and examine its effects on research productivity, reproducibility, and innovation output. Using fixed-effects regression and structural equation modeling (SEM), we show that AI-assisted evaluation significantly enhances productivity and reduces variance in research quality. Results indicate that a one standard deviation increase in AIRC is associated with an 18-25% increase in scientific productivity, mediated through improvements in review efficiency and reproducibility. This paper provides the first cross-country empirical validation of AI-augmented scientific evaluation systems and contributes to the emerging literature on AI as a structural driver of knowledge production.

</details>


### [305] [From Pixels to Personas: Tracking the Evolution of Anime Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05507)
*Rongze Liu,Jiaxin Pei,Jian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究利用大规模多模态动漫角色数据集，结合LLM提取的人格特征与头像特征，运用计算方法分析了动漫角色的人格原型与视觉刻板印象的历时演变规律。研究揭示了动漫目标受众从儿童向青少年/青年人群体的系统性转变，以及角色设计"萌化"趋势，发现视觉信号在塑造观众偏好方面比人格特征更具主导作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动漫文化现象研究对动漫角色如何随时间演变缺乏深入理解。尽管动漫作为文化产品在现代社会影响力巨大，尤其在年轻群体中广受欢迎，但尚未有研究系统揭示其角色设计的历时演变规律及其与受众偏好的关系。

Method: 研究团队从动漫评论网站获取大规模多模态动漫角色数据集，整合文本、视觉和生产特征信息，结合在线人气追踪数据。使用LLM提取角色人格特征，并与头像特征进行整合分析，识别反复出现的人格原型和视觉刻板印象及其跨十年期的时序演变。

Result: 研究获得四项主要发现：(1)动漫目标受众经历了从儿童向青少年/青年群体的系统性转变；(2)角色设计呈现"萌化"趋势，2000年代以来更柔和或性化的女性特征日益突出；(3)部分人格原型在视觉上具有可预测性，但观众也倾向于偏好非常规化的角色；(4)视觉信号在塑造观众偏好方面比人格特征更具主导作用，"萌"系面孔和机械设计等人格特征对人气贡献巨大。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了视觉信号（尤其是"萌"系特征和机械设计元素）在决定动漫角色流行度中的核心作用。这些发现为理解动漫文化与创意实践的更广泛动态提供了重要洞察，表明动漫产业正在经历从儿童娱乐向成熟受众市场的转型。

Abstract: Anime, originated from Japan, is one of the most influential cultural products in modern society and is especially popular among younger generations. The popularity of anime reflects important cultural evolutions in our society. Despite existing research on anime as a cultural phenomenon, we still have a limited understanding of how anime really evolves over the years. In this study, using a large-scale multimodal dataset of anime characters from an anime review site, we applied computational methods that integrate textual, visual, and production features of anime characters with online popularity traces. By combining LLM-extracted personality features with avatar features, we identify recurring personality archetypes and visual tropes with their temporal evolution over the past decades. We found that the target audience of anime has undergone a systematic shift from children to a maturing audience of teenagers and young adults over time. Character design has been undergoing moe-ification, with softer or sexualized female traits becoming increasingly prominent since the 2000s. Some personality archetypes are often visually predictable, yet audiences also tend to prefer less conventionalized characters. Finally, we reveal that visual signals play a more dominant role than personality traits in shaping audience preferences, with features such as moe-style faces and mechanical designs contributing greatly to popularity. These findings offer insights into the broader dynamics of anime's cultural and creative practices.

</details>


### [306] [Conditional Publics: Shared Events and Divergent Meanings in the European Twitter Debate on the Ukraine War](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05800)
*Corrado Monti,Arthur Capozzi,Yelena Mejova,Gianmarco De Francisci Morales*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究分析了俄乌战争前8个月来自20个欧洲国家的3800万条推文，发现欧洲各国内部普遍存在"鹰派"与"鸽派"观点集群。 polarization并非源于激进化的加剧，而是由普通用户的退出所驱动。对立阵营是否关注同一事件取决于议题性质：务实性议题下形成对抗性公共领域，而解释性议题下则形成情感性公共领域与反公共领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地缘政治危机频发，理解公众如何在社交媒体上讨论冲突具有重要意义。研究的核心问题是：欧洲公众在讨论俄乌冲突时是否共享同一个信息现实？结构性极化是如何形成的？对立观点群体是否会关注相同的事件？

Method: 研究采集了3800万条来自20个欧洲国家的带地理标记的推文，通过转发社区检测识别意见群体，并对六个议题进行立场标注以区分"鹰派"与"鸽派"立场。

Result: 研究发现：①几乎每个国家都存在鹰派与鸽派两大阵营；②极化的驱动因素是普通用户的退出而非激进化的加剧；③对立群体是否共享事件参照框架取决于议题类型——务实性议题下双方对同一高关注事件做出反应，形成"对抗性公共领域"，而解释性议题下双方构建了不同的情感性公共领域。

Conclusion: 研究提出了"条件性公共领域"(Conditional Publics)概念，用以描述其关系结构（共享或分裂参照框架）取决于辩论议题的认知性质的公共形成体。这一框架有助于理解数字时代极化并非简单的好战vs温和二元对立，而是在不同议题上呈现动态的公共领域结构。

Abstract: How do European publics debate a geopolitical crisis on social media, and do they inhabit a shared informational reality? We analyze over 38 million geolocated tweets from 20 European countries during the first eight months of the Russian invasion of Ukraine. Using retweet community detection and stance annotation across six issues, we identify 'hawkish' and 'doveish' opinion clusters present within almost every country studied. We find that structural polarization is driven not by radicalization, but by the exit of casual users. Crucially, whether opposing sides orient to the same events depends on the issue. On pragmatist issues, both sides react to the same high-profile events, forming an agonistic public sphere. Instead, on interpretive issues, they operate as affective publics and counterpublics constructing divergent meanings. We propose conditional publics to describe formations whose relational structure, sharing or fracturing a referential frame, depends on the epistemic character of the debated issue.

</details>


### [307] [Governance and Regulation of Artificial Intelligence in Developing Countries: A Case Study of Nigeria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06018)
*Uloma Okoro,Tammy Mckenzie,Branislav Radeljic*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究以尼日利亚为案例，探讨法律专业人士对发展中国家人工智能治理的看法。研究采用定性案例研究方法，通过27次半结构化访谈和7人焦点小组访谈收集数据。研究发现，法律从业者对数据隐私风险和缺乏可执行的法律框架表示担忧，对机构能力信心有限，强调需要本地化适应的人工智能治理模式，而非直接照搬外国框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术的快速发展对发展中国家的法律和监管框架提出了挑战。现有研究多聚焦于发达国家的AI治理实践，而对发展中国家特别是非洲国家法律专业人士的视角关注不足。本研究旨在填补这一研究空白，为发展中国家量身定制AI治理策略提供实证依据。

Method: 研究采用定性案例研究设计，以尼日利亚为研究情境。数据收集方法包括：(1)对27名尼日利亚法律从业者进行半结构化访谈；(2)与来自金融、保险和公司法等领域的7名法律从业者进行焦点小组讨论。数据采用主题分析法进行分析，以识别受访者回应中的关键模式。

Result: 研究结果显示：①法律从业者对数据隐私风险表示担忧；②现有法律框架缺乏可执行性；③对机构能力信心有限；④强调需要本地化适应的治理模式，而非直接采用外国框架；⑤对AI潜力持谨慎乐观态度，但强调需有强有力的法律监督和公众问责机制。

Conclusion: 研究为发展中国家AI治理话语体系做出了贡献，强调监管方法应具有情境适应性、包容性，并能够弥合全球伦理原则与本地现实之间的差距。研究结论为政策制定者、监管机构和学者提供了实践指导，特别适用于与尼日利亚具有相似社会经济和法律环境的发展中国家。

Abstract: This study examines the perception of legal professionals on the governance of AI in developing countries, using Nigeria as a case study. The study focused on ethical risks, regulatory gaps, and institutional readiness. The study adopted a qualitative case study design. Data were collected through 27 semi-structured interviews with legal practitioners in Nigeria. A focus group discussion was also held with seven additional legal practitioners across sectors such as finance, insurance, and corporate law. Thematic analysis was employed to identify key patterns in participant responses. Findings showed that there were concerns about data privacy risks and the lack of enforceable legal frameworks. Participants expressed limited confidence in institutional capacity and emphasized the need for locally adapted governance models rather than direct adoption of foreign frameworks. While some expressed optimism about AI's potential, this was conditional on the presence of strong legal oversight and public accountability. The study contributes to the growing discourse on AI governance in developing countries by focusing on the perspectives of legal professionals. It highlights the importance of regulatory approaches that are context-specific, inclusive, and capable of bridging the gap between global ethical principles and local realities. These insights offer practical guidance for policymakers, regulators, and scholars working to shape responsible AI governance in similar environments.

</details>


### [308] [Algorithmic Monoculture and its Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06047)
*Brian Hedden,Manish Raghavan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文（Hedden & Raghavan, MIT）系统性地形式化并检验了对"算法单一化"（algorithmic monoculture，即某一领域全部决策由同一算法做出）的三类批评：系统性排斥、主体性/博弈、信息层面（群体智慧与探索）。结论：最常被引用的排斥类批评不成立；能动性类批评方向不一；只有信息类批评（在单一算法与多样算法同等优劣的前提下）真正支持"算法多元"，而通过"集成"（把多样算法打包成一个算法）可修复这一劣势，因此算法单一化总体上并不像批评者所担忧的那样糟糕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法决策正在取代招聘、信贷、医疗与刑事司法中的"因人而异"的人类判断，承诺减少偏见、提升一致性。但许多学者（哲学、法律与计算机科学界）担心一致性会走向极端：整个领域的决策都由同一算法完成，即"算法单一化"，并由此担忧"算法黑名单"式的系统性排斥（Creel & Hellman 2022；O'Neil 2016；Bommasani 等 2022；Toups 等 2023；Jain 等 2024b；Peng & Garg 2024a），甚至提出应禁止单一公司或其算法主导整个招聘或信贷行业。农业与网络安全的类比强化了这一直觉，但作者认为不能靠类比或直觉下定论，需要把批评形式化并严格评估：算法单一化究竟在何种条件下、相对算法多元（polyculture）真正更差？

Method: 以招聘为主案例（兼及信贷、生成式AI、药物发现），采用"极限情形"设定：整个流程由算法排序、既定数量的岗位、候选人至多接受一份工作（外部性约束）。作者对三类批评逐一形式化：(1) 系统性排斥——在序贯/同时招聘模型中计算被排除人数与群体（种族、性别等）被系统排除的概率，比较"无知之幕"（随机排序）下的组合计数与不同信息状态下的模型，并讨论"机会/概率"与跨期不平等（方差、年度变动）版本；(2) 能动性与博弈——用Kleinberg & Raghavan (2020) 的多维投资模型分析Goodhart式"刷分"，并用SAT/ACT 大学录取示例说明多态竞争池改变了努力的回报结构；(3) 信息层面——引用Kleinberg & Raghavan (2021)（公共 vs 私有算法、均衡与Braess式悖论）、Peng & Garg (2024a,b)（稳定匹配、轻尾噪声下群体智慧成立、重尾下失效）、Kleinberg et al. (2025)（无政府状态的price of anarchy ≤ 2），并做数值模拟（1000名候选人，顺序招聘与同时招聘 + 延迟接受匹配，噪声为高斯分布；另设ensemble monoculture取均值聚合）；探索部分使用多臂老虎机（bandit）模型，形式化为"lock-in time / wrong lock-in 事件"，证明Theorem 1（当T足够大时，单一化比多元更可能无法识别最优臂），附录给出完整证明（13个引理，基于大数律、迭代对数律、Blackwell界、支配/反向Fatou收敛定理）。

Result: (1) 排斥批评不成立：岗位数固定时，单一化与多元下失业人数相同（Peng & Garg 2024a的结论），"被一家拒绝即被全部拒绝"的直觉错误（各公司候选池不同，排序式招聘下runner-up仍可能被录用）；社会显著群体被系统排除的概率依赖于信息状态——存在单一化风险更高、多元更高、以及两者相同的模型，没有哪个明显更现实；"事前机会"版本可由Broome式公平理论回击，且概率分布的极端形态未必比中间形态离理想更远；跨期不平等版本也不成立，因为算法排名本身会随时间变化，多元仅多一个招聘顺序的噪声源，且多元的信息聚合反而降低方差；(2) 能动性批评不具决定性：单一化若仅依据不可变特征会削弱努力回报，但这类"漫画式"单一化不应作为靶子；而多元带来的"多投申请"优势往往偏向更有资源者；(3) 信息批评最有分量：群体智慧与探索机制确实支持多元——Kleinberg & Raghavan (2021) 表明即便公共算法更准，均衡中两家都采用公共算法的社会结果反而更差（类囚徒困境/Braess悖论），Peng & Garg (2024a) 表明轻尾噪声下公司数增大时多元几乎必然选出最优候选人（重尾则相反），模拟显示多元在顺序与同时招聘中都优于普通单一化，Theorem 1 证明在时间跨度足够长时多元识别最优臂的概率高于单一化，且在含外部性的招聘模型中多元在Total Bayesian Regret与"中立观察者误分类数"上仍占优；但三项反驳削弱该批评：人类与算法误差实际高度相关（共享数据与偏差），使独立性假设不成立；市场竞争下选择共享算法时均衡的price of anarchy至多为2；并且把多元算法"集成"（ensemble，如取各算法分数的均值或最大值）成单一算法后，其表现可与多元持平甚至更好（模拟中ensemble单调性优于两种多元）。集成在实践中可能因数据清洗、特征异质、需征求各招聘经理意见等而不可行。此外单一化的一个好处是更容易发现与消除歧视。

Conclusion: 算法单一化并不像批评者所担心的那样必然是灾难：常见的排斥类批评失败，能动性类批评双向抵消，唯一有实质力量的信息类批评只在"单一算法与多元各算法同等水平"这一条件下成立，而该劣势可通过集成式单一化加以修复（代价是实践可行性存疑，且多元所依赖的算法独立性在现实中难以实现）。关键结构性洞察在于招聘、信贷、大学录取等领域存在外部性（岗位固定、机会互斥），这既防止单一化比多元排斥更多人，也迫使单一化进行相当程度的探索；缺乏此类外部性的领域（如各类奖项评选）单一化的排斥效应才可能更严重，但仍受竞争压力与博弈（herding、差异化使用共享信号）约束。作者呼吁进一步研究医学、刑事司法等结构不同的领域，纳入职位发布不同步、申请不全、市场摩擦与拥堵等真实复杂性，并开展涉及"自然实验"的实证研究；结论强调单一化是一种可通过有意设计（随机化、个性化、集成）加以改进的结构，而非必然的坏结果，实现其潜力需要持续的设计投入与后果研究。

Abstract: Algorithmic decision-making is replacing idiosyncratic human judgment in domains such as hiring, lending, and criminal justice. This shift promises increased consistency, but many scholars worry that it can go too far. They warn of the dangers of algorithmic monoculture, in which all decisions across a domain are made using a single algorithm. We systematically evaluate a range of objections to monoculture, formalizing and rigorously assessing familiar critiques alongside novel ones. These objections concern systematic exclusion, agency and gaming, and information aggregation and exploration. We conclude that monoculture is less problematic than its critics have supposed: commonly cited objections fail, and while other objections have some force, they are not decisive against monoculture in general.

</details>
